CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.ceilnum (
    p_num                          IN    NUMBER,
    p_scale                        IN    NUMBER DEFAULT 0
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 소수 이하에서 절상하는 function
--
    p_num3                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    p_num3  := trunc(NVL(p_num,0) / (10 ** (p_scale * -1)) + .9) * (10 ** (p_scale * -1));
    RETURN  p_num3;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_051_NM (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- Find 종목약어명
--
    w_jj_nm                        VARCHAR (200);
BEGIN
    w_jj_nm := F_JM_NM (p_corp_gr, p_jm_cd);
    if  w_jj_nm IS NULL OR w_jj_nm = p_jm_cd    then    w_jj_nm := F_GWAMOK_NM (p_corp_gr, p_jm_cd);
    end if;
    if  w_jj_nm IS NULL OR w_jj_nm = p_jm_cd    then    w_jj_nm := F_TR_CO_NM (p_corp_gr, p_jm_cd);
    end if;

    RETURN  W_JJ_NM;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_3BF_YMD (
    i_date                         IN    DATE
 )  RETURN DATE IS
-- 전전영업일
--
    w_date                         DATE;
    w_ii                           NUMBER   default 0;
BEGIN
    w_date := i_date;
    LOOP
        w_date := w_date - 1;
        BEGIN
            SELECT holi_ymd
              INTO w_date
              FROM SZX0HL t1  /* 공휴일 */
             WHERE t1.nation_cd = 'KR'
               AND t1.holi_ymd  = w_date;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            if  w_ii = 1    then    EXIT;
            end if;
            w_ii := w_ii + 1;
        END;
    end loop;
    RETURN  w_date;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_49_1 (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_tr_ymd                       IN    DATE,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 채권 발행기관 신용등급
--
    w_grade                        VARCHAR (10);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'KIS,' || kis_pg_nm
          INTO w_grade
          FROM SCM1SM t1  /* 채권 시가평가 자료 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_tr_ymd
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_grade := ',';
    END;
    RETURN  w_grade;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_49_2A (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,

 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 발행기관 신용등급

    w_grade                        VARCHAR (10);
BEGIN

          INTO w_grade

         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_tr_ymd
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_grade := ',';
    END;
    RETURN  w_grade;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_49_2B (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_tr_ymd                       IN    DATE,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 보증기관 신용등급
--
    w_grade                        VARCHAR (10);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'KIS,' || kis_bojng_pg_nm
          INTO w_grade
          FROM SHM1SM t1  /* 현금 시가평가 자료 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_tr_ymd
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;

        if  w_grade = 'KIS,'    then    w_grade := ',';
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_grade := ',';
    END;

    RETURN  w_grade;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_5BF_YMD (
    i_date                         IN    DATE,
    i_ilsu                         IN    NUMBER
 )  RETURN DATE IS
-- 입력일기준 5영업일 산출
-- 2009.12.14부터 5, 20영업일로 변경
--
    w_date                         DATE;
    w_ii                           NUMBER   default 0;
    w_ilsu                         NUMBER   default 0;
BEGIN
    w_date := i_date;
    w_ilsu := i_ilsu - 2;
    LOOP
        w_date := w_date - 1;
        BEGIN
            SELECT holi_ymd
              INTO w_date
              FROM SZX0HL t1  /* 공휴일 */
             WHERE t1.nation_cd = 'KR'
               AND t1.holi_ymd  = w_date;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            if  w_ii = w_ilsu   then    EXIT;
            end if;
            w_ii := w_ii + 1;
        END;
    end loop;
    RETURN  w_date;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_ACCT2FUND (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_mg_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_enc_acct_no                  IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 계좌번호를 펀드코드로
--
    w_open_ymd                     DATE;
    w_fund_cd                      szm0ia.fund_cd%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT fund_cd
          INTO w_fund_cd
          FROM SZM0IA t1  /* 자문 계좌정보 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr               = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.mg_cd                 = p_mg_cd
           AND t1.enc_acct_no           = p_enc_acct_no
           AND NVL(t1.haeji_ymd, p_ymd) >= p_ymd
         ORDER BY fst_seolj_ymd DESC;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_fund_cd := '';
    END;
    RETURN  w_fund_cd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_ACC_NM (
    p_gwamok                       IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 계정과목명
    w_acc_nm                       SZX0GM.GWAMOK_NM%type;
BEGIN

    BEGIN
        SELECT GWAMOK_NM
          INTO w_acc_nm
          FROM SZX0GM t1  /* 계정과목 */
         WHERE t1.GWAMOK = p_gwamok;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_acc_nm := '';
    END;

    RETURN  w_acc_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_AMT_HAN (
    p_amt                          IN    NUMBER
 )  RETURN VARCHAR2 DETERMINISTIC IS
    v_digits                       CONSTANT VARCHAR2(9 CHAR)  := '일이삼사오육칠팔구';
    v_units                        CONSTANT VARCHAR2(3 CHAR)  := '십백천';
    v_big_units                    CONSTANT VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) := '만억조경해자양구간정';

    v_amt_str                      VARCHAR2(40);
    v_len                          NUMBER;
    v_digit                        NUMBER;
    v_result                       VARCHAR2(200 CHAR) := '';
    v_unit_idx                     NUMBER;
    v_chunk_val                    NUMBER;
    v_is_negative                  BOOLEAN := FALSE;
BEGIN
    if  p_amt IS NULL then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    if TRUNC (p_amt) = 0 then
        RETURN '영';
    end if;

    -- 음수 체크 및 절대값 변환
    if  p_amt < 0 then
        v_is_negative := TRUE;
    end if;

    v_amt_str := TO_CHAR (ABS(TRUNC(p_amt)));
    v_len     := LENGTH (v_amt_str);

    -- 금액 변환 루프 (작성하신 훌륭한 로직 유지)
    for i IN 1 .. v_len loop
        v_digit    := TO_NUMBER (SUBSTR(v_amt_str, v_len - i + 1, 1));
        v_unit_idx := MOD (i - 1, 4);

        if  v_digit > 0 then
            -- 큰 단위 처리 (만, 억, 조 등)
            if  v_unit_idx = 0 AND i > 1 then
                v_result := SUBSTR (v_big_units, (i - 1) / 4, 1) || v_result;
            end if;

            -- 작은 단위 처리 (십, 백, 천)
            if  v_unit_idx > 0 then
                v_result := SUBSTR (v_units, v_unit_idx, 1) || v_result;
            end if;

            -- 숫자 처리 ('일' ~ '구')
            if  v_digit > 1 OR v_unit_idx = 0 OR i = v_len then
                v_result := SUBSTR (v_digits, v_digit, 1) || v_result;
            end if;
        else
            -- 0 처리 (해당 4자리 구간에 유효 숫자가 있을 때만 큰 단위 유지)
            if  v_unit_idx = 0 AND i > 1 then
                v_chunk_val := TO_NUMBER (SUBSTR(v_amt_str, GREATEST(v_len - i - 2, 1), LEAST(v_len - i + 1, 4)));
                if  v_chunk_val > 0 then
                    v_result := SUBSTR (v_big_units, (i - 1) / 4, 1) || v_result;
                end if;
            end if;
        end if;
    end loop;

    -- 음수일 경우 부호 추가
    if  v_is_negative then
        v_result := '마이너스 ' || v_result;
    end if;

    RETURN v_result;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
    RETURN NULL;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_API_KEY (
    p_table                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_subtable                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_api_key                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR AS
--
-- API_KEY 자료 점검
--
    w_return                       char(1) := 'N';
BEGIN
    if  p_table = 'API_OPT_PRICE'   then
        BEGIN
            SELECT 'Y'
              INTO w_return
              FROM API_OPT_PRICE t1  /* 해외선물옵션 기본시세 - 해외옵션종목현재가 */
             WHERE t1.API_KEY   = p_api_key
               AND t1.PROC_DATE IS NOT NULL;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            w_return := 'N';
        END;
        RETURN w_return;

    elsif p_subtable = 'API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB' then
        BEGIN
            SELECT 'Y'
              INTO w_return
              FROM API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB t1  /* 해외옵션 상품기본정보 */
             WHERE t1.API_KEY = p_api_key
               AND t1.EXCH_CD IS NOT NULL;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            w_return := 'N';
        END;
        RETURN w_return;

    elsif p_table = 'API_STOCK_INFO' then
        BEGIN
            SELECT 'Y'
              INTO w_return
              FROM API_STOCK_INFO t1  /* 국내주식기본정보 */
             WHERE t1.API_KEY = p_api_key
               AND t1.PDNO IS NOT NULL;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            w_return := 'N';
        END;
        RETURN w_return;
    else
        RETURN 'Y';
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BACKGROUND_COLOR (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN NUMBER DETERMINISTIC IS
-- 펀드 당기구간 배경색상
--
    w_color                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE WHEN p_ymd BETWEEN t1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1 AND t1.af_gyul_ymd
                    THEN 16443086
                    ELSE 536870912 END
          INTO w_color
          FROM SZM0GI t1
         WHERE 1 = 1
           AND t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND p_ymd      BETWEEN (t1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1) AND t1.af_gyul_ymd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_color := 536870912;
    END;

    RETURN  w_color;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BALH_NM (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_balh_co                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 발행처명
--
    w_balh_nm                      sjx0jb.balh_nm%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT balh_nm
          INTO w_balh_nm
          FROM SJX0JB t1  /* 발행기관 */
         WHERE balh_co = p_balh_co;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        BEGIN
            SELECT tr_co_nm
              INTO w_balh_nm
              FROM SZX2MM t1  /* 매매처 */
             WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.tr_co_cd = p_balh_co;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            w_balh_nm := NULL;
        END;
    END;
    RETURN  w_balh_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BALH_YMD (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN DATE IS
-- 발행일자
--
    w_balh_ymd                     DATE;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT balh_ymd
          INTO w_balh_ymd
          FROM SCM0CJ t1  /* 채권종목 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        BEGIN
            SELECT balh_ymd
              INTO w_balh_ymd
              FROM SHM0HJ t1  /* 현금 종목정보 */
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            w_balh_ymd := NULL;
        END;
    END;

    RETURN  w_balh_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BANK_NO (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_sell_co                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_sutak_cd                     IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 입금 계좌번호 Return   ...
--
    w_bank_no                      szx1pa.enc_bank_no%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT TO_DECRYPTS(enc_bank_no)
          INTO w_bank_no
          FROM SZX1PA t1  /* 판매사/수탁사별 입금계좌번호 관리 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.sell_co  = p_sell_co
           AND t1.sutak_cd = p_sutak_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_bank_no := p_sell_co || ' ' || p_sutak_cd || ' - 계좌번호가 없습니다';
    END;
    RETURN  w_bank_no;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BETA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jisu_jm                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
 )  RETURN BETA_TAB AS
--
-- 지수대비 베타
--
    w_tab                          BETA_TAB;

    w_days                         NUMBER := 0;
    d20                            DATE;
    d60                            DATE;
    d120                           DATE;

    w_beta                         NUMBER;
    w_beta20                       NUMBER;
    w_beta60                       NUMBER;
    w_beta120                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab := BETA_TAB( );

    -- 구간 시작일
    for xx IN (
        SELECT t1.YMD
             , NVL(t1.JONGA,0) / LAG(NVL(t1.JONGA,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.JM_CD
                                       ORDER BY t1.JM_CD, t1.YMD) - 1  AS rt
          FROM SYT0LP t1  /* 해외수신 종목 시세 */
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr                /* 운용(자문)사 */
           AND t1.YMD     BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd  /* 적용기준일자 */
           AND t1.YMD     NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                    FROM SZX0HL ta  /* 공휴일 */
                                   WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'US'
                                     AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd  /* 공휴일일자 */
                                  )                 /* 적용기준일자 */
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd                  /* 종목코드 */
         ORDER BY t1.YMD DESC
    ) loop
        if  xx.rt IS NULL   then    CONTINUE;
        end if;

        w_days := w_days + 1;
        if  w_days <= 20    then    d20 := xx.ymd;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 60    then    d60 := xx.ymd;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 120   then    d120 := xx.ymd;
        end if;
    end loop;

    WITH DAEBI_RT AS (
         SELECT t1.JM_CD
              , t1.YMD
              , NVL(t1.JONGA,0) / LAG(NVL(t1.JONGA,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.JM_CD
                                        ORDER BY t1.JM_CD, t1.YMD) - 1  AS rt
           FROM SYT0LP t1  /* 해외수신 종목 시세 */
          WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr                /* 운용(자문)사 */
            AND t1.YMD     BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd  /* 적용기준일자 */
            AND t1.YMD     NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                     FROM SZX0HL ta  /* 공휴일 */
                                    WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'US'
                                      AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd)
            AND t1.JM_CD   IN (p_jisu_jm, p_jm_cd)    /* 종목코드 */
         ) , 
     RT AS (
         SELECT t1.ymd
              , MAX(CASE WHEN t1.jm_cd=p_jisu_jm THEN t1.rt END)  AS jisu_rt
              , MAX(CASE WHEN t1.jm_cd=p_jm_cd   THEN t1.rt END)  AS jm_rt
           FROM DAEBI_RT t1
          GROUP BY t1.ymd
          ORDER BY t1.ymd DESC
         ) , 
     AVG_VALUES AS (
         SELECT AVG(jisu_rt)                                                    AS jisu_avg    /* _1- */
              , AVG(jm_rt)                                                      AS jm_avg      /* _2- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d20 AND p_tymd  THEN jisu_rt END)  AS jisu_avg20  /* _3- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d20 AND p_tymd  THEN jm_rt END)    AS jm_avg20    /* _4- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d60 AND p_tymd  THEN jisu_rt END)  AS jisu_avg60  /* _5- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d60 AND p_tymd  THEN jm_rt END)    AS jm_avg60    /* _6- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d120 AND p_tymd THEN jisu_rt END)  AS jisu_avg120 /* _7- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d120 AND p_tymd THEN jm_rt END)    AS jm_avg120   /* _8- */
           FROM RT t1
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN p_fymd + 1 AND p_tymd
         ) , 
     COV_VAR AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                          AS VAR
           FROM RT t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
         ) , 
     COV_VAR20 AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg20) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg20)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                              AS VAR
           FROM RT         t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN d20 AND p_tymd
         ) , 
     COV_VAR60 AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg60) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg60)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                              AS VAR
           FROM RT         t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN d60 AND p_tymd
         ) , 
     COV_VAR120 AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg120) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg120)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                                AS VAR
           FROM RT         t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN d120 AND p_tymd
         )
    -- Main SQL --
    --------------
    SELECT MAX(t1.COV / t1.VAR)
         , MAX(t20.COV / t20.VAR)
         , MAX(t60.COV / t60.VAR)
         , MAX(t120.COV / t120.VAR)
      INTO w_beta
         , w_beta20
         , w_beta60
         , w_beta120
      FROM COV_VAR t1
         , COV_VAR20 t20
         , COV_VAR60 t60
         , COV_VAR120 t120;

    w_tab.extend;
    w_tab (1) := BETA_REC (ROUND(w_beta, 4), ROUND(w_beta20, 4), ROUND(w_beta60, 4), ROUND(w_beta120, 4));

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BETA_K (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jisu_jm                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
 )  RETURN BETA_TAB AS
--
-- 지수대비 베타
--
    w_tab                          BETA_TAB;

    w_days                         NUMBER := 0;
    d20                            DATE;
    d60                            DATE;
    d120                           DATE;

    w_beta                         NUMBER;
    w_beta20                       NUMBER;
    w_beta60                       NUMBER;
    w_beta120                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab := BETA_TAB( );

    -- 구간 시작일
    for xx IN (
        SELECT t1.YMD
             , NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0) / LAG(NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.KOSCOM_CD
                                        ORDER BY t1.KOSCOM_CD, t1.YMD) - 1  AS rt
          FROM SJT1TG t1  /* 주식/선물/Option 종목별 종가(영업일만 존재) */
         WHERE t1.YMD       BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.YMD       NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                      FROM SZX0HL ta  /* 공휴일 */
                                     WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'KR'
                                       AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd)
           AND t1.KOSCOM_CD = p_jm_cd
         ORDER BY t1.YMD DESC
    ) loop
        if  xx.rt IS NULL   then    CONTINUE;
        end if;

        w_days := w_days + 1;
        if  w_days <= 20    then    d20 := xx.ymd;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 60    then    d60 := xx.ymd;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 120   then    d120 := xx.ymd;
        end if;
    end loop;

    WITH DAEBI_RT AS (
         SELECT t1.KOSCOM_CD
              , t1.YMD
              , NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0) / LAG(NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.KOSCOM_CD
                                         ORDER BY t1.KOSCOM_CD, t1.YMD) - 1  AS rt
           FROM SJT1TG t1  /* 주식/선물/Option 종목별 종가(영업일만 존재) */
          WHERE t1.YMD       BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd
            AND t1.YMD       NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                       FROM SZX0HL ta  /* 공휴일 */
                                      WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'KR'
                                        AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd)
            AND t1.KOSCOM_CD IN (p_jisu_jm, p_jm_cd)
         UNION ALL
         SELECT T2.COL_ID
              , T2.YMD
              , NVL(t2.COL_VAL, 0) / LAG(NVL(t2.COL_VAL, 0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t2.COL_ID
                                           ORDER BY t2.COL_ID, t2.YMD) - 1  AS rt
           FROM SKT0BM t2  /* 벤치마크수익률/성과보수 산출기준 각종비율 */
          WHERE t2.YMD BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd
            AND t2.YMD NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                 FROM SZX0HL ta  /* 공휴일 */
                                WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'KR'
                                  AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd)
         ) , 
     RT AS (
         SELECT t1.ymd
              , MAX(CASE WHEN t1.KOSCOM_CD=p_jisu_jm THEN t1.rt END)  AS jisu_rt
              , MAX(CASE WHEN t1.KOSCOM_CD=p_jm_cd   THEN t1.rt END)  AS jm_rt
           FROM DAEBI_RT t1
          GROUP BY t1.ymd
          ORDER BY t1.ymd DESC
         ) , 
     AVG_VALUES AS (
         SELECT AVG(jisu_rt)                                                    AS jisu_avg    /* _1- */
              , AVG(jm_rt)                                                      AS jm_avg      /* _2- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d20 AND p_tymd  THEN jisu_rt END)  AS jisu_avg20  /* _3- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d20 AND p_tymd  THEN jm_rt END)    AS jm_avg20    /* _4- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d60 AND p_tymd  THEN jisu_rt END)  AS jisu_avg60  /* _5- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d60 AND p_tymd  THEN jm_rt END)    AS jm_avg60    /* _6- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d120 AND p_tymd THEN jisu_rt END)  AS jisu_avg120 /* _7- */
              , AVG(CASE WHEN t1.ymd BETWEEN d120 AND p_tymd THEN jm_rt END)    AS jm_avg120   /* _8- */
           FROM RT t1
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN p_fymd + 1 AND p_tymd
         ) , 
     COV_VAR AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                          AS VAR
           FROM RT t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
         ) , 
     COV_VAR20 AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg20) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg20)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                              AS VAR
           FROM RT         t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN d20 AND p_tymd
         ) , 
     COV_VAR60 AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg60) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg60)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                              AS VAR
           FROM RT         t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN d60 AND p_tymd
         ) , 
     COV_VAR120 AS (
         SELECT SUM((t1.jm_rt - t2.jm_avg120) * (t1.jisu_rt - t2.jisu_avg120)) / COUNT(t1.jisu_rt)  AS COV
              , VARIANCE(t1.jisu_rt)                                                                AS VAR
           FROM RT         t1
              , AVG_VALUES t2
          WHERE t1.ymd BETWEEN d120 AND p_tymd
         )
    -- Main SQL --
    --------------

    --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 내부 함수(테이블) 이용한 쿼리 실행
    --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    SELECT MAX(t1.COV / t1.VAR)
         , MAX(t20.COV / t20.VAR)
         , MAX(t60.COV / t60.VAR)
         , MAX(t120.COV / t120.VAR)
      INTO w_beta
         , w_beta20
         , w_beta60
         , w_beta120
      FROM COV_VAR t1
         , COV_VAR20 t20
         , COV_VAR60 t60
         , COV_VAR120 t120;

    w_tab.extend;
    w_tab (1) := BETA_REC (ROUND(w_beta,4), ROUND(w_beta20,4), ROUND(w_beta60,4), ROUND(w_beta120,4));

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BF_GYUL_GIJUN_GA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    i_bf_gyul_ymd                  IN    DATE,
    i_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 결산 후 기준가
--
    w_gijun_ga                     NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT t1.gijun_ga
          INTO w_gijun_ga
          FROM SKT0GI t1  /* 펀드별 기준가 */
             , SZM0IA ia  /* 자문 계좌정보 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr       = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd       = i_fund_cd
           AND t1.ymd           = i_bf_gyul_ymd
           AND t1.work_gb       = 'B'
           AND ia.corp_gr       = t1.corp_gr
           AND ia.fund_cd       = t1.fund_cd
           AND ia.fund_currency = t1.fund_currency;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_gijun_ga := 1000;
    END;
    RETURN  w_gijun_ga;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BLOB2STR (
    p_blob                         IN    blob
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
    w_str                          VARCHAR (32000);

    w_amount                       NUMBER := 32000;
    w_pos                          NUMBER := 1;
    w_blob_len                     INTEGER;
BEGIN
    w_blob_len := DBMS_LOB.getlength (p_blob);

    -- Read chunks of the BLOB and write them to the file until complete.
    while w_pos <= w_blob_len loop
        DBMS_LOB.read ( p_blob, w_amount, w_pos, w_str );
        w_pos := w_pos + w_amount;
    end loop;

    RETURN  w_str;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BM (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,  /* 펀드코드 */
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE      /* 기준일 */
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 펀드 BM 값
--

    w_bm                           NUMBER;

    w_col_id                       VARCHAR (50);
    w_col_per                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT col_id
             , col_per
          INTO w_col_id
             , w_col_per
          FROM SKT1BM t1  /* 펀드별 벤치마크용 편입비율 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr        = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd        = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.ymd            <= p_ymd
           AND NVL(t1.col_per,0) > 0;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        RETURN  NULL;
    END;

    BEGIN
        SELECT col_val * w_col_per / 100  /* 기준일 BM 지수 */
          INTO w_bm
          FROM SKT0BM t1  /* 벤치마크수익률/성과보수 산출기준 각종비율 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
           AND t1.col_id  = w_col_id;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_bm := 0;
    END;

    RETURN  w_bm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BMSUIK_RT (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_col_id                       IN    VARCHAR,  /* 지수 */
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,     /* 기준일 */
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE      /* 비교일 */
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 지수 수익율
-- kospi, kosdaq 지수등
--
    w_gijun_ga                     NUMBER;
    t_gijun_ga                     NUMBER;
    w_suik_per                     NUMBER;

BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT MAX(col_val)  /* 기준일 BM 지수 */
          INTO w_gijun_ga
          FROM SKT0BM t1  /* 벤치마크수익률/성과보수 산출기준 각종비율 */
         WHERE t1.corp_Gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_fymd
           AND t1.col_id  = p_col_id;
    END;

    BEGIN
        SELECT MAX(col_val)  /* 비교일 BM 지수 */
          INTO t_gijun_ga
          FROM SKT0BM t1  /* 벤치마크수익률/성과보수 산출기준 각종비율 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_tymd
           AND t1.col_id  = p_col_id;
    END;

    BEGIN
        w_suik_per := ROUND  ((t_gijun_ga / w_gijun_ga - 1) * 100,2);
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_suik_per := NULL;
    END;

    RETURN  w_suik_per;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BM_BIGO (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 벤치마크 기타사항
--
    w_bigo                         skt1bm.bigo%type;
BEGIN
    for xx IN (
        SELECT bigo
          FROM SKT1BM t1  /* 펀드별 벤치마크용 편입비율 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.ymd     = (SELECT MAX(ymd)
                               FROM SKT1BM ta  /* 펀드별 벤치마크용 편입비율 */
                              WHERE ta.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                AND ta.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                                AND ta.ymd     <= p_ymd)
           AND t1.col_id  = 'sayu:bigo'
    ) loop
        w_bigo := xx.bigo;
        EXIT;
    end loop;
    RETURN  w_bigo;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BOND_DANGA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,  /* 종목코드 */
    p_trns_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 평가일자 */
    p_mk_suik_rt                   IN    NUMBER,   /* 수익율 */
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'B' -- C:세전(이표채 권리락후), B:세전, A:세후, D:경과세금
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 채권 시장수익율에 의한 단가계산
--
    r_danga                        NUMBER := -1;  /* 단가 */

    w_sunme_gb                     VARCHAR (1); /* 선매출 계산여부 */

    w_mangi_amt                    NUMBER := 0;  /* 만기금액 */
    w_mang_ija                     NUMBER := 0;
BEGIN
    if  p_mk_suik_rt = 0    then    RETURN 0;
    end if;

    for xx IN (
        SELECT balh_ymd                        /* _1- */
             , sanghw_ymd                      /* _2- */
             , medo_ymd                        /* _3- */
             , bunhal_gb                       /* _4- */
             , sunhu_gb                        /* _5- */
             , ija_jigub_gb                    /* _6- */
             , 12 / yy_ija_hoisu  AS ija_mons  /* _7- */
             , yy_ija_hoisu                    /* _8- */
             , pyom_iyul                       /* _9- */
             , tot_ija_hoicha                  /* _10- */
             , sunme_ija_ymd                   /* _11- */
             , balh_co                         /* _12- */
             , bond_attr                       /* _13- */
             , bond_cd                         /* _14- */
             , halin_iyul                      /* _15- */
             , halj_iyul                       /* _16- */
             , bf_ija_ymd                      /* _17- */
             , af_ija_ymd                      /* _18- */
             , mangi_iyul                      /* _19- */
          FROM SCM0CJ t1  /* 채권종목 */
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = F_LINK_CORP_GR(p_corp_gr)
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
    ) loop
        BEGIN
            SELECT bf_ija_ymd
                 , af_ija_ymd
              INTO xx.bf_ija_ymd
                 , xx.af_ija_ymd
              FROM SCJ0IG t1  /* 채권 종목별 이자구간 */
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = F_LINK_CORP_GR(p_corp_gr)
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
               AND t1.ija_gb  = 'B'
               AND p_trns_ymd BETWEEN t1.bf_ija_ymd AND (t1.af_ija_ymd - 1);
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            NULL;
            -- 종목정보 이자일 사용
        END;

        w_sunme_gb := 'N';
        if  p_trns_ymd <= NVL (xx.sunme_ija_ymd, p_trns_ymd - 1)     then   w_sunme_gb := 'Y';
        end if;

        if  xx.bunhal_gb = '1'  then    -- 분할채권
            r_danga := F_PF_PRT (p_corp_gr, '1', 10000, p_mk_suik_rt, xx.pyom_iyul, xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, xx.medo_ymd,
                                 xx.ija_mons, w_sunme_gb, p_trns_ymd, p_jm_cd);

        elsif INSTR ('456DEF', xx.ija_jigub_gb) > 0          then   -- 복리채
            -- 복리채 선매출 이자는 할인채 형식으로 발행하므로 선매출 'N' 처리 20210901 yjs
            w_mangi_amt := F_FV_ETC (10000, xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, xx.medo_ymd, xx.ija_jigub_gb, xx.ija_mons, xx.sunhu_gb, xx.pyom_iyul, xx.tot_ija_hoicha, 'N');
            r_danga := F_PV_ETC (p_gb, w_mangi_amt, p_mk_suik_rt, xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, p_trns_ymd,
                                 xx.ija_mons, xx.tot_ija_hoicha, xx.ija_jigub_gb, xx.pyom_iyul,
                                 xx.medo_ymd, xx.sunhu_gb, 'N');
            -- dbms_output.put_line (' mangi : ' || w_mangi_amt || ' danga:' || r_danga);

        elsif INSTR ('78G', xx.ija_jigub_gb) > 0             then   -- 이표채
            if  xx.bond_attr = '6' AND xx.bond_cd = '5' then    -- CB인경우 만기보장금액 계산
                if NVL (xx.mangi_iyul,0) != 0   then
                        w_mang_ija := ROUND  (POWER(1 + ((xx.mangi_iyul - xx.pyom_iyul) / xx.yy_ija_hoisu), xx.tot_ija_hoicha) - 1,6) * 10000;
                else    w_mang_ija := 0;
                end if;
                    r_danga := F_PV_CPN (p_gb, 10000, p_mk_suik_rt, xx.pyom_iyul, xx.tot_ija_hoicha,
                                         xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, xx.medo_ymd, xx.bf_ija_ymd, xx.af_ija_ymd,
                                         xx.sunhu_gb, xx.ija_mons, w_sunme_gb, p_trns_ymd, xx.halin_iyul, w_mang_ija);
            else
                r_danga := F_PV_CPN (p_gb, 10000, p_mk_suik_rt, xx.pyom_iyul, xx.tot_ija_hoicha,
                                     xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, xx.medo_ymd, xx.bf_ija_ymd, xx.af_ija_ymd,
                                     xx.sunhu_gb, xx.ija_mons, w_sunme_gb, p_trns_ymd, xx.halin_iyul, 0);
            end if;

        elsif INSTR ('0123AFBC', xx.ija_jigub_gb) > 0            then   -- 통화채/할인채
            r_danga := F_PV_ETC (p_gb, 10000, p_mk_suik_rt, xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, p_trns_ymd,
                                 xx.ija_mons, xx.tot_ija_hoicha, xx.ija_jigub_gb, xx.pyom_iyul,
                                 xx.medo_ymd, xx.sunhu_gb, w_sunme_gb);
        elsif xx.ija_jigub_gb = '9'     then    -- 단리채
            w_mangi_amt := F_FV_ETC (10000, xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd, xx.medo_ymd, '1', xx.ija_mons, xx.sunhu_gb, xx.pyom_iyul, xx.tot_ija_hoicha, w_sunme_gb);
            r_danga := F_PV_ETC (p_gb, w_mangi_amt, p_mk_suik_rt, xx.balh_ymd, xx.sanghw_ymd,
                                 p_trns_ymd, xx.ija_mons, xx.tot_ija_hoicha, xx.ija_jigub_gb,
                                 xx.pyom_iyul, xx.medo_ymd, xx.sunhu_gb, w_sunme_gb);
        end if;
    end loop;

    RETURN  r_danga;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BOND_NM (
    p_bond_attr                    IN    VARCHAR,
    p_bond_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 채권 분류명
--
BEGIN
    FOR xx IN  (
        SELECT bond_fnm
          FROM SCX0CB t1
         WHERE bond_attr = p_bond_attr
           AND bond_cd   = p_bond_cd
    ) loop
        RETURN  xx.bond_fnm;
    END LOOP;
    RETURN  p_bond_attr || ',' || p_bond_cd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BOND_TYPE (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 채권종목 유형명
--
    r_nm                         VARCHAR (10);
BEGIN
    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT t1.bond_attr
             , t1.bond_cd
             , t1.mojib_gb
             , t1.cj_fnm
             , NVL(SUBSTR(t2.jy_suik_cd,1,1),' ')  AS bond_gubun
          FROM SCM0CJ t1
             , SCX0CB t2
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.jm_cd     = p_jm_cd
           AND t2.bond_attr = t1.bond_attr
           AND t2.bond_cd   = t1.bond_cd
    ) loop
        if  xx.bond_attr = '1'                  then    RETURN  '국채';
        elsif xx.bond_attr = '2'                then    RETURN  '지방채';
        elsif xx.bond_attr = '3'                then    -- 특수채
            if  xx.bond_gubun = '4'             then    RETURN  '통안채';
            elsif xx.bond_gubun IN ('5','6')    then    RETURN  '금융채';
            else                                        RETURN  '특수채';
            end if;
        else
            if NVL (xx.mojib_gb,'1') = '2'  then    r_nm    := '사모-';
            else                                    r_nm    := '공모-';
            end if;

            if  xx.bond_cd IN ('1','A','B','C','D')     then    RETURN  r_nm || '보증채';
            elsif xx.bond_cd IN ('4','J','K','L','M')   then    RETURN  r_nm || '무보증채';
            elsif xx.bond_cd IN ('5','N','P')           then    RETURN  r_nm || '전환사채';
            elsif xx.bond_cd IN ('7','S','T')           then    RETURN  r_nm || '교환사채';
            elsif xx.bond_cd IN ('6','Q','R')           then    RETURN  r_nm || '신주인수권부사채';
            elsif xx.bond_cd IN ('8','V')               then    RETURN  r_nm || '옵션부사채';
            else                                                RETURN  r_nm || '회사채';
            end if;
        end if;
    END LOOP;
    RETURN  p_jm_cd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BUNGI (
    p_gyul_ymd                     IN    DATE,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 펀드 분기결산일 점검
--
    w_gyul_ymd                   DATE;
BEGIN
    w_gyul_ymd  := TO_DATE(TO_CHAR(p_fymd,'yyyy') || TO_CHAR(p_gyul_ymd,'mmdd'),'yyyymmdd');

    if  w_gyul_ymd between p_fymd AND p_tymd                    then    RETURN  w_gyul_ymd;
    elsif ADD_MONTHS (w_gyul_ymd, 3) between p_fymd AND p_tymd  then    RETURN  ADD_MONTHS(w_gyul_ymd,3);
    elsif ADD_MONTHS (w_gyul_ymd, 6) between p_fymd AND p_tymd  then    RETURN  ADD_MONTHS(w_gyul_ymd,6);
    elsif ADD_MONTHS (w_gyul_ymd, 9) between p_fymd AND p_tymd  then    RETURN  ADD_MONTHS(w_gyul_ymd,9);
    end if;

    RETURN  NULL;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_BUNGI_YMD (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_mm                           IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN DATE IS
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_temp                       DATE;
    w_year                       VARCHAR (4);
BEGIN
    w_temp  := LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(p_ymd, p_mm));
    w_year  := TO_CHAR (LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(p_ymd, p_mm)), 'yyyy');

    if  TO_CHAR (w_temp, 'mmdd') between '1001' AND '1231'      then    w_ymd   := TO_DATE(w_year||'1231','yyyymmdd');
    elsif TO_CHAR (w_temp, 'mmdd') between '0701' AND '0930'    then    w_ymd   := TO_DATE(w_year||'0930','yyyymmdd');
    elsif TO_CHAR (w_temp, 'mmdd') between '0401' AND '0630'    then    w_ymd   := TO_DATE(w_year||'0630','yyyymmdd');
    else                                                                w_ymd   := TO_DATE(w_year||'0331','yyyymmdd');
    end if;

    RETURN  w_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_CASH_DANGA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    i_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,  /* 종목코드 */
    i_trns_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 평가일자 */
    i_mk_suik_rt                   IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 현금자산의 단가
--
    w_jan_yy                     NUMBER;
    w_jan_dd                     NUMBER;

    w_chui_aek                   NUMBER;
    w_danga                      NUMBER;
    w_misu_ija                   NUMBER;
    w_sung_cost                  NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  i_mk_suik_rt = 0    then    RETURN  0;
    end if;

    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT cash_cd      /* _1- */
             , pyom_iyul    /* _2- */
             , balh_ymd     /* _3- */
             , sanghw_ymd   /* _4- */
             , meib_mk_gb   /* _5- */
             , cd_jigub_gb  /* _6- */
             , sunhu_gb     /* _7- */
          FROM SHM0HJ t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.jm_cd   = i_jm_cd
    ) loop
        if (xx.cash_cd = '20') OR (xx.cash_cd = '60' AND xx.meib_mk_gb = '1')   then
            w_sung_cost := 10000 * i_mk_suik_rt * (i_trns_ymd - xx.balh_ymd) / 365;
            w_misu_ija  := 10000 * i_mk_suik_rt * (xx.sanghw_ymd - xx.balh_ymd) / 365;

            if  xx.sunhu_gb = '2'   then
                    w_chui_aek  := 10000;
            else    w_chui_aek  := 10000 - w_misu_ija;
            end if;

            w_danga := w_chui_aek + w_sung_cost;

        elsif (xx.cash_cd = '60') AND (xx.meib_mk_gb = '2')     then
            w_jan_yy    := TRUNC((xx.sanghw_ymd - i_trns_ymd) / 365);

            if  w_jan_yy < 0    then    w_jan_yy    := 0;
            end if;

            w_jan_dd    := (xx.sanghw_ymd - i_trns_ymd) - (w_jan_yy * 365);

            if  xx.cd_jigub_gb = '1'    then    -- 할인식
                w_danga := 10000 / (Power(1 + i_mk_suik_rt, w_jan_yy) * (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * w_jan_dd / 365));

            elsif xx.cd_jigub_gb = '2'  then    -- 액면식
                w_misu_ija  := 10000 * xx.pyom_iyul * (xx.sanghw_ymd - xx.balh_ymd) / 365;
                w_danga     := (10000 + w_misu_ija) / (Power(1 + i_mk_suik_rt, w_jan_yy) * (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * w_jan_dd / 365));
            end if;
        else
            RETURN  -1;
        end if;

        RETURN  w_danga / 10000;
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  -1;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_CASH_YUHYO_RT (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,  /* 종목코드 */
    p_trns_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 평가일자 */
    p_mk_suik_rt                   IN    NUMBER,   /* 수익률 */
    p_sanghw_ymd                   IN    DATE,
    p_jangbu_danga                 IN    NUMBER    /* 장부단가 */
 ) RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 현금 유효이자율
--
    w_suik_rt                    NUMBER := 0; -- 수익률
    w_suik_rt_gaj                NUMBER;

    danga                        NUMBER := 0;
    old_sign                     NUMBER;
    new_sign                     NUMBER;
    w_try_cnt                    NUMBER DEFAULT 0;
BEGIN
    if NVL (p_mk_suik_rt,0) = 0 then
        SELECT NVL(MAX(jy_suik_rt),0) / 100
          INTO w_suik_rt
          FROM SHM1SM t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_trns_ymd - 1
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    else
        w_suik_rt   := p_mk_suik_rt;
    end if;

    -- 상환일이 지난경우는 계산이 안된다.
    if  p_sanghw_ymd <= p_trns_ymd  then    RETURN  w_suik_rt;
    end if;

    danga         := F_CASH_DANGA (p_corp_gr, p_jm_cd, p_trns_ymd, w_suik_rt) * 10000;
    old_sign      := sign(p_jangbu_danga - danga);
    w_suik_rt_gaj := abs(danga / p_jangbu_danga - 1) * 0.69;
    WHILE true LOOP     -- 수익율과 단가는 반비례, 수익율을 증감시키며 원하는 단가를 찾는다
        w_suik_rt := w_suik_rt - (w_suik_rt_gaj * old_sign);
        danga     := F_CASH_DANGA (p_corp_gr, p_jm_cd, p_trns_ymd, w_suik_rt) * 10000;

        -- 장부단가와의 차이가 0.00001원 미만이면 맞는걸로 간주한다.
        if  abs (p_jangbu_danga - danga) < 0.00001  then    RETURN  TRUNC(w_suik_rt,9);
        end if;

        new_sign    := sign(p_jangbu_danga - danga);  -- 장부단가와 차이를 부호로 표시하여 체크한다.

        if  old_sign != new_sign    then
            w_suik_rt_gaj := w_suik_rt_gaj * .01;
            old_sign      := new_sign;
        end if;

        w_try_cnt   := w_try_cnt + 1;
        if  w_try_cnt > 500 then    RETURN  -1;
        end if;
    END LOOP;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_CHECK (
    p_check                        IN    NUMBER,
    p_low                          IN    NUMBER,
    p_high                         IN    NUMBER,
    p_add                          IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
BEGIN
    if  p_low IS NULL   then
        if  SUBSTR (NVL(p_add,'--'),2,1) = '0'  then
            if  p_check >= p_high   then    RETURN  1;
            end if;
        else
            if  p_check > p_high    then    RETURN  1;
            end if;
        end if;

    elsif p_high IS NULL    then
        if  SUBSTR (NVL(p_add,'--'),2,1) = '0'  then
            if  p_check <= p_low    then    RETURN  1;
            end if;
        else
            if  p_check < p_low     then    RETURN  1;
            end if;
        end if;
    else
        if  NOT (p_check between p_low AND p_high)  then    RETURN  1;
        end if;
    end if;
    RETURN  0;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_CLOB2STR (
    p_clob                         IN    clob
 ) RETURN VARCHAR IS
    BEGINNING                    INTEGER := 32700;
    ENDING                       INTEGER := 1;
    w_str                        VARCHAR (32700) DEFAULT '';
BEGIN
    if  p_clob is not null  then
        DBMS_LOB.READ (p_clob, BEGINNING, ENDING, w_str);
    else    w_str := ' ';
    end if;
    RETURN  w_str;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_COMMISSION (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    VARCHAR
 ) RETURN COMMISSION_TAB AS
-- 성과보수 현황
--
    w_tab                        COMMISSION_TAB;

    w_ymd                        DATE;

    w_nav                        NUMBER;
    w_pj                         NUMBER;

    w_baedang                    NUMBER;
    w_ija                        NUMBER;
    w_bond_sonik                 NUMBER;

    w_stock_pyunga               NUMBER;
    w_bond_pyunga                NUMBER;

    w_sonik                      NUMBER;
    w_pyunga_sonik               NUMBER;

    w_commission_sonik           NUMBER;
    w_commission                 NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_ymd := TO_DATE (p_ymd,'yyyymmdd');
    w_tab := COMMISSION_TAB();

    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT t1.FUND_CD                                               /* _1- */
             , NVL(t1.BM_PER,0) / 100                 AS bm_rt          /* _2- */
             , NVL(t1.SUCCESS_PER,0) / 100            AS commission_rt  /* _3- */
             , t1.TARGET_JASAN                                          /* _4- */
             , gi.BF_GYUL_YMD + 1                     AS bf             /* _5- */
             , NVL(gi.haeji_ymd - 1, gi.AF_GYUL_YMD)  AS af             /* _6- */
          FROM SZM0IA t1
             , SZM0GI gi
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.FUND_CD LIKE p_fund_cd
           AND gi.CORP_GR = t1.CORP_GR
           AND gi.FUND_CD = t1.FUND_CD
           AND p_ymd      Between gi.BF_GYUL_YMD + 1 AND gi.AF_GYUL_YMD
    ) loop
        if  xx.af > p_ymd   then    xx.af   := p_ymd;
        end if;

        SELECT NVL(SUM(t1.sun_jasan_aek),0)
          INTO w_nav
          FROM SKT0GI t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
           AND t1.ymd     = xx.af
           AND t1.work_gb = 'A';

        SELECT NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK IN ('60300','60400')                 THEN NVL(t1.JUNJ, 0) - NVL(t1.DR_AEK, 0) + NVL(t1.CR_AEK, 0) END),0)  /* _1- */
             , NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK='60500'                              THEN NVL(t1.JUNJ,0) - NVL(t1.DR_AEK,0) + NVL(t1.CR_AEK,0) END),0)     /* _2- */
             , NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK='90102'                              THEN NVL(t1.JUNJ,0) + NVL(t1.DR_AEK,0) END),0)                        /* _3- */
             , NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK='A0202'                              THEN NVL(t1.JUNJ,0) + NVL(t1.DR_AEK,0) END),0)                        /* _4- */
             , NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK='A0402'                              THEN NVL(t1.JUNJ,0) + NVL(t1.DR_AEK,0) - NVL(t1.CR_AEK,0) END),0)     /* _5- */
             , NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK IN ('A02A2','A02A3','A0231','A02A1') THEN NVL(t1.JUNJ, 0) + NVL(t1.DR_AEK, 0) END),0)                      /* _6- */
          INTO w_baedang       /* _1- */
             , w_ija           /* _2- */
             , w_pyunga_sonik  /* _3- */
             , w_bond_pyunga   /* _4- */
             , w_pj            /* _5- */
             , w_stock_pyunga  /* _6- */
          FROM SKM0AG t1
         WHERE CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND YMD     = xx.af
           AND FUND_CD = xx.fund_cd
           AND WORK_GB = 'A'
           AND GWAMOK  IN ('60300','60400','60500','90102','A0202','A0402','A02A2','A02A3','A0231','A02A1');

        if  xx.target_jasan = '--'  then    -- 성과보수 없음
            w_tab.extend;
            w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := COMMISSION_REC (xx.fund_cd, NULL, NULL, 0, 0);

        elsif xx.target_jasan = '++'    then    -- 계약원금초과
            w_tab.extend;

            w_sonik        := w_nav - w_pj;
            w_pyunga_sonik := 0;

            w_commission_sonik  := w_sonik - TRUNC (w_nav * xx.bm_rt);
            if  w_commission_sonik < 0      then
                    w_commission    := 0;
            else    w_commission    := TRUNC (w_commission_sonik * xx.commission_rt, -4);
            end if;
            w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := COMMISSION_REC (xx.fund_cd, w_sonik, w_pyunga_sonik, w_commission_sonik, w_commission);

        elsif xx.target_jasan = '+J'    then    -- 주식손익
            SELECT NVL(SUM(NVL(t1.gyul_aek,0) - NVL(t1.meib_chui_aek,0) - NVL(ja.CY_SUSU,0)),0)
              INTO w_sonik
              FROM SJT0JG t1
              Left OUTER JOIN ( SELECT b.sj_jm_cd             AS jm_cd
                                     , NVL(SUM(a.cy_susu),0)  AS cy_susu
                                  FROM SJG1FC a
                                     , SJG1JC b
                                 WHERE a.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                   AND a.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
                                   AND b.JC_JOIN = a.JC_JOIN
                                   AND b.sj_ymd  Between ADD_MONTHS (xx.bf,-6) AND xx.af
                                 GROUP BY b.sj_jm_cd ) ja  ON ja.jm_cd = t1.jm_cd
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
               AND t1.tr_ymd  Between xx.bf AND xx.af
               AND t1.tr_cd   LIKE 'K%';

            w_commission_sonik  := w_sonik + w_stock_pyunga;
            if  w_commission_sonik < 0      then
                    w_commission    := 0;
            else    w_commission    := TRUNC (w_commission_sonik * xx.commission_rt, -4);
            end if;

            w_tab.extend;
            w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := COMMISSION_REC (xx.fund_cd, w_sonik, w_stock_pyunga, w_commission_sonik, w_commission);

        else    -- 기간손익
            w_sonik := w_nav - w_pj;
            if  xx.target_jasan = '-C'      then    -- 채권손익 제외
                SELECT NVL(SUM(CASE When t1.GWAMOK IN ('50105','50201')
                                    THEN (NVL(t1.JUNJ,0) + NVL(t1.DR_AEK,0) - NVL(t1.CR_AEK,0)) * -1
                                    ELSE NVL(t1.JUNJ,0) - NVL(t1.DR_AEK,0) + NVL(t1.CR_AEK,0) END),0)
                  INTO w_bond_sonik
                  FROM SKM0AG t1
                 WHERE CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
                   AND YMD     = xx.af
                   AND FUND_CD = xx.fund_cd
                   AND WORK_GB = 'A'
                   AND GWAMOK  IN ('50105','50201','6015','60142','60201','60202');

                w_sonik        := w_sonik - w_bond_sonik - w_ija;
                w_pyunga_sonik := w_pyunga_sonik - w_bond_pyunga;
            end if;
            w_commission_sonik  := w_sonik + w_pyunga_sonik;
            if  w_commission_sonik < 0      then
                    w_commission    := 0;
            else    w_commission    := TRUNC (w_commission_sonik * xx.commission_rt, -4);
            end if;

            w_tab.extend;
            w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := COMMISSION_REC (xx.fund_cd, w_sonik, w_pyunga_sonik, w_commission_sonik, w_commission);
        end if;
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_companycode (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 
--
BEGIN
    RETURN  p_corp_gr;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_corp_gr_nm (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 운용사명
--
    w_company_name               szx0aa.company_name%type;
BEGIN
    if NVL (p_corp_gr,'0000') = '0000'  then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT COMPANY_NAME
          INTO w_company_name
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_company_name  := p_corp_gr;
    END;

    RETURN  w_company_name;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_COVCUR (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_currency                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_aek                          IN    NUMBER,
    p_fund_currency                IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 외화(원화)를 외화로 환산처리
--
    w_won_aek                    NUMBER;
    w_ret                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_currency = p_fund_currency    then
        RETURN p_aek;
    else
        if  p_currency = 'KRW'  then
            w_won_aek   := p_aek;
        else
            BEGIN
                SELECT trunc(p_aek * gijun_rt)
                  INTO w_won_aek
                  FROM SYX1HY t1
                 WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
                   AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                BEGIN
                    SELECT trunc(p_aek * gijun_rt)
                      INTO w_won_aek
                      FROM SYX0HY t1
                     WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
                       AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                    RETURN -1;
                END;
            END;
        end if;
        if  p_fund_currency = 'KRW'     then
            w_ret   := trunc (w_won_aek);
        else
            BEGIN
                SELECT F_ROUND (w_won_aek / gijun_rt,p_fund_currency)
                  INTO w_ret
                  FROM SYX1HY t1
                 WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
                   AND t1.currency  = p_fund_currency;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                BEGIN
                    SELECT F_ROUND (w_won_aek / gijun_rt,p_fund_currency)
                      INTO w_ret
                      FROM SYX0HY t1
                     WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
                       AND t1.currency  = p_fund_currency;
                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                    RETURN -1;
                END;
            END;
        end if;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_ret;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_COVWON (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_currency                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_tr_aek                       IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 외화액을 원화로 환산처리
--
    w_ret                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT trunc(gijun_rt * p_tr_aek)
          INTO w_ret
          FROM SYX1HY t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
           AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        BEGIN
            SELECT trunc(gijun_rt * p_tr_aek)
              INTO w_ret
              FROM SYX0HY t1
             WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
               AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            w_ret   := -1;
        END;
    END;
    RETURN  w_ret;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_COVWON_TR (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_currency                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_tr_aek                       IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 외화액을 원화로 환산처리(거래환율)
--
    w_trans_rt                   NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT gijun_rt
          INTO w_trans_rt
          FROM SYX1HY t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
           AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        BEGIN
            SELECT trans_rt
              INTO w_trans_rt
              FROM SYX0HY t1
             WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
               AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            w_trans_rt  := -1;
        END;
    END;

    RETURN  NVL(TRUNC(NVL(p_tr_aek,0) * w_trans_rt,0),0);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_currency_enm (
    p_currency                     IN    varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 통화 영문명
--
    w_currency_enm               szx0wa.currency_enm%type;
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT currency_enm
          INTO w_currency_enm
          FROM SZX0WA t1
         WHERE t1.currency = p_currency
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    exception when OTHERS then
        RETURN  p_currency;
    end;

    RETURN  w_currency_enm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_currency_gb (
    p_currency                     IN    varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 통화국가여부
--
    w_currency_gb                varchar(1);
BEGIN
    SELECT MIN(currency_gb)
      INTO w_currency_gb
      FROM SZX0WA t1
     WHERE t1.currency = p_currency;

    RETURN  w_currency_gb;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_currency_nm (
    p_currency                     IN    varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 통화 한글명
--
    w_currency_nm                szx0wa.currency_nm%type;
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT currency_nm
          INTO w_currency_nm
          FROM SZX0WA t1
         WHERE t1.currency = p_currency
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    exception when OTHERS then
        RETURN  p_currency;
    end;

    RETURN  w_currency_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_currency_rt (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    varchar,
    p_ymd                          IN    date,
    p_currency                     IN    varchar
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 기준일 환율구하기
--
    w_currency_rt                number;
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT gijun_rt
          INTO w_currency_rt
          FROM SYX1HY t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
           AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
    exception when OTHERS then
        begin
            SELECT gijun_rt
              INTO w_currency_rt
              FROM SYX0HY t1
             WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
               AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
        exception when OTHERS then
            w_currency_rt   := -1;
        end;
    end;

    RETURN  w_currency_rt;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_currency_rt1 (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    varchar,
    p_ymd                          IN    date,
    p_currency                     IN    varchar
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 기준일 환율구하기(당일 환율이 모두 0인 경우 전일 환율로)
--
    w_ymd                        date;
    w_cnt1                       number;
    w_cnt2                       number;

    w_currency_rt                number;
BEGIN
    SELECT NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN gijun_rt=0 THEN 1 END), 0)
         , COUNT(*)
      INTO w_cnt1
         , w_cnt2
      FROM SYX0HY t1
     WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd;

    -- 아직 환율 LOAD 전이면 전일 환율 사용
    w_ymd   := p_ymd;
    if  w_cnt1 = w_cnt2 then    w_ymd   := p_ymd - 1;
    end if;

    begin
        SELECT gijun_rt
          INTO w_currency_rt
          FROM SYX1HY t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.gijun_ymd = w_ymd
           AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
    exception when OTHERS then
        begin
            SELECT gijun_rt
              INTO w_currency_rt
              FROM SYX0HY t1
             WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = w_ymd
               AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
        exception when OTHERS then
            w_currency_rt   := -1;
        end;
    end;

    RETURN  w_currency_rt;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_CURRENCY_SORT (
    p_currency                     IN    varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 통화 SORT 순서
--
    w_sort                       varchar(20);
BEGIN
    if  p_currency IN ('USD','CNY','HKD','JPY','EUR')   then
            w_sort  := '1';
    else    w_sort  := '2';
    end if;

    begin
        SELECT w_sort || MIN(currency_gb) || currency
          INTO w_sort
          FROM SZX0WA t1
         WHERE t1.currency = p_currency
         GROUP BY currency;
    exception when OTHERS then
        RETURN  '99' || p_currency;
    end;

    RETURN  w_sort;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DANC_NM (
    p_jm_cd                        IN    varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 주식 단축식별자명
--
    w_danc_nm                    varchar(40);
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT CASE danc_gb WHEN 'A' THEN '(시장)'
                            WHEN 'B' THEN '(비상장)'
                            WHEN 'C' THEN '(코스닥)'
                            WHEN 'D' THEN '(코넥스)'
                            WHEN 'G' THEN '(ETN)'
                                     ELSE '(권리주)'
               END
          INTO w_danc_nm
          FROM SJM0JJ t1
         WHERE t1.jm_cd = p_jm_cd;
    exception when OTHERS then
        w_danc_nm   := p_jm_cd;
    end;

    RETURN  w_danc_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_danga_gb (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_ymd                       IN        date,
    p_jm_cd                     IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 예수원천세 대상 CHECK
--
    w_sunme_yn                  varchar(1);
    w_ija_rak_hoisu             number;
    w_bf_ija_ymd                date;
    w_af_ija_ymd                date;
    w_msg                       varchar(100);
BEGIN
    -- 과세방법이 보유기간 과세로 변경됨에 따라 모두 세전단가 사용
    RETURN  '0';
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DANGJ (
    p_chadae_gb                    IN    VARCHAR,
    p_junj                         IN    NUMBER,
    p_dr_aek                       IN    NUMBER,
    p_cr_aek                       IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 계정잔액(전잔이 없는경우 당일금액)
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_aek   := CASE p_chadae_gb WHEN 'D' THEN NVL(p_junj,0) + NVL(p_dr_aek,0) - NVL(p_cr_aek,0)
                                WHEN 'C' THEN NVL(p_junj,0) - NVL(p_dr_aek,0) + NVL(p_cr_aek,0)
                                         ELSE 0 END;
    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DATE_COLOR (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER DETERMINISTIC IS
-- 일자별 컬러값
--
    w_holi_ymd                   DATE;
    w_color                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd IS NULL   then    RETURN 2960685;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT holi_ymd
          INTO w_holi_ymd
          FROM SZX0HL t1
         WHERE nation_cd = 'KR'
           AND holi_ymd  = trunc(p_ymd,'dd');

        RETURN 255;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        NULL;
    END;

    -- holiday 등록되지 않은 해
    if  INSTR ('0101,0301,0501,0505,0606,0815,1003,1225',TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'mmdd')) > 0 then    RETURN 255; -- 양력휴일 Check
    end if;
    if  TO_CHAR (p_ymd, 'd') IN ('1','7')   then    RETURN 255;
    end if;

    RETURN 2960685;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DATE_GUGAN_COLOR (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER DETERMINISTIC IS
-- 일자구간 컬러값
--
    w_days                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_fymd IS NULL  then    RETURN 255;
    end if;
    if  p_tymd IS NULL  then    RETURN 255;
    end if;

    w_days  := ABS(F_DAYS (p_tymd, p_fymd));

    if  w_days > 30     then    RETURN 16719390;
    elsif w_days > 14   then    RETURN 8388736;
    end if;

    RETURN 2960685;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DAY365 (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER DETERMINISTIC IS
-- 일수(한편넣기)
--
    w_days                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT p_tymd - p_fymd INTO w_days FROM DUAL;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_days  := 0;
    END;
    if  w_days = 366    then    w_days  := 365;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_days;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DAYS (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER DETERMINISTIC IS
-- 일수(한편넣기)
--
    w_days                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT p_tymd - p_fymd INTO w_days FROM DUAL;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_days  := 0;
    END;
    RETURN  w_days;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DCF_ILSU (
    p_ija_dcf                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_bf_ymd                       IN    DATE,
    p_af_ymd                       IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 해외채권 계산일수에 따른 일수계산
--
    w_ilsu                       NUMBER;
    w_mod_ymd                    DATE;

    d1                           NUMBER;
    d2                           NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        if  p_ija_dcf = '1' then
            w_ilsu  := p_af_ymd - p_bf_ymd;     -- act/act

        elsif p_ija_dcf = '4'   then    -- 30/360
            w_ilsu  := (TO_CHAR (p_af_ymd, 'yyyy') - TO_CHAR (p_bf_ymd, 'yyyy')) * 360 + (TO_CHAR (p_af_ymd, 'mm') - TO_CHAR (p_bf_ymd, 'mm')) * 30;

            if  p_bf_ymd = LAST_DAY (p_bf_ymd)      then
                    d1  := 30;
            else    d1  := TO_CHAR (p_bf_ymd, 'dd');
            end if;
            if  p_af_ymd = LAST_DAY (p_af_ymd)      then
                    d2  := 30;
            else    d2  := TO_CHAR (p_af_ymd, 'dd');
            end if;

            w_ilsu  := w_ilsu + (d2 - d1);

        elsif p_ija_dcf = '5'   then    -- 30E/360
            if  TO_CHAR (p_bf_ymd, 'yyyymm') = TO_CHAR (p_af_ymd, 'yyyymm')     then
                w_ilsu  := LEAST (TO_CHAR (p_af_ymd, 'dd'), '30') - LEAST (TO_CHAR (p_bf_ymd, 'dd'), '30');
            else
                w_ilsu  := (TRUNC (months_between (p_af_ymd,LAST_DAY (p_bf_ymd) + 1)) * 30) + (30 - LEAST (TO_CHAR (p_bf_ymd, 'dd'), '30')) + LEAST (TO_CHAR (p_af_ymd, 'dd'), '30');
            end if;
        else
            if  TRUNC (months_between(p_af_ymd,p_bf_ymd)) = 0   then
                w_ilsu  := 0;
            else
                if  p_ija_dcf = '2' then
                        w_ilsu  := 365 / (12 / TRUNC (months_between(p_af_ymd,p_bf_ymd)));   -- act/365
                else    w_ilsu  := 360 / (12 / TRUNC (months_between(p_af_ymd,p_bf_ymd)));  -- act/360
                end if;
            end if;
            if  TRUNC (months_between(p_af_ymd,p_bf_ymd)) != months_between (p_af_ymd, p_bf_ymd)    then
                w_mod_ymd   := ADD_MONTHS (p_bf_ymd,TRUNC (months_between (p_af_ymd,p_bf_ymd)));
                w_ilsu      := TRUNC (w_ilsu) + (p_af_ymd - w_mod_ymd);
            end if;
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_ilsu  := 0;
    END;
    RETURN  w_ilsu;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DGTVR (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_port_num                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DGTVR_TAB AS
--
-- 델타,감마,세타,베가,로
--
    w_tab                        DGTVR_TAB;

    w_year_T                     NUMBER;
    W_year_sigma                 NUMBER;

    w_r                          NUMBER;
    w_d1                         NUMBER;
    w_d2                         NUMBER;

    call_delta                   NUMBER;
    put_delta                    NUMBER;
    w_gamma                      NUMBER;
    w_vega                       NUMBER;
    call_theta                   NUMBER;
    put_theta                    NUMBER;
    call_rho                     NUMBER;
    put_rho                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := DGTVR_TAB();

    SELECT IJA_PER / 100
      INTO w_r
      FROM SYM0YA t1
     WHERE CORP_GR  = p_corp_gr
       AND JASAN_GB = '5'
       AND JM_NM    = '기준금리'
       AND CURRENCY = 'USD'
       AND BALH_YMD = (SELECT MAX(BALH_YMD)
                         FROM SYM0YA h1
                        WHERE CORP_GR  = p_corp_gr
                          AND JASAN_GB = '5'
                          AND JM_NM    = '기준금리'
                          AND CURRENCY = 'USD'
                          AND p_ymd    Between BALH_YMD AND SANGHW_YMD);

    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT t1.TT
             , t2.JONGA          AS S
             , t1.DT             AS T
             , t2.PUT_PRICE       AS K
             , t2.TT_VOLATILITY  AS sigma
          FROM USPM_PORT t1
             , USPM      t2
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.FUND_CD  = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.TYMD     = p_ymd
           AND t1.PORT_NUM = p_port_num
           AND t2.CORP_GR  = t1.CORP_GR
           AND t2.FUND_CD  = t1.FUND_CD
           AND t2.YMD      = t1.TYMD
           AND t2.JM_CD    = p_jm_cd
           AND t2.PORT_NUM = t1.PORT_NUM
    ) loop
        w_year_T        := xx.T / 365;
        w_year_sigma    := xx.sigma * (365 / xx.T) ** (1 / 2);

        call_delta  := F_STD_CDF ((LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + POWER(w_year_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T)));
        put_delta   := F_STD_CDF ((LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + POWER(w_year_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T))) - 1;

        w_gamma := F_STD_PDF ((LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + POWER(w_year_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T))) / (xx.S * w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T));

        w_d1    := (LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + 0.5 * w_year_sigma * w_year_sigma) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T));
        w_d2    := w_d1 - w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T);

        call_theta  := - (xx.S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * w_year_sigma) / (2 * SQRT(w_year_T)) - w_r * xx.K * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (w_d2);
        put_theta   := - (xx.S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * w_year_sigma) / (2 * SQRT(w_year_T)) + w_r * xx.K * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF ( - w_d2);

        w_vega  := xx.S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * SQRT(w_year_T) / 100;

        call_rho    := xx.K * w_year_T * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (w_d2) / 100;
        put_rho     := - xx.K * w_year_T * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF ( - w_d2) / 100;

        w_tab.extend;
        w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := DGTVR_REC (xx.S, xx.K, xx.T, w_r, xx.sigma, ROUND(w_year_sigma, 4),
                                          ROUND(call_delta, 4), ROUND(put_delta, 4),
                                          ROUND(w_gamma, 6),
                                          ROUND(call_theta / 365, 6), ROUND(put_theta / 365, 6),
                                          ROUND(w_vega, 6),
                                          ROUND(call_rho, 6), ROUND(put_rho, 6));
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  w_tab;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    w_tab.extend;
    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DGTVR_COM (
    p_S                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_T                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_K                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_r                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_sigma                        IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN DGTVR_TAB AS
--
-- 델타,감마,세타,베가,로
--
    w_tab                        DGTVR_TAB;

    w_year_T                     NUMBER;

    w_d1                         NUMBER;
    w_d2                         NUMBER;

    call_delta                   NUMBER;
    put_delta                    NUMBER;
    w_gamma                      NUMBER;
    w_vega                       NUMBER;
    call_theta                   NUMBER;
    put_theta                    NUMBER;
    call_rho                     NUMBER;
    put_rho                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := DGTVR_TAB();

    w_year_T    := p_T / 365;

    call_delta  := F_STD_CDF ((LN(p_S / p_K) + (p_r + POWER(p_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (p_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T)));
    put_delta   := F_STD_CDF ((LN(p_S / p_K) + (p_r + POWER(p_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (p_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T))) - 1;

    w_gamma := F_STD_PDF ((LN(p_S / p_K) + (p_r + POWER(p_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (p_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T))) / (p_S * p_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T));

    w_d1    := (LN(p_S / p_K) + (p_r + 0.5 * p_sigma * p_sigma) * w_year_T) / (p_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T));
    w_d2    := w_d1 - p_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T);

    call_theta  := - (p_S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * p_sigma) / (2 * SQRT(w_year_T)) - p_r * p_K * EXP(- p_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (w_d2);
    put_theta   := - (p_S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * p_sigma) / (2 * SQRT(w_year_T)) + p_r * p_K * EXP(- p_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (- w_d2);

    w_vega  := p_S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * SQRT(w_year_T) / 100;

    call_rho    := p_K * w_year_T * EXP(- p_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (w_d2) / 100;
    put_rho     := - p_K * w_year_T * EXP(- p_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (- w_d2) / 100;

    w_tab.extend;
    w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := DGTVR_REC (p_S, p_K, p_T, p_r, p_Sigma, ROUND(p_sigma, 4),
                                      ROUND(call_delta, 4), ROUND(put_delta, 4),
                                      ROUND(w_gamma, 6),
                                      ROUND(call_theta / 365, 6), ROUND(put_theta / 365, 6),
                                      ROUND(w_vega, 6),
                                      ROUND(call_rho, 6), ROUND(put_rho, 6));

    RETURN  w_tab;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    w_tab.extend;
    w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := DGTVR_REC (p_S, p_K, p_T, p_r, p_Sigma, ROUND(p_sigma, 4),
                                      NULL, NULL,
                                      NULL,
                                      NULL, NULL,
                                      NULL,
                                      NULL, NULL);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DGTVR_K (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_port_num                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DGTVR_TAB AS
--
-- 델타,감마,세타,베가,로
--
    w_tab                        DGTVR_TAB;

    w_year_T                     NUMBER;
    W_year_sigma                 NUMBER;

    w_r                          NUMBER;
    w_d1                         NUMBER;
    w_d2                         NUMBER;

    call_delta                   NUMBER;
    put_delta                    NUMBER;
    w_gamma                      NUMBER;
    w_vega                       NUMBER;
    call_theta                   NUMBER;
    put_theta                    NUMBER;
    call_rho                     NUMBER;
    put_rho                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := DGTVR_TAB();
    w_r     := 0.01;
--    SELECT IJA_PER / 100
--      INTO w_r
--      FROM SYM0YA t1
--     WHERE CORP_GR  = p_corp_gr
--       AND JASAN_GB = '5'
--       AND JM_NM    = '기준금리'
--       AND CURRENCY = 'USD'
--       AND BALH_YMD = (SELECT MAX(BALH_YMD)
--                         FROM SYM0YA h1
--                        WHERE CORP_GR  = p_corp_gr
--                          AND JASAN_GB = '5'
--                          AND JM_NM    = '기준금리'
--                          AND CURRENCY = 'USD'
--                          AND p_ymd    Between BALH_YMD AND SANGHW_YMD);

    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT t1.TT
             , t2.JONGA          AS S
             , t1.DT             AS T
--컬럼 없음            , t2.TT_PRICE       AS K
             , t2.put_PRICE      as K
             , t2.TT_VOLATILITY  AS sigma
          FROM USPM_PORT t1
             , USPM      t2
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.FUND_CD  = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.TYMD     = p_ymd
           AND t1.PORT_NUM = p_port_num
           AND t2.CORP_GR  = t1.CORP_GR
           AND t2.FUND_CD  = t1.FUND_CD
           AND t2.YMD      = t1.TYMD
           AND t2.JM_CD    = p_jm_cd
           AND t2.PORT_NUM = t1.PORT_NUM
    ) loop
        w_year_T        := xx.T / 365;
        w_year_sigma    := xx.sigma * (365 / xx.T) ** (1 / 2);

        call_delta  := F_STD_CDF ((LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + POWER(w_year_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T)));
        put_delta   := F_STD_CDF ((LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + POWER(w_year_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T))) - 1;

        w_gamma := F_STD_PDF ((LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + POWER(w_year_sigma, 2) / 2) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T))) / (xx.S * w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T));

        w_d1    := (LN(xx.S / xx.K) + (w_r + 0.5 * w_year_sigma * w_year_sigma) * w_year_T) / (w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T));
        w_d2    := w_d1 - w_year_sigma * SQRT(w_year_T);

        call_theta  := - (xx.S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * w_year_sigma) / (2 * SQRT(w_year_T)) - w_r * xx.K * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (w_d2);
        put_theta   := - (xx.S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * w_year_sigma) / (2 * SQRT(w_year_T)) + w_r * xx.K * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF ( - w_d2);

        w_vega  := xx.S * F_STD_PDF (w_d1) * SQRT(w_year_T) / 100;

        call_rho    := xx.K * w_year_T * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF (w_d2) / 100;
        put_rho     := - xx.K * w_year_T * EXP( - w_r * w_year_T) * F_STD_CDF ( - w_d2) / 100;

        w_tab.extend;
        w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := DGTVR_REC (xx.S, xx.K, xx.T, w_r, xx.sigma, ROUND(w_year_sigma, 4),
                                          ROUND(call_delta, 4), ROUND(put_delta, 4),
                                          ROUND(w_gamma, 6),
                                          ROUND(call_theta / 365, 6), ROUND(put_theta / 365, 6),
                                          ROUND(w_vega, 6),
                                          ROUND(call_rho, 6), ROUND(put_rho, 6));
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  w_tab;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    w_tab.extend;
    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_DTRUNC (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DATE IS
BEGIN
    RETURN  TRUNC (p_ymd,p_gb);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FULLPATH (
    p_sys_id                       IN    VARCHAR,
    p_pgm_no                       IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- FULL PATH
--
    w_fullpath                     VARCHAR (500);
BEGIN
    WITH t_pgm4path (
               level_no    /* _1- */
             , sys_id      /* _2- */
             , pgm_no      /* _3- */
             , parent_pgm  /* _4- */
             , pgm_nm      /* _5- */
             )
         AS (
             SELECT 1                                          AS level_no    /* _1- */
                  , lz.sys_id                                  AS sys_id      /* _2- */
                  , lz.pgm_no                                  AS pgm_no      /* _3- */
                  , lz.parent_pgm                              AS parent_pgm  /* _4- */
                  , F_N0(lz.sort_order,3) || ':' || lz.pgm_nm  AS pgm_nm      /* _5- */
               FROM FW_PGM_MST lz  /* 프로그램 */
              WHERE lz.sys_id = p_sys_id
                AND lz.pgm_no = p_pgm_no
             UNION ALL
             SELECT level_no + 1                               /* _1- */
                  , lz.sys_id                                  /* _2- */
                  , lz.pgm_no                                  /* _3- */
                  , lz.parent_pgm                              /* _4- */
                  , F_N0(lz.sort_order,3) || ':' || lz.pgm_nm  /* _5- */
               FROM FW_PGM_MST lz  /* 프로그램 */
                  , T_PGM4PATH ly
              WHERE lz.sys_id     = p_sys_id
                AND ly.sys_id     = lz.sys_id
                AND ly.parent_pgm = lz.pgm_no
             )
         -- Main SQL --
         --------------
         SELECT MAX(CASE When level_no=3 THEN lx.pgm_nm END)
                   || ' > ' || MAX(CASE When level_no=2 THEN lx.pgm_nm END)
                   || ' > ' || MAX(CASE When level_no=1 THEN lx.pgm_nm END)
           INTO w_fullpath
           FROM T_PGM4PATH lx;

    RETURN  w_fullpath;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FUND_COLOR (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_fund_cd                   IN        varchar,
    p_ymd                       IN        date default null
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 펀드명 색상
--
    w_ymd                       date;
    w_color                     number;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd is null   then
        SELECT hyun_ymd
          INTO w_ymd
        FROM   szx0aa t1
        WHERE  t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    else
        w_ymd   := p_ymd;
    end if;

    begin
        SELECT CASE WHEN NVL(haeji_ymd,w_ymd) < w_ymd
                         THEN 8421504
                         ELSE 2960685 END
          INTO w_color
        FROM   szm0ia t1
        WHERE  t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
          AND  t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;
    exception when  OTHERS  then
        w_color := 128;
    end;

    RETURN  w_color;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FUND_NM (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nm_gb                        IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'nm'
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 펀드명
--
