    w_fund_nm                    szm0ia.fund_nm%type;
BEGIN
    if  p_fund_cd IS NULL   then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;
    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE p_nm_gb WHEN 'cdnm' THEN fund_cd || ' ' || NVL(fund_nm,'null')
                                        ELSE fund_nm
               END
          INTO w_fund_nm
          FROM SZM0IA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_fund_nm   := p_fund_cd;
    END;

    RETURN  NVL(w_fund_nm,p_fund_cd || ' szm0fd 미등록');
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FUND_SUCCESS (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE DEFAULT NULL
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 성과보수 펀드
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_success                    VARCHAR (2);
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd IS NULL   then
        SELECT hyun_ymd
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    else
        w_ymd   := p_ymd;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE WHEN t1.SUCCESS_PER > 0 THEN 'ok'
                                            ELSE 'no' END
          INTO w_success
          FROM SKT0GS t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd  = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.gyul_ymd = (SELECT MAX(gyul_ymd)
                                FROM SKT0GS ta
                               WHERE ta.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
                                 AND ta.gyul_ymd <= p_ymd);
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        SELECT CASE WHEN t1.SUCCESS_PER > 0 THEN 'ok'
                                            ELSE 'no' END
          INTO w_success
          FROM SZM0IA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;
    END;

    RETURN  w_success;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FUND_SUIK_PER (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN SUIK_PER_TAB AS
-- 펀드수익률(평잔수익률)
--
    w_tab                        SUIK_PER_TAB;

    w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER         NUMBER;
    w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER          NUMBER;
    w_WONBON_AEK                 NUMBER;
    w_NAV                        NUMBER;
    w_FST_PJ                     NUMBER;
    w_RE_PJ                      NUMBER;

    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_af_ymd                     DATE;
    w_fst_seolj_ymd              DATE;
    w_re_seolj_ymd               DATE;

    w_suik_rt                    NUMBER;
    w_pj                         NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  LENGTH (p_ymd) = 10     then
            w_ymd   := TO_DATE (p_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd');
    else    w_ymd   := TO_DATE (p_ymd,'yyyymmdd');
    end if;
    w_tab   := SUIK_PER_TAB();

    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT ia.FUND_CD
             , ia.FST_SEOLJ_YMD
             , ia.RE_SEOLJ_YMD
             , ia.HAEJI_YMD
          FROM SZM0IA ia
         WHERE ia.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ia.fund_cd LIKE p_fund_cd
         ORDER BY fund_cd
    ) loop
        w_fst_seolj_ymd := xx.fst_seolj_ymd;
        w_re_seolj_ymd  := xx.re_seolj_ymd;

        w_suik_rt   := 1;
        FOR x1 IN (
            SELECT t1.bf_gyul_ymd
                 , t1.af_gyul_ymd
              FROM SZM0GI t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
               AND (t1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1)  < w_ymd
             ORDER BY t1.bf_gyul_ymd
        ) loop
            if  xx.haeji_ymd IS NOT NULL    then
                w_af_ymd    := LEAST(x1.af_gyul_ymd, xx.haeji_ymd);
            else
                w_af_ymd    := LEAST(x1.af_gyul_ymd, w_ymd);
            end if;

            SELECT NVL(TRUNC(AVG(INVEST_AMT)),0)
              INTO w_pj
              FROM UZM0HY t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
               AND t1.ymd     Between  (x1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1) AND w_af_ymd;

            if  w_pj != 0   then
                BEGIN
                    SELECT w_suik_rt * t1.NAV / w_pj
                      INTO w_suik_rt
                      FROM UZM0HY t1
                     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                       AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
                       AND t1.ymd     = w_af_ymd;
                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                    CONTINUE;
                END;
            end if;
        END LOOP;

        BEGIN
            SELECT t1.INVEST_AMT
                 , t1.NAV
                 , (w_suik_rt - 1) * 100
                 , CASE WHEN NVL(w_pj,0)=0 THEN 0
                                           ELSE (t1.NAV - t1.INVEST_AMT) / w_pj * 100 END
              INTO w_WONBON_AEK
                 , w_NAV
                 , w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER
                 , w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER
              FROM UZM0HY t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
               AND t1.ymd     = w_ymd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            w_WONBON_AEK            := 0;
            w_NAV                   := 0;
            w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER    := 0;
            w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER     := 0;
        END;

        w_tab.extend;
        w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := SUIK_PER_REC (xx.FUND_CD, w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER, w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER, w_FST_PJ, w_pj, w_WONBON_AEK, w_NAV);
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FUND_SUIK_PER1 (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN SUIK_PER_TAB AS
-- 펀드수익률(평잔수익률) - 순자산제외포함
--
    w_tab                        SUIK_PER_TAB;

    w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER         NUMBER;
    w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER          NUMBER;
    w_WONBON_AEK                 NUMBER;
    w_NAV                        NUMBER;
    w_FST_PJ                     NUMBER;
    w_RE_PJ                      NUMBER;

    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_af_ymd                     DATE;
    w_re_seolj_ymd               DATE;

    w_suik_rt                    NUMBER;
    w_pj                         NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_ymd   := TO_DATE (p_ymd,'yyyymmdd');
    w_tab   := SUIK_PER_TAB();

    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT ia.FUND_CD
             , ia.RE_SEOLJ_YMD
             , ia.HAEJI_YMD
             , aa.CUSTOMER_GR
          FROM SZM0IA ia
             , SZX0AA aa
         WHERE ia.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ia.fund_cd LIKE p_fund_cd
           AND aa.corp_gr = ia.corp_gr
         ORDER BY fund_cd
    ) loop
        w_re_seolj_ymd  := xx.re_seolj_ymd;

        w_suik_rt   := 1;
        FOR x1 IN (
            SELECT t1.bf_gyul_ymd
                 , t1.af_gyul_ymd
              FROM SZM0GI t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
               AND (t1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1)  < w_ymd
             ORDER BY t1.bf_gyul_ymd
        ) loop
            if  xx.haeji_ymd IS NOT NULL    then
                    w_af_ymd    := LEAST(x1.af_gyul_ymd, xx.haeji_ymd);
            else    w_af_ymd    := LEAST(x1.af_gyul_ymd, w_ymd);
            end if;

            BEGIN
                if  xx.customer_gr = '자산운용' then
                    SELECT INVEST_AMT
                      INTO w_pj
                      FROM UZM0HY t1
                     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                       AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
                       AND t1.ymd     = w_af_ymd;
                else
                    SELECT NVL(TRUNC(AVG(INVEST_AMT)),0)
                      INTO w_pj
                      FROM UZM0HY t1
                     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                       AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
                       AND t1.ymd     Between  (x1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1) AND w_af_ymd;
                end if;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                w_pj    := 0;
            END;

            if  w_pj != 0   then
                BEGIN
                    SELECT w_suik_rt * (t1.NAV + NVL(t1.EX_SIGA_AEK,0)) / w_pj
                      INTO w_suik_rt
                      FROM UZM0HY t1
                     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                       AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
                       AND t1.ymd     = w_af_ymd;
                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                    CONTINUE;
                END;
            end if;
        END LOOP;

        BEGIN
            SELECT t1.INVEST_AMT
                 , (t1.NAV + NVL(t1.EX_SIGA_AEK,0))
                 , (w_suik_rt - 1) * 100
                 , CASE WHEN NVL(w_pj,0)=0 THEN 0
                                           ELSE ((t1.NAV + NVL(t1.EX_SIGA_AEK,0)) - t1.INVEST_AMT) / w_pj * 100 END
              INTO w_WONBON_AEK
                 , w_NAV
                 , w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER
                 , w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER
              FROM UZM0HY t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = xx.fund_cd
               AND t1.ymd     = w_ymd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            w_WONBON_AEK            := 0;
            w_NAV                   := 0;
            w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER    := 0;
            w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER     := 0;
        END;

        w_tab.extend;
        w_tab (w_tab.LAST)  := SUIK_PER_REC (xx.FUND_CD, w_FST_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER, w_RE_SEOLJ_SUIK_PER, w_FST_PJ, w_pj, w_WONBON_AEK, w_NAV);
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_FV_ETC (
    i_aekm                         IN    NUMBER,   /* 액면 */
    i_balh_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 발행일 */
    i_sanghw_ymd                   IN    DATE,     /* 만기일 */
    i_medo_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 매출일 */
    i_ija_gigub_gb                 IN    VARCHAR,  /* 이자계산방법 1:단리, 2:복리 */
    i_ija_mons                     IN    NUMBER,   /* 이자지급월수 */
    i_sunhu_gb                     IN    VARCHAR,  /* 이자지급구분 1:선급, 2:후급 */
    i_pyom_iyul                    IN    NUMBER,   /* 표면이자율 */
    i_tot_ija_hoicha               IN    NUMBER,
    i_sunme_gb                     IN    VARCHAR   /* 선매출이자 계산여부 */
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 만기이자채권(복리채, 단리채)의 만기금액을 계산한다.
--
    w_sunme_days                 NUMBER := 0; -- 선매출일수
    w_mangi_mm                   NUMBER; -- 만기월수
    r_mangi_amt                  NUMBER; -- 만기금액
BEGIN
    if  i_sunme_gb = 'Y'    then    w_sunme_days    := i_balh_ymd - i_medo_ymd;    -- 선매출 일수
    end if;

    w_mangi_mm  := ROUND(months_between(i_sanghw_ymd,i_balh_ymd));

    if  i_ija_gigub_gb IN ('4','5','6','D','E','F') then    -- 복리채
        if  i_sunhu_gb = '1'    then
                r_mangi_amt := i_aekm * Power(1 - i_pyom_iyul * i_ija_mons / 12, (w_mangi_mm / i_ija_mons) * -1);   -- 선급
        else    r_mangi_amt := i_aekm * Power(1 + i_pyom_iyul / (12 / i_ija_mons), i_tot_ija_hoicha); -- 후급
        end if;
        if  w_sunme_days > 0    then    r_mangi_amt := r_mangi_amt + (i_aekm * Power(1 + i_pyom_iyul * i_ija_mons / 12, w_sunme_days / 365 / i_ija_mons * 12)); -- 선매출이자
        end if;
    else    -- 단리채
        if  i_sunhu_gb = '1'    then
                r_mangi_amt := i_aekm * (1 + i_pyom_iyul / (1 - i_pyom_iyul) * w_mangi_mm / 12);    -- 선급
        else    r_mangi_amt := i_aekm * (1 + i_pyom_iyul * w_mangi_mm / 12);    -- 후급
        end if;
        if  w_sunme_days > 0    then    r_mangi_amt := r_mangi_amt + i_aekm * i_pyom_iyul * w_sunme_days / 365; -- 선매출이자
        end if;
    end if;

    if  i_ija_gigub_gb IN ('4','5','6','D','E','F') then
        RETURN  TRUNC(r_mangi_amt + 0.00009);   -- 복리채
    else
        RETURN  TRUNC(r_mangi_amt);
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_G20_YMD (
    p_balh_co                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_brok_ymd                     IN    DATE,
    p_baed_mth                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_baej_mth                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_G25_ymd                      IN    DATE
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 배당확정일자 : 2차확정(주총)배당 처리키 위해
--
    w_tr_ymd                     DATE;
BEGIN
    SELECT MAX(tr_ymd)
      INTO w_tr_ymd
      FROM SJT0BK t1
     WHERE t1.corp_gr  = '%'
       AND t1.tr_cd    = 'G20'
       AND t1.tr_ymd   Between p_brok_ymd AND NVL(p_G25_ymd, now ())
       AND t1.balh_co  = p_balh_co
       AND t1.brok_ymd = p_brok_ymd
       AND t1.baed_mth = p_baed_mth
       AND t1.baej_mth = p_baej_mth;

    RETURN  w_tr_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GAEK (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 권리평가액
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT NVL(SUM(NVL(bfil_chui_aek,0) + NVL(up_chui_aek,0)
                   - NVL(dw_chui_aek,0) + NVL(sonik,0)
                   + NVL(gongs_up_chui_aek,0)
                   - NVL(gongs_dw_chui_aek,0)),0)
      INTO w_aek
      FROM SJM0JM t1
     WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND ymd     = p_ymd
       AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
       AND danc_gb NOT IN ('A','B','C','D','G','X');

    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GETDT (
    i_fr_ymd                       IN    DATE,
    i_to_ymd                       IN    DATE
 )  RETURN VARCHAR2 DETERMINISTIC IS
    v_mm                           NUMBER;
BEGIN
    -- 1. 개월 수 계산 (한 번만 수행)
    v_mm    := MONTHS_BETWEEN(i_to_ymd, i_fr_ymd);

    -- 2. 조건에 따른 결과 반환 (큰 숫자부터 비교하여 효율화)
    RETURN CASE When v_mm >= 12 THEN '12'
                When v_mm >= 9  THEN '9'
                When v_mm >= 6  THEN '6'
                When v_mm >= 3  THEN '3'
                                ELSE '0' END;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GETDT3 (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 기준가 전일대비일 구하기
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_chk                        VARCHAR(1);
BEGIN
    w_ymd   := p_ymd - 1;
    LOOP
        BEGIN
            SELECT '1'
              INTO w_chk
              FROM SZX0HL t1
             WHERE t1.nation_cd = 'KR'
               AND t1.holi_ymd  = w_ymd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            EXIT;
        END;
        w_ymd   := w_ymd - 1;
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  (w_ymd - 1);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GETHL_YN (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
--  휴일 여부  Y/N
--
    w_gethl                      VARCHAR(1);
BEGIN

    BEGIN
        SELECT 'Y'
          INTO w_gethl
          FROM SZX0HL t1
         WHERE t1.nation_cd = 'KR'
           AND t1.holi_ymd  = p_ymd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        w_gethl := 'N';
    END;

    RETURN  w_gethl;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GET_GWAMOK (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jasan_gr1                    IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
    w_cash_cd                    VARCHAR (10);
    w_gwamok                     VARCHAR (40);
    w_gwamok_cd                  VARCHAR (40);
    w_gwamok_nm                  VARCHAR (40);
    w_jm_gr                      VARCHAR (1);
    w_tr_cd                      VARCHAR (3);
    w_jasan_gb                   VARCHAR (4);
    w_tr_ymd                     DATE;
BEGIN
    if  p_jasan_gr1 = '1'   then
        BEGIN
            SELECT jm_gr
              INTO w_jm_gr
              FROM UZM0UI t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
               AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_gwamok    := '';
        END;

        if  w_jm_gr = '1'       then    -- 채권
            BEGIN
                SELECT DISTINCT t1.tr_cd
                     , t1.tr_ymd
                     , t2.slip_attr || t2.sangj_gb
                  INTO w_tr_cd
                     , w_tr_ymd
                     , w_jasan_gb
                  FROM SCT0CG t1
                     , SCM0CJ t2
                 WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                   AND t1.tr_cd   LIKE 'J%'
                   AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
                   AND t1.tr_ymd  = (SELECT MAX(tr_ymd)
                                       FROM SCT0CG ta
                                      WHERE ta.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                        AND ta.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                                        AND ta.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd)
                   AND t2.corp_gr = t1.corp_gr
                   AND t2.jm_cd   = t1.jm_cd;
            END;
        elsif w_jm_gr = '2' then    -- 유동성
            BEGIN
                SELECT DISTINCT tr_cd
                     , tr_ymd
                  INTO w_tr_cd
                     , w_tr_ymd
                  FROM SHT0HG t1
                 WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                   AND t1.tr_cd   LIKE 'J%'
                   AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
                   AND t1.tr_ymd  = (SELECT MAX(tr_ymd)
                                       FROM SHT0HG ta
                                      WHERE ta.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                        AND ta.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                                        AND ta.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd);
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                BEGIN
                    SELECT DISTINCT cash_cd
                      INTO w_cash_cd
                      FROM SHM0HJ t1
                     WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                       AND jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
                END;
            END;
        elsif w_jm_gr = '3' then    -- 주식
            BEGIN
                SELECT DISTINCT tr_cd
                     , tr_ymd
                  INTO w_tr_cd
                     , w_tr_ymd
                  FROM SJT0JG t1
                 WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                   AND t1.tr_cd   LIKE 'J%'
                   AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
                   AND t1.tr_ymd  = (SELECT MAX(tr_ymd)
                                       FROM SJT0JG ta
                                      WHERE ta.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                        AND ta.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                                        AND ta.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd);
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                BEGIN
                    SELECT tr_cd
                         , MIN(tr_ymd)
                      INTO w_tr_cd
                         , w_tr_ymd
                      FROM SJT0SK t1
                     WHERE new_jm_cd = p_jm_cd
                     GROUP BY tr_cd;
                EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                    BEGIN
                        SELECT tr_cd
                             , MIN(tr_ymd)
                          INTO w_tr_cd
                             , w_tr_ymd
                          FROM SJT0FS t1
                         WHERE fund_cd = p_fund_cd
                           AND jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
                         GROUP BY tr_cd;
                    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                        w_tr_cd     := '';
                        w_tr_ymd    := '';
                    END;
                END;
            END;
        else
            w_gwamok    := '';
            RETURN  w_gwamok;
        end if;

        if  w_jm_gr = '3'   then
            BEGIN
                SELECT SUBSTR(bigo,-2)
                  INTO w_jasan_gb
                  FROM SKT0BU t1
                 WHERE corp_gr              = p_corp_gr
                   AND fund_cd              = p_fund_cd
                   AND tr_ymd               = w_tr_ymd
                   AND tr_cd                = w_tr_cd
                   AND chadae_gb            = 'D'
                   AND NVL(jm_cd, p_jm_cd)  = p_jm_cd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                w_jasan_gb  := '';
            END;
        end if;

        BEGIN
            SELECT gwamok
              INTO w_gwamok_cd
              FROM SZX0GA t1
             WHERE t1.tr_cd    = w_tr_cd
               AND t1.jasan_gb = w_jasan_gb
               AND t1.row_no   = (SELECT MIN(row_no)
                                    FROM SZX0GA ta
                                   WHERE ta.tr_cd               = w_tr_cd
                                     AND ta.jasan_gb            = w_jasan_gb
                                     AND ta.chadae_gb           = 'D'
                                     AND SUBSTR(ta.gwamok,1,1)  = '1');
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_gwamok_cd := '';
        END;

        if  w_jm_gr = '2'   then
            if  w_cash_cd = '90'    then
                w_gwamok_cd := '10101';
            end if;
        end if;
    else
        w_gwamok_cd := p_jm_cd;
    end if;

    if  p_gb = 'nm' then
        BEGIN
            SELECT gwamok_nm
              INTO w_gwamok_nm
              FROM SZX0GM t1
             WHERE gwamok = w_gwamok_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_gwamok_nm := '';
        END;
        w_gwamok    := w_gwamok_nm;
    else
        w_gwamok    := w_gwamok_cd;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_gwamok;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GET_JASAN_GB (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
--  2018.08
    w_jasan_gb                   VARCHAR (2);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'BO'
          INTO w_jasan_gb
          FROM SCM0CJ t1
         WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CASE cash_cd WHEN '10' THEN 'CP'
                                WHEN '20' THEN 'CP'
                                WHEN '40' THEN 'CP'
                                WHEN '60' THEN 'CD'
                                          ELSE '' END
              INTO w_jasan_gb
              FROM SHM0HJ t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
               AND ROWNUM = 1;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_jasan_gb  := '';
        END;
    END;

    RETURN  w_jasan_gb;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_gi (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 현재 회기계산
--
    w_gyul_gi                    NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT gyul_gi
          INTO w_gyul_gi
          FROM SZM0GI t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND p_ymd      Between  (t1.bf_gyul_ymd + 1) AND t1.af_gyul_ymd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        RETURN  99;
    END;
    RETURN w_gyul_gi;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GIGAN_PER (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_bf_ymd                       IN    DATE,
    p_af_ymd                       IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 기간 가중비율(%)
--
    w_per                        NUMBER;
    w_mm                         NUMBER;

    w_bf_ymd                     DATE;
    w_af_ymd                     DATE;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd between p_bf_ymd AND p_af_ymd then
        w_per   := (p_af_ymd - p_ymd + 1) / (p_af_ymd - p_bf_ymd) * 100;
    else
        w_mm    := ROUND(months_between (p_af_ymd,p_bf_ymd));
        if  w_mm = 0    then    w_mm    := 1;
        end if;
        if  p_ymd < p_bf_ymd    then    w_mm    := w_mm * -1;
        end if;
--        SR_BSS ('1701', 'COM1', to_char (p_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd')||' '||f_ntrim (w_mm),
--                        to_char(p_bf_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd')||' - '||to_char (p_af_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd'));

        w_bf_ymd    := p_bf_ymd;
        w_af_ymd    := p_af_ymd;
        while true loop
            if  w_mm < 0    then
                w_af_ymd    := w_bf_ymd;
                w_bf_ymd    := ADD_MONTHS (w_af_ymd, w_mm);
            else
                w_bf_ymd    := w_af_ymd;
                w_af_ymd    := ADD_MONTHS (w_bf_ymd, w_mm);
            end if;
--            SR_BSS ('1701', 'COM2', to_char (p_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd'),
--                            to_char(w_bf_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd')||' - '||to_char (w_af_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd'));
            if  p_ymd between w_bf_ymd AND w_af_ymd then
                w_per   := (w_af_ymd - p_ymd + 1) / (w_af_ymd - w_bf_ymd) * 100;
                EXIT;
            end if;
        END LOOP;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_per;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GIJUN_YMD (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DATE IS
--
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
BEGIN
    SELECT MAX(t1.gyul_ymd)
      INTO w_ymd
      FROM SKT0GS t1
     WHERE t1.corp_gr         = p_corp_gr
       AND t1.fund_cd         = p_fund_cd
       AND t1.gyulbf_gijun_ga >= 1000;

    if  w_ymd IS NULL   then
        SELECT re_seolj_ymd
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZM0IA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;
    else    w_ymd   := F_OPEN_YMD (w_ymd, '-');
    end if;

    RETURN w_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GROUND (
    p_aek                          IN    NUMBER,
    p_fund_currency                IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 기준통화에 따른 기준가 반올림
--
BEGIN
    if  p_fund_currency = 'KRW' then
            RETURN  ROUND (NVL(p_aek,0),2);
    else    RETURN  ROUND (NVL(p_aek,0),4);
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GR_CD (
    p_comments                     IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
--
--
    w_gr_cd                      VARCHAR (100) := '';
BEGIN
    if  INSTR (p_comments,'[') > 0  then
        w_gr_cd := SUBSTR (p_comments,INSTR (p_comments,'[') + 1);
        w_gr_cd := SUBSTR (w_gr_cd,1,INSTR (w_gr_cd,']') - 1);

    elsif INSTR (p_comments,'{') > 0    then
        w_gr_cd := SUBSTR (p_comments,INSTR (p_comments,'{') + 1);
        w_gr_cd := SUBSTR (w_gr_cd,1,INSTR (w_gr_cd,'}') - 1);
    end if;
    RETURN  w_gr_cd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GSONIK1 (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 권리평가액
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER := 0;
BEGIN
    if  p_corp_gr = '2201'  then
        SELECT NVL(SUM(t1.sonik),0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM SJM0JM t1
             , SZM0IA ia
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
           AND t1.danc_gb NOT IN ('A','B','C','Y','X')
           AND ia.corp_gr = t1.corp_gr
           AND ia.fund_cd = t1.fund_cd
           AND ia.mg_cd   != '00020';

        SELECT w_aek + NVL(SUM(t1.sonik),0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM SJM0JM t1
             , SZM0IA ia
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
           AND t1.danc_gb = 'T'
           AND ia.corp_gr = t1.corp_gr
           AND ia.fund_cd = t1.fund_cd
           AND ia.mg_cd   = '00020';
    end if;

    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GUGAN (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN GUGAN_TAB AS
--
-- 3개월 구간 및 설정일기준 3개월 기준
--
    w_tab                        GUGAN_TAB;

    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_bf_haeji_ymd               DATE;
    w_af_gyul_ymd                DATE;

    r_b3_fymd                    DATE;
    r_b3_tymd                    DATE;
    r_m3_fymd                    DATE;
    r_m3_tymd                    DATE;
    r_m3_gu                      NUMBER := 0;
    r_s3_fymd                    DATE;
    r_s3_tymd                    DATE;
    r_g3b_fymd                   DATE;
    r_g3a_fymd                   DATE;
    r_g3b_tymd                   DATE;
    r_g3a_tymd                   DATE;
    r_g3_gu                      NUMBER := 0;

    w_fst_seolj_ymd              DATE;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT fst_seolj_ymd
             , ADD_MONTHS (af_gyul_ymd,-3) + 1
             , af_gyul_ymd
          INTO w_fst_seolj_ymd
             , w_bf_haeji_ymd
             , w_af_gyul_ymd
          FROM SZM0IA ia
         WHERE ia.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ia.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;

        if  p_ymd < w_fst_seolj_ymd then
            r_b3_fymd   := w_fst_seolj_ymd;
            r_b3_tymd   := p_ymd;
            r_m3_fymd   := w_fst_seolj_ymd;
            r_m3_tymd   := LAST_DAY(p_ymd);
            r_g3a_fymd  := w_fst_seolj_ymd;
            r_g3a_tymd  := p_ymd;
        else
            -- 분기평잔구간(금투협 보고용 분기)
            r_b3_fymd   := ADD_MONTHS (TRUNC (p_ymd,'q') - 1, -3) + 1;
            r_b3_tymd   := TRUNC (p_ymd,'q') - 1;
            if  w_fst_seolj_ymd > r_b3_tymd then
                r_b3_fymd   := p_ymd;
                r_b3_tymd   := p_ymd;

            elsif p_ymd = ADD_MONTHS (r_b3_tymd, 3)     then
                r_b3_fymd   := ADD_MONTHS (r_b3_fymd, 3);
                r_b3_tymd   := ADD_MONTHS (r_b3_tymd, 3);
            end if;

            -- 설정일 3개월평잔구간
            r_m3_gu := 1;
            if  p_ymd between w_fst_seolj_ymd AND ADD_MONTHS (w_fst_seolj_ymd, 3) - 1       then
                r_m3_fymd   := p_ymd;
                r_m3_tymd   := p_ymd;
            else
                r_m3_fymd   := w_fst_seolj_ymd;
                r_m3_tymd   := ADD_MONTHS (r_m3_fymd, 3) - 1;
                LOOP
                    r_m3_gu := r_m3_gu + 1;
                    if  p_ymd between ADD_MONTHS (r_m3_fymd, 3) AND ADD_MONTHS (r_m3_tymd, 3)   then
                        if  p_ymd between w_bf_haeji_ymd AND w_af_gyul_ymd  then    r_m3_gu := 999; -- 만기 3개월 미만인 경우
                        end if;
                        exit;
                    end if;
                    r_m3_fymd   := r_m3_tymd + 1;
                    r_m3_tymd   := ADD_MONTHS (r_m3_tymd, 3);
                END LOOP;
            end if;

            -- 분기말 구간 기준
            -- 분기 round (q) 인 경우 는 3, 6, 9, 12월인 경우 익분기가 나옴(주의필요), - 2 말일은 미포함
            if  p_ymd between w_fst_seolj_ymd AND ADD_MONTHS (TRUNC (w_fst_seolj_ymd,'q'),6) - 1    then
                r_g3_gu     := 1;
                r_g3b_fymd  := w_fst_seolj_ymd;
                r_g3b_tymd  := ADD_MONTHS (TRUNC (w_fst_seolj_ymd,'q'),6) - 1;
                if  p_ymd = r_g3b_tymd  then    r_g3_gu := r_g3_gu + 1;
                end if;
            else
                r_g3_gu     := 3;
                r_g3b_fymd  := ADD_MONTHS (w_fst_seolj_ymd, 3);
                r_g3b_tymd  := ADD_MONTHS (TRUNC (w_fst_seolj_ymd,'q'),6) - 1;
                r_g3a_fymd  := r_g3b_tymd + 1;
                r_g3a_tymd  := ADD_MONTHS (r_g3a_fymd, 3) - 1;
                LOOP
                    if  p_ymd between r_g3a_fymd AND r_g3a_tymd then
                        if  w_bf_haeji_ymd between r_g3b_fymd AND r_g3b_tymd    then
                            r_g3b_tymd  := w_bf_haeji_ymd - 1;
                            r_g3_gu     := 999;
                        end if;
                        exit;
                    end if;
                    r_g3_gu     := r_g3_gu + 1;
                    r_g3b_fymd  := r_g3a_fymd;
                    r_g3b_tymd  := r_g3a_tymd;
                    r_g3a_fymd  := r_g3b_tymd + 1;
                    r_g3a_tymd  := ADD_MONTHS (r_g3a_fymd, 3) - 1;
                END LOOP;
            end if;
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        r_b3_fymd   := ADD_MONTHS (p_ymd, -3) + 1;
        r_b3_tymd   := p_ymd;
        r_m3_tymd   := LAST_DAY (p_ymd);
        r_m3_fymd   := ADD_MONTHS (r_m3_tymd, -3) + 1;
        r_g3a_fymd  := ADD_MONTHS (p_ymd, -3) + 1;
        r_g3a_tymd  := p_ymd;
    END;

    r_s3_fymd   := r_m3_tymd + 1;
    r_s3_tymd   := ADD_MONTHS (r_m3_tymd, 3);

    w_tab   := GUGAN_TAB();
    w_tab.extend;
    w_tab (1)   := GUGAN_REC (r_b3_fymd, r_b3_tymd, r_b3_tymd - r_b3_fymd + 1,
                              r_m3_fymd, r_m3_tymd, r_m3_tymd - r_m3_fymd + 1, r_m3_gu,
                              r_s3_fymd, LEAST (p_ymd, r_s3_tymd), LEAST (p_ymd, r_s3_tymd) - r_s3_fymd + 1,
                              r_g3b_fymd, r_g3b_tymd, r_g3b_tymd - r_g3b_fymd + 1, r_g3_gu,
                              r_g3a_fymd, r_g3a_tymd, r_g3a_tymd - r_g3a_fymd + 1);

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GUGAN_HAN (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 금액을 한글로
--
    w_mm                         NUMBER;
    w_to                         DATE;

    w_han                        VARCHAR (40) := '';
BEGIN
    w_mm    := TRUNC (months_between (p_tymd,p_fymd));
    w_to    := ADD_MONTHS (p_fymd, w_mm);
    if  w_mm >= 12  then
        w_han   := TRUNC (w_mm / 12) || '년';
        w_mm    := w_mm - (TRUNC (w_mm / 12) * 12);
    end if;
    if  w_mm >= 1   then
        if  w_han IS NULL   then
                w_han   := w_mm || '개월';
        else    w_han   := w_han || ' ' || w_mm || '개월';
        end if;
    end if;
    if (p_tymd - w_to) >= 7     then
        if  w_han IS NULL   then
                w_han   := TO_CHAR (TRUNC ((p_tymd - w_to) / 7)) || '주';
        else    w_han   := w_han || ' ' || TO_CHAR (TRUNC ((p_tymd - w_to) / 7)) || '주';
        end if;
        w_to    := w_to + (TRUNC ((p_tymd - w_to) / 7) * 7);
    end if;
    if  w_to < p_tymd   then
        if  w_han IS NULL   then
                w_han   := TO_CHAR (p_tymd - w_to) || '일';
        else    w_han   := w_han || ' ' || TO_CHAR (p_tymd - w_to) || '일';
        end if;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_han;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GWAMOK_AEK (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund                         IN    VARCHAR,
    p_gwamok                       IN    VARCHAR,
    p_work_gb                      IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'A'
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 특정일 특정계정과목 총계정원장 잔액조회
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT CASE t1.chadae_gb WHEN 'D' THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0)
                                      ELSE NVL(t1.junj,0) - NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) + NVL(t1.cr_aek,0) END
      INTO w_aek
      FROM SKM0AG t1
     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund
       AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
       AND t1.gwamok  = p_gwamok
       AND t1.work_gb = p_work_gb;

    RETURN  w_aek;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN 0;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_GWAMOK_GG (
    p_gwamok_gg                    IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jasan                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 분개유형 계정그룹 - 계정과목
--
    w_jasan                      VARCHAR(4);
    w_gwamok                     szx0gm.gwamok%type;
BEGIN
    w_jasan := REPLACE (p_jasan, '[', '');
    w_jasan := REPLACE (w_jasan, ']', '');
    if  p_gwamok_gg LIKE '[%]'  then
        SELECT GWAMOK
          INTO w_gwamok
          FROM (SELECT t1.CPOS
                     , gm.GWAMOK
                     , ja.COLUMN_VALUE
                  FROM SZX0GG t1
                     , SZX0GM gm
                     , TABLE (F_SPLIT(gm.JASAN,',')) ja
                 WHERE t1.GWAMOK_GG = p_gwamok_gg
                   AND gm.JASAN IS NOT NULL
                   AND gm.GWAMOK_GG = t1.GWAMOK_GG) tt
         WHERE CASE tt.CPOS WHEN 'R1' THEN SUBSTR (w_jasan, LENGTH(w_jasan),1)
                            WHEN 'L1' THEN SUBSTR (w_jasan,1,1)
                                      ELSE w_jasan END = tt.COLUMN_VALUE;
    else
        w_gwamok    := p_gwamok_gg;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_gwamok;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN NULL;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_gwamok_nm (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_gwamok                       IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nm_gb                        IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'nm'
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 계정과목명
--
    w_gwamok_nm                  szx0gm.gwamok_fnm%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE WHEN p_nm_gb='fnm' THEN gwamok_fnm
                                       ELSE CASE WHEN gwamok_gb='9' THEN gwamok_fnm
                                                                    ELSE gwamok_nm END
               END
          INTO w_gwamok_nm
          FROM SZX0GM t1
         WHERE gwamok = p_gwamok;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        if  p_nm_gb = 'tr_co_cd'    then    -- 자가진단시 계정코드와 매매처코드 사용으로
            BEGIN
                SELECT tr_co_nm
                  INTO w_gwamok_nm
                  FROM SZX2MM t1
                 WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.tr_co_cd = p_gwamok;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                w_gwamok_nm := p_gwamok;
            END;
        else
            w_gwamok_nm := p_gwamok;
        end if;
    END;
    RETURN  w_gwamok_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_HIGH_LOW (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
 ) RETURN HIGH_LOW_TAB AS
--
-- 구간 최고가, 최저가
--
    w_tab                        HIGH_LOW_TAB;

    w_jonga                      NUMBER;

    w_max_ymd                    DATE;
    w_max_jonga                  NUMBER;
    w_min_ymd                    DATE;
    w_min_jonga                  NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := HIGH_LOW_TAB( );

    FOR jm IN (
        SELECT DISTINCT t1.JM_CD
             , t1.OVRS_EXCG_CD
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = p_tymd
    ) loop
        w_max_jonga := 1;
        w_min_jonga := 999999999;
        if  jm.ovrs_excg_cd = 'KRX' then
            FOR xx IN (
                SELECT t1.YMD
                     , NVL(t1.CLOSE,0)                                  AS jonga
                     , LAG(NVL(t1.CLOSE,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.KOSCOM_CD
                                                      ORDER BY t1.YMD)  AS bf_jonga
                     , CASE When LAG(NVL(t1.CLOSE,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.KOSCOM_CD
                                                                ORDER BY t1.YMD)=0
                            THEN 0
                            ELSE NVL(t1.CLOSE,0) / LAG(NVL(t1.CLOSE,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.KOSCOM_CD
                                                                                  ORDER BY t1.YMD) - 1
                       END                                              AS rt
                  FROM SJT1TG t1
                 WHERE t1.YMD       BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd
                   AND t1.YMD       NOT IN ( SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                               FROM SZX0HL ta
                                              WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'KR'
                                                AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd )
                   AND t1.KOSCOM_CD = jm.JM_CD
                 ORDER BY t1.YMD DESC
            ) loop
                if  xx.rt IS NULL   then    CONTINUE;
                end if;

                if  xx.ymd = p_tymd then    w_jonga := xx.jonga;
                end if;
                if  xx.jonga > w_max_jonga  then
                    w_max_jonga := xx.jonga;
                    w_max_ymd   := xx.ymd;
                end if;
                if  xx.jonga < w_min_jonga  then
                    w_min_jonga := xx.jonga;
                    w_min_ymd   := xx.ymd;
                end if;
            END LOOP;
        else
            FOR xx IN (
                SELECT t1.YMD
                     , NVL(t1.JONGA,0)                                                                      AS jonga
                     , LAG(NVL(t1.JONGA,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.JM_CD
                                                      ORDER BY t1.YMD)                                      AS bf_jonga
                     , CASE When LAG(NVL(t1.JONGA,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.JM_CD
                                                                ORDER BY t1.YMD)=0
                            THEN 0
                            ELSE NVL(t1.JONGA,0) / LAG(NVL(t1.JONGA,0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.JM_CD
                                                                                  ORDER BY t1.YMD) - 1 END  AS rt
                  FROM SYT0LP t1
                 WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
                   AND t1.YMD     BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd
                   AND t1.YMD     NOT IN ( SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                             FROM SZX0HL ta
                                            WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'US'
                                              AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd )
                   AND t1.JM_CD   = jm.jm_cd
                 ORDER BY t1.YMD DESC
            ) loop
                if  xx.rt IS NULL   then    CONTINUE;
                end if;

                if  xx.ymd = p_tymd then    w_jonga := xx.jonga;
                end if;
                if  xx.jonga > w_max_jonga  then
                    w_max_jonga := xx.jonga;
                    w_max_ymd   := xx.ymd;
                end if;
                if  xx.jonga < w_min_jonga  then
                    w_min_jonga := xx.jonga;
                    w_min_ymd   := xx.ymd;
                end if;
            END LOOP;
        end if;
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := HIGH_LOW_REC (jm.jm_cd,
                                                             F_NTRIM (w_max_jonga, 0, 2) || ' ( ' || F_N# ((w_jonga / w_max_jonga - 1) * 100, 0, 2) || '% ) ', w_max_ymd,
                                                             F_NTRIM (w_min_jonga, 0, 2) || ' ( ' || F_N# ((w_jonga / w_min_jonga - 1) * 100, 0, 2) || '% ) ', w_min_ymd);
    END LOOP;

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_holi_chk (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 휴일체크 return
--
    w_holi_chk                   VARCHAR (1);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'Y'
          INTO w_holi_chk
          FROM SZX0HL t1
         WHERE holi_ymd = p_ymd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        w_holi_chk  := 'N';
    END;

    RETURN  w_holi_chk;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_HWANMOD (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_currency                     IN    VARCHAR,
    p_danga                        IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 환율변동분 (변동분 * 평균환율)
--
    w_ret                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT gijun_rt - p_danga
          INTO w_ret
          FROM SYX1HY t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
           AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        BEGIN
            SELECT gijun_rt - p_danga
              INTO w_ret
              FROM SYX0HY t1
             WHERE t1.gijun_ymd = p_ymd
               AND t1.currency  = p_currency;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            w_ret   := NULL;
        END;
    END;

    RETURN  w_ret;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_IJAY (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_aekm                         IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 해외 이자
--
    w_ija                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_gugan_ija                  NUMBER;
    w_gugan_ilsu                 NUMBER;
    w_dcf_ilsu                   NUMBER;
BEGIN
    -- 구간 미수이자 계산은 이자수령일 까지 계산 '(bf_ija_ymd + 1) And af_ija_ymd' 20181218
    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT CASE WHEN ija_dcf IN ('2','3') THEN '1'
                                              ELSE ija_dcf END  AS ija_dcf
             , days_calc_gb
          FROM SYM0YA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
    ) loop
        if  xx.days_calc_gb = '1'   then
            SELECT NVL(gugan_ija,0) * p_aekm
                 , gugan_ilsu
                 , F_DCF_ILSU (xx.ija_dcf, bf_ija_ymd, p_ymd)  AS dcf_ilsu
              INTO w_gugan_ija
                 , w_gugan_ilsu
                 , w_dcf_ilsu
              FROM SYJ0IG t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
               AND p_ymd      Between  (bf_ija_ymd + 1) AND af_ija_ymd;
        else
            -- 양편넣기
            SELECT NVL(gugan_ija,0) * p_aekm
                 , gugan_ilsu
                 , F_DCF_ILSU (xx.ija_dcf, bf_ija_ymd - 1, p_ymd)  AS dcf_ilsu
              INTO w_gugan_ija
                 , w_gugan_ilsu
                 , w_dcf_ilsu
              FROM SYJ0IG t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
               AND p_ymd      Between bf_ija_ymd AND af_ija_ymd;
        end if;
        w_ija   := ROUND (w_gugan_ija / w_gugan_ilsu * w_dcf_ilsu,2);
    END LOOP;
    RETURN  w_ija;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    w_ija   := 0;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_IMP_VOLATILITY (
    p_S                            IN    NUMBER,  /* 기초 자산 가격 */
    p_K                            IN    NUMBER,  /* 옵션 행사가격 */
    p_T_days                       IN    NUMBER,  /* 잔존일수 (일 기준) */
    p_r                            IN    NUMBER,  /* 무위험이자율 */
    p_market_price                 IN    NUMBER,  /* 시장에서 거래된 옵션 가격 */
    p_option_type                  IN    CHAR := 'P' /* C=콜, P=풋 */
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
    w_sigma                      NUMBER := 0.9; -- 초기 추정 변동성
    w_price                      NUMBER;
    w_vega                       NUMBER;
    w_tolerance                  CONSTANT NUMBER    := 0.0001;
    w_cnt                        INTEGER    := 0;
    w_T_years                    NUMBER := p_T_days / 365;
BEGIN
    if  p_S <= 0 OR p_K <= 0 OR w_T_years <= 0 OR p_market_price <= 0   then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    WHILE w_cnt < 100 loop
        -- 현재 추정된 변동성으로 옵션 가격 계산
        w_price := KFP.F_OPTION_PRICE(p_S, p_K, w_T_years, p_r, w_sigma, p_option_type);
        --dbms_output.put_line (w_cnt || ', compute price = ' || f_n# (w_price));
        if  w_price IS NULL then
            RETURN NULL;
        end if;

        -- 수렴 여부 확인
        if  ABS (w_price - p_market_price) < w_tolerance    then
            RETURN ROUND(w_sigma,4);
        end if;

        -- Vega 계산 (콜/풋 공통)
        w_vega  := KFP.F_OPTION_VEGA(p_S, p_K, w_T_years, p_r, w_sigma);

        if NVL (w_vega,0) <= 1e-6   then
            -- Vega가 너무 작으면 수렴 불가능
            RETURN -999;
        end if;

        -- Newton-Raphson 갱신
        w_sigma := w_sigma - (w_price - p_market_price) / w_vega;

        -- 변동성이 음수로 가는 경우 방어
        if  w_sigma <= 0    then
            w_sigma := 0.0001;
        end if;

        w_cnt   := w_cnt + 1;
    END LOOP;

    -- 수렴하지 못한 경우
    RETURN NULL;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN NULL;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_INDEX_SUIK_RT (
    p_index                        IN    VARCHAR,  /* (index: kospi_jisu, kosdaq_jisu) */
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,     /* 기준일 */
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE      /* t일 */
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
-- index 수익율 --kospi, kosdaq
--
    w_pre_jisu                     NUMBER;
    w_cur_jisu                     NUMBER;

    w_suik_per                     NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT MAX(col_val)
          INTO w_pre_jisu
          FROM SKT0BM t1  /* 지수_유가증권 t1 t1 */
         WHERE ymd    = F_TRADE_YMD(p_fymd,'-')
           AND col_id = p_index;

        SELECT MAX(col_val)
          INTO w_cur_jisu
          FROM SKT0BM t1  /* 지수_유가증권 t1 t1 */
         WHERE ymd    = F_TRADE_YMD(p_tymd,'-')
           AND col_id = p_index;

    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_cur_jisu := 0;
        w_pre_jisu := 0;
    END;

    if  w_pre_jisu != 0 then
            w_suik_per  := (w_cur_jisu / w_pre_jisu - 1) * 100;
    else    w_suik_per  := NULL;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_suik_per;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JA010H (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_gubun                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 구분별 금액
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_gubun = '1'   then
        -- D+2일 예수금
        if  p_corp_gr = '2202' AND p_fund_cd = '99999'  then
            SELECT NVL(SUM(F_JAN (chadae_gb,junj,dr_aek,cr_aek) * CASE WHEN gwamok='20201' THEN -1
                                                                                           ELSE 1 END), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SKM0AG t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     = p_ymd
               AND work_gb = 'A'
               AND fund_cd IN ('1904','2348')
               AND gwamok  IN ('10101','12001','20201');
        else
            SELECT NVL(SUM(F_JAN (chadae_gb,junj,dr_aek,cr_aek) * CASE WHEN gwamok='20201' THEN -1
                                                                                           ELSE 1 END), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SKM0AG t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     = p_ymd
               AND work_gb = 'A'
               AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
               AND gwamok  IN ('10101','12001','20201');
        end if;

    elsif p_gubun = '2' then
        -- 설정일 또는 월 일임수수료
        SELECT GREATEST (re_seolj_ymd,trunc(p_ymd,'mm'))
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZM0IA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;

        SELECT NVL(SUM(dr_aek), 0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM SKM0AG t1
         WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ymd     Between w_ymd AND p_ymd
           AND work_gb = 'A'
           AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND gwamok  = '90120';

    elsif p_gubun = '2m'    then
        -- 월 일임수수료
        SELECT NVL(SUM(dr_aek), 0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM SKM0AG t1
         WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ymd     Between trunc (p_ymd,'mm') AND p_ymd
           AND work_gb = 'A'
           AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND gwamok  = '90120';

    elsif p_gubun = '3' then
        -- 평잔액
        if  p_corp_gr = '2202' AND p_fund_cd = '99999'  then
            SELECT CASE WHEN aa.CUSTOMER_GR='자산운용'
                        THEN SUM(CASE WHEN t1.gwamok='B0303'
                                      THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0) END)
                        ELSE NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN t1.gwamok='A0403'
                                          THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0)
                                     END) / SUM(CASE WHEN t1.gwamok='A0412'
                                                     THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0) END), 0)
                   END
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SKM0AG t1
                 , SZX0AA aa
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
               AND t1.fund_cd IN ('10101','12001','20201')
               AND t1.work_gb = 'A'
               AND t1.gwamok  IN ('A0403','A0412','B0303')
               AND aa.corp_gr = t1.corp_gr;
        else
            SELECT CASE WHEN aa.CUSTOMER_GR='자산운용'
                        THEN SUM(CASE WHEN t1.gwamok='B0303'
                                      THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0) END)
                        ELSE NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN t1.gwamok='A0403'
                                          THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0)
                                     END) / SUM(CASE WHEN t1.gwamok='A0412'
                                                     THEN NVL(t1.junj,0) + NVL(t1.dr_aek,0) - NVL(t1.cr_aek,0) END), 0)
                   END
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SKM0AG t1
                 , SZX0AA aa
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
               AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
               AND t1.work_gb = 'A'
               AND t1.gwamok  IN ('A0403','A0412','B0303')
               AND aa.corp_gr = t1.corp_gr;
        end if;

    elsif p_gubun = '4' then
        -- 주식취득액계
        if  p_corp_gr = '2202' AND p_fund_cd = '99999'  then
            SELECT NVL(SUM(NVL(t1.bfil_chui_aek,0) + NVL(t1.up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(t1.dw_chui_aek,0)
                               + NVL(t1.gongs_up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(t1.gongs_dw_chui_aek,0) + NVL(t1.sonik,0) ), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SJM0JM t1
             WHERE corp_gr     = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd         = p_ymd
               AND fund_cd     IN ('1904','2348')
               AND bs_type     = 0
               AND type_trench = '00:';
        else
            SELECT NVL(SUM(NVL(t1.bfil_chui_aek,0) + NVL(t1.up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(t1.dw_chui_aek,0)
                               + NVL(t1.gongs_up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(t1.gongs_dw_chui_aek,0) + NVL(t1.sonik,0) ), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SJM0JM t1
             WHERE corp_gr     = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd         = p_ymd
               AND fund_cd     = p_fund_cd
               AND bs_type     = 0
               AND type_trench = '00:';
        end if;

    elsif p_gubun = '5' then
        -- 시가평가액계
        SELECT NVL(SUM(siga_aek), 0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM UZM0UI t1
         WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ymd     = p_ymd
           AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd;

    elsif p_gubun = '6' then
        -- 종목별 취득액
        SELECT NVL(t1.bfil_chui_aek,0) + NVL(t1.up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(t1.dw_chui_aek,0)
                                       + NVL(t1.gongs_up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(t1.gongs_dw_chui_aek,0)
                                       + NVL(t1.sonik,0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM SJM0JM t1
         WHERE corp_gr     = p_corp_gr
           AND ymd         = p_ymd
           AND fund_cd     = p_fund_cd
           AND bs_type     = 0
           AND type_trench = '00:'
           AND jm_cd       = p_jm_cd;

    elsif p_gubun = '7' then
        -- 미수배당액
        if  p_corp_gr = '2202' AND p_fund_cd = '99999'  then
            SELECT NVL(SUM(misu_baed_aek), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SJM0JM t1
             WHERE corp_gr     = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd         = p_ymd
               AND fund_cd     IN ('1904','2348')
               AND bs_type     = 0
               AND type_trench = '00:';
        else
            SELECT NVL(SUM(misu_baed_aek), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SJM0JM t1
             WHERE corp_gr     = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd         = p_ymd
               AND fund_cd     = p_fund_cd
               AND bs_type     = 0
               AND type_trench = '00:';
        end if;

    elsif p_gubun = '8' then
        -- 투적투자일임수수료
        SELECT NVL(SUM(junj + NVL(dr_aek,0)), 0)
          INTO w_aek
          FROM SKM0AG t1
         WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ymd     = p_ymd
           AND work_gb = 'A'
           AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND gwamok  = '90120';

    elsif p_gubun = 'jan'   then
        -- 원본액
        if  p_corp_gr = '2202' AND p_fund_cd = '99999'  then
            SELECT NVL(SUM(wonbon_aek), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SZT0IO t1
             WHERE corp_gr           = p_corp_gr
               AND (fund_cd,tr_ymd)  IN (SELECT ia.fund_cd
                                              , MAX (ta.tr_ymd)
                                           FROM SZM0IA ia
                                              , SZT0IO ta
                                          WHERE ia.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                            AND ia.fund_cd IN ('1904','2348')
                                            AND ta.corp_gr = ia.corp_gr
                                            AND ta.tr_ymd  <= NVL(ia.haeji_ymd,p_ymd)
                                            AND ta.fund_cd = ia.fund_cd
                                          GROUP BY ia.fund_cd);
        else
            SELECT NVL(wonbon_aek,0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SZT0IO t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
               AND tr_ymd  = (SELECT MAX (tr_ymd)
                                FROM SZM0IA ia
                                   , SZT0IO ta
                               WHERE ia.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                 AND ia.fund_cd = P_fund_cd
                                 AND ta.corp_gr = ia.corp_gr
                                 AND ta.tr_ymd  <= NVL(ia.haeji_ymd,p_ymd)
                                 AND ta.fund_cd = ia.fund_cd);
        end if;
    else
        -- 계정별 잔액
        if  p_corp_gr = '2202' AND p_fund_cd = '99999'  then
            SELECT NVL(SUM(F_JAN (chadae_gb,junj,dr_aek,cr_aek)), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SKM0AG t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     = p_ymd
               AND work_gb = 'A'
               AND fund_cd IN ('1904','2348')
               AND gwamok  = p_gubun;
        else
            SELECT NVL(SUM(F_JAN (chadae_gb,junj,dr_aek,cr_aek)), 0)
              INTO w_aek
              FROM SKM0AG t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     = p_ymd
               AND work_gb = 'A'
               AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
               AND gwamok  = p_gubun;
        end if;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JA032N (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN JA032N_TAB AS
--
-- S&P500, NASDAQ100 수익율 및 펀드수익율
--
    w_tab                          JA032N_TAB;

    w_ymd                          DATE;

    w_spx_start                    NUMBER;
    w_spx_today                    NUMBER;
    w_ndx_start                    NUMBER;
    w_ndx_today                    NUMBER;
    w_fund_start                   NUMBER;
    w_fund_today                   NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := JA032N_TAB( );
    w_tab.extend;

    SELECT MIN(TR_YMD)
      INTO w_ymd
      FROM SZT0IA_V t1
     WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
       AND t1.FUND_CD = p_fund_cd;

    BEGIN
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_spx_start
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = w_ymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'S&P500';
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_spx_start
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = F_OPEN_US(w_ymd,'-')
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'S&P500';
    END;

    BEGIN
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_spx_today
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = p_ymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'S&P500';
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_spx_today
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = F_OPEN_US(p_ymd,'-')
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'S&P500';
    END;

    BEGIN
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_ndx_start
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = w_ymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'NASDAQ100';
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_spx_start
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = F_OPEN_US(w_ymd,'-')
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'NASDAQ100';
    END;

    BEGIN
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_ndx_today
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = p_ymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'NASDAQ100';
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        SELECT JONGA
          INTO w_spx_today
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = F_OPEN_US(p_ymd,'-')
           AND t1.JM_CD   = 'NASDAQ100';
    END;

    BEGIN
        SELECT GIJUN_GA
          INTO w_fund_start
          FROM SKT0GI t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.FUND_CD = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.YMD     = w_ymd
           AND t1.WORK_GB = F_WORK_GB(p_corp_gr, p_fund_cd, w_ymd);

        SELECT GIJUN_GA
          INTO w_fund_today
          FROM SKT0GI t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.FUND_CD = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.YMD     = p_ymd
           AND t1.WORK_GB = 'A';

        w_tab (1)   := JA032N_REC (w_ymd, w_spx_start, w_spx_today, w_ndx_start, w_ndx_today, w_fund_start, w_fund_today);
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_tab (1)   := JA032N_REC (NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    END;

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_jan (
    p_chadae_gb                 IN        varchar,
    p_junj                      IN        number,
    p_dr_aek                    IN        number,
    p_cr_aek                    IN        number
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 계정구분에 따른 잔액을 구함
--
    w_jan                       number;
BEGIN
    if  p_chadae_gb = 'D'       then    w_jan := nvl(p_junj,0)+nvl(p_dr_aek,0)-nvl(p_cr_aek,0); -- 차변계정
    elsif  p_chadae_gb = 'C'    then    w_jan := nvl(p_junj,0)-nvl(p_dr_aek,0)+nvl(p_cr_aek,0); -- 대변계정
    else                                w_jan := 0;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_jan;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JASAN_NM (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_gr                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_currency                     IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 자산분류명
--
    w_jasan_nm                   VARCHAR (80);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT sebu_cd_nm || CASE WHEN p_currency IS NOT NULL THEN '(' || p_currency || ')' END
          INTO w_jasan_nm
          FROM SZX0GR t1
         WHERE gr_cd   = '96'
           AND sebu_cd = p_jm_gr;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        w_jasan_nm  := '';
    END;

    RETURN  w_jasan_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_jeokyo_nm (
    p_jeokyo_cd                    IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 적요명
--
    w_nm                         szx0gy.jeokyo_nm%type;
BEGIN
    SELECT NVL(MIN(jeokyo_nm), p_jeokyo_cd)
      INTO w_nm
      FROM SZX0GY t1
     WHERE jeokyo_cd = p_jeokyo_cd;

    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JJ_NM (
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 주식종목명
--
    w_jj_nm                      VARCHAR (160);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        if  LENGTH (p_jm_cd) = 12   then
            SELECT jj_nm
              INTO w_jj_nm
              FROM SJM0JJ t1
             WHERE t1.jm_cd = p_jm_cd;
        else
            SELECT jj_nm
              INTO w_jj_nm
              FROM SJM0JJ t1
             WHERE t1.koscom_cd = p_jm_cd
               AND ROWNUM = 1;
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_jj_nm := p_jm_cd;
    END;

    RETURN  w_jj_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_jk_nm (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_jk_gu                     IN        varchar,
    p_jk_cd                     IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 컴플관련 적용 코드명
--
    w_nm                        varchar(60);
BEGIN
    if  p_jk_gu in ('CH01','DN00')  then
        w_nm    := f_jm_nm (p_corp_gr, p_jk_cd);
    end if;

    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JM_CHECK (
    SJM_CD                         IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 종목코드 CHECK DIGIT 산출 방법
--
    sBuf                         VARCHAR (30);
    sJm                          VARCHAR (30);
    c                            VARCHAR (1);
    i                            integer;
    iLen                         integer;
    iJm                          integer;
    iFlag                        integer;
    len                          integer;
BEGIN
    -- EX) KR612345101
    len := LENGTH (sJm_Cd);
    sJm := '';
    iJm := 0;
    iFlag   := 1;
    FOR j IN 1..len LOOP
        i   := len - j + 1;
        c   := SUBSTR(sJm_Cd,i,1);
        if  ascii (c) between 48 AND 57         then    sJm := c || sJm;
        elsif ascii (c) between 65 AND 90       then    sJm := (ascii (c) - 55) || sJm;
        else
            RETURN  ' ';
        end if;
    END LOOP;

    -- EX) 2027612345101
    len     := LENGTH (sJm);
    sBuf    := '';
    FOR j IN 1..len LOOP
        i   := len - j + 1;
        c   := SUBSTRB (sJm,i,1);
        if  iFlag = 2   then
                iFlag   := 1;
        else    iFlag := 2;
        end if;
        BEGIN
            sBuf    := TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER (c) * iFlag) || sBuf;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            sBuf    := '0' || sBuf;
        END;
    END LOOP;

    -- EX) 40471214385202
    iJm := 0;
    len := LENGTH (sBuf);
    FOR j IN 1..len LOOP
        i   := len - j + 1;
        c   := SUBSTRB (sBuf,i,1);
        BEGIN
            iJm := iJm + TO_NUMBER (c);
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            iJm := iJm + 0;
        END;
    END LOOP;
    iJm := Mod (10 - Mod(iJm, 10), 10);

    RETURN  sJm_cd || iJm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JM_MANGI (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN DATE IS
    w_dt_sanghw                  DATE;
BEGIN
    if  p_jm_cd LIKE 'KR7%' OR p_jm_cd LIKE 'KR4%'  then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT sanghw_ymd
          INTO w_dt_sanghw
          FROM SCM0CJ t1
         WHERE jm_cd  = p_jm_cd
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        BEGIN
            SELECT sanghw_ymd
              INTO w_dt_sanghw
              FROM SHM0HJ t1
             WHERE jm_cd  = p_jm_cd
               AND ROWNUM = 1;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_dt_sanghw := '';
        END;
    END;

    RETURN(w_dt_sanghw);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JM_NM (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 모든종목 종목명 Found
--
    w_nm                         VARCHAR (200);
BEGIN
    if  LENGTH (p_jm_cd) = 12 OR p_jm_cd LIKE 'KR%' OR p_jm_cd LIKE 'TR%'       then
        BEGIN
            SELECT NVL(jj.jj_nm || cj.cj_nm || sc.fund_nm || hj.hj_nm || kj.sj_nm || ya.jm_nm || dj.jm_nm || tj.jm_nm || gj.jm_nm, p_jm_cd)
              INTO w_nm
              FROM DUAL t0
                 , SJM0JJ jj
                 , SCM0CJ cj
                 , SJM0SC sc
                 , SHM0HJ hj
                 , SSX0KJ kj
                 , SYM0YA ya
                 , SDX0DJ dj
                 , OTS0JM tj
                 , SGM0JM gj
             WHERE jj.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', jj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND cj.corp_gr(+)          = F_LINK_CORP_GR(p_corp_gr)
               AND cj.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', cj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND sc.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND sc.tasa_jm_cd(+)       = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', sc.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND hj.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND hj.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', hj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND kj.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND kj.sj_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', kj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND ya.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND ya.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', ya.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND dj.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND dj.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', dj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND tj.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND tj.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', tj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND gj.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND gj.jm_cd(+)            = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', gj.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy);
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            w_nm    := p_jm_cd;
        END;
    else
        BEGIN
            SELECT jm_nm
              INTO w_nm
              FROM SYM0YA t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            SELECT NVL(t1.tr_co_nm || ia.fund_nm, p_jm_cd)
              INTO w_nm
              FROM DUAL t0
                 , SZX2MM t1
                 , SZM0IA ia
             WHERE t1.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.tr_co_cd(+)         = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', t1.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy)
               AND ia.corp_gr(+)          = p_corp_gr
               AND ia.fund_cd(+)          = p_jm_cd
               AND NVL('x', ia.rowid(+))  = NVL('x', t0.dummy);
        END;
    end if;

    if  LENGTH (p_jm_cd) = 5    then
        BEGIN
            SELECT gwamok_nm
              INTO w_nm
              FROM SZX0GM t1
             WHERE gwamok = p_jm_cd;

            RETURN  w_nm;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

GRANT EXECUTE ON KFP.F_JM_NM TO AFMS;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JM_SORT (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 주식 보유종목 SORT 순서조정
--
    w_sort                       char(1);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT '0'
          INTO w_sort
          FROM SJM0JM t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = p_ymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_sort  := '1';
    END;
    RETURN w_sort;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JONGA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_koscom_cd                    IN    VARCHAR,  /* 표준코드(12자리)인 경우 단축코드 조회 후 조회 */
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE DEFAULT NULL
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 주식/해외주식/채권/해외채권 종가
--
    w_koscom_cd                  VARCHAR(6);
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_jonga                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd IS NULL   then
        SELECT F_TRADE_YMD (junyong_ymd,'-')
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    else
        w_ymd   := F_TRADE_YMD (p_ymd, '-');
    end if;

    if  LENGTH (p_koscom_cd) <> 6   then
        BEGIN
            SELECT KOSCOM_CD
              INTO w_koscom_cd
              FROM SJM0JJ t1
             WHERE t1.DANC_GB IN ('A','C','D','G')
               AND JM_CD      = p_koscom_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            RETURN NULL;
        END;
    else
        w_koscom_cd := p_koscom_cd;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT close
          INTO w_jonga
          FROM SJT0TG t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd       = w_ymd
           AND t1.koscom_cd = w_koscom_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        BEGIN
            SELECT close
              INTO w_jonga
              FROM SJT1TG t1
             WHERE t1.ymd       = w_ymd
               AND t1.koscom_cd = w_koscom_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_jonga := NULL;
        END;
    END;
    RETURN  w_jonga;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_junil_daebi (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_yong_ymd                     IN    DATE,  /* 영업일 */
    p_gijun_ga                     IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 펀드 영업일 기준 전일대비액 return
--
    w_yong_bymd                  DATE;
    w_daebi                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_yong_bymd := F_OPEN_YMD (p_yong_ymd, '-1');

    BEGIN
        SELECT p_gijun_ga - gijun_ga
          INTO w_daebi
          FROM SKT0GI t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = w_yong_bymd - 1
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_daebi := p_gijun_ga - 1000;
    END;

    RETURN  w_daebi;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_JUSU (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 주식종목마스타의 운용가능주수
--
    w_jusu                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    -- 운용가능주수 = 전일보유주수+당일증가주수-당일감소주수+공시증가주수-공시감소주수-
    --                전일질권주수-당일질권증가+당일질권감소-공개매수할당
    BEGIN
        SELECT NVL(bfil_boyu_jusu,0) + NVL(up_jusu,0) - NVL(dw_jusu,0) + NVL(gongs_up_jusu,0)
                                     - NVL(gongs_dw_jusu,0)
                                     - NVL(bfil_jilg_jusu,0) - NVL(jilg_up_jusu,0) + NVL(jilg_dw_jusu,0)
                                     - NVL(mesu_hald_jusu,0) - NVL(lock_jusu,0)
          INTO w_jusu
          FROM SJM0JM t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
           AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        w_jusu  := -1;
    END;
    RETURN  w_jusu;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_KOSCOM_NM (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 주식 단축코드 종목명
--
    w_jm_nm                      VARCHAR(50);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT jj_nm
          INTO w_jm_nm
          FROM SJM0JJ t1
         WHERE koscom_cd = p_jm_cd
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_jm_nm := p_jm_cd;
    END;
    RETURN  w_jm_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_KSDCODE (
    p_gr_cd                     IN        varchar,
    p_sebu_cd                   IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- KSD 예탁결제용 그룹코드명
--
    w_leng                      number;
    w_sebu_cd                   ksdcode.sebu_cd%type;
    w_nm                        ksdcode.sebu_cd_nm%type;
BEGIN
    if  p_sebu_cd is null   then    RETURN  null;
    end if;

    if  p_sebu_cd = 'max'   then
        SELECT NVL(max (lengthb (sebu_cd_nm)),0)
          INTO w_leng
        FROM   ksdcode t1
        WHERE  gr_cd = p_gr_cd;
        if  w_leng = 0  then
                w_nm    := null;
        elsif w_leng > 40   then
                w_nm    := '40';
        else    w_nm    := to_char (w_leng);
        end if;
    else
--        if  p_gr_cd = 'CORP_GR' then
--            if  length (p_sebu_cd) != 12    then
--                RETURN f_ksd2corp (p_sebu_cd,1,length(p_sebu_cd));
--            else
--                -- 운용회사가 없어 예탁펀드종목코드로 회사코드를 만드는경우
--                RETURN F_JM_NM ('1700', p_sebu_cd);
--            end if;
--        elsif p_gr_cd = 'jnm'   then
--            RETURN F_JM_NM ('1700', p_sebu_cd);
--        end if;

        if  p_gr_cd = 'K01' And length(p_sebu_cd) = 10  then
                w_sebu_cd := substrb(p_sebu_cd,2,5);
        else    w_sebu_cd := p_sebu_cd;
        end if;

        begin
            SELECT sebu_cd_nm
              INTO w_nm
            FROM   ksdcode t1
            WHERE  gr_cd   = p_gr_cd
              AND  sebu_cd = w_sebu_cd;
        exception when  OTHERS  then
            if  w_sebu_cd = '0' then
                    w_nm    := '해당없슴';
            else    w_nm    := '미등록';
            end if;
        end;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_LINK_CORP_GR (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
BEGIN
    -- 자문과 일임만 사용
    RETURN  p_corp_gr;
--    if  p_corp_gr is null   then
--        RETURN null;
--    end if;
--
--    begin
--        SELECT  NVL(link_corp_gr,corp_gr)
--          INTO  w_link_corp_gr
--        FROM    szx0aa t1
--        WHERE   t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
--    end;
--
--    RETURN  w_link_corp_gr;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_MAIL_NM (
    p_mail_gb                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_mail_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
    w_nm                         VARCHAR (80);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        if  SUBSTR (p_mail_gb,1,1) = 'F'    then
            SELECT fund_nm
              INTO w_nm
              FROM SZM0IA t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = p_mail_cd;

        elsif SUBSTR (p_mail_gb,1,1) = 'G'  then
            SELECT sell_nm
              INTO w_nm
              FROM SZX0PA t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.sell_co = p_mail_cd;
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_nm    := p_mail_cd;
    END;
    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_MEIB_YMD (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 발행일자
--
    w_balh_ymd                   DATE;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT lst_meib_ymd
          INTO w_balh_ymd
          FROM SCM0CM t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd      = p_ymd
           AND t1.fund_cd  = p_fund_cd
           AND t1.jm_cd    = p_jm_cd
           AND t1.buy_date = '%';
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        BEGIN
            SELECT lst_meib_ymd
              INTO w_balh_ymd
              FROM SHM0HM t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
               AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
               AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            w_balh_ymd  := NULL;
        END;
    END;

    RETURN  w_balh_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_MIJIGUB_AEK (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 미수입(지급)액
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT SUM(t1.siga_aek) 
      INTO w_aek
      FROM UZM0UI t1
     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND t1.ymd     = p_ymd
       AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
       AND t1.jm_gr   = '2'
       AND t1.jm_cd   IN ('12001','20201');

    RETURN w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_MISUBAED_AEK (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 주식+현금배당금
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT NVL(SUM(misu_baed_aek),0)
      INTO w_aek
      FROM SJM0JM t1
     WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND ymd     = p_ymd
       AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd;

    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_MISUBAED_JAEK (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 주식배당금평가액
--
    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT NVL(SUM(NVL(bfil_chui_aek,0) + NVL(up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(dw_chui_aek,0)
                   + NVL(sonik,0)
                   + NVL(gongs_up_chui_aek,0) - NVL(gongs_dw_chui_aek,0)),0)
      INTO w_aek
      FROM SJM0JM t1
     WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND ymd     = p_ymd
       AND fund_cd = p_fund_cd
       AND jm_cd   LIKE 'KRY%';

    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_n# (
