    p_num                       IN        number,
    p_leng                      IN        number default 0,
    p_scale                     IN        number default null
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 숫자변환
--
    w_num                       number;
    w_leng                      number;
    w_scale                     number;

    w_data                      varchar(100);
    w_fmt                       varchar(100);
begin
    if  p_num is null   then    return '';
    end if;

    w_num   := f_num (p_num);

    if  p_leng = 0  then
            w_leng := 17 + nvl(p_scale,0);
    else    w_leng := p_leng;
    end if;
    if  p_scale is null then
        if  w_num - trunc (w_num) != 0  then
                w_scale := length(w_num - trunc (w_num)) - 1;
        else    w_scale := 0;
        end if;
        w_leng  := w_leng + w_scale;
    else
        w_scale := p_scale;
        w_leng  := w_leng - 1;
    end if;

    if  w_num < 0   then
            w_fmt := LPAD ('0',w_leng - w_scale - 1,'9'); -- sign 길이제외
    else    w_fmt := LPAD ('0',w_leng - w_scale,'9');
    end if;
    if  w_scale > 0 then    w_fmt := w_fmt || RPAD ('.',w_scale + 1,'0');
    else
        if  w_num - trunc (w_num) != 0  then    w_fmt := w_fmt || RPAD ('.',length(w_num - trunc (w_num)),'0');
        end if;
    end if;

    w_data  := to_char (w_num, w_fmt);
    if  p_leng = 0  then    w_data := TRIM (w_data);
    else
        if  w_num >= 0  then    w_data := substr (w_data,2);
        end if;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_data;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_N0 (
    p_num                       IN        number,
    p_leng                      IN        number default 0,
    p_scale                     IN        number default null
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 숫자변환(소숫점포함 모드)
--
    w_num                       number;
    w_leng                      number;
    w_scale                     number;

    w_data                      varchar(100);
    w_fmt                       varchar(50);
begin
    if  p_num is null   then    return null;
    end if;

    if  p_scale is null then
        w_num   := trunc (F_NUM (p_num));
        if  w_num - trunc (w_num) != 0  then
                w_scale := length(w_num - trunc (w_num)) - 1;
        else    w_scale := 0;
        end if;
        w_leng  := w_leng + w_scale;
    else
        w_num   := trunc (F_NUM (p_num),p_scale);
        w_scale := p_scale;
        w_leng  := w_leng - 1;
    end if;

    if  p_leng = 0  then
        w_fmt   := '9999999999999999990';
        if  w_scale > 0 then    w_fmt := w_fmt || RPAD ('.',w_scale + 1,'0');
        else
            if  w_num - trunc (w_num) != 0  then    w_fmt := w_fmt || RPAD ('.',length(w_num - trunc (w_num)),'0');
            end if;
        end if;
        w_data  := trim (to_char (w_num,w_fmt));
    else
        w_leng  := p_leng;
        if  w_num < 0   then
            if  w_scale > 0 then
                    w_fmt := RPAD ('0', w_leng - w_scale - 2, '0');     -- 소숫점 제외
            else    w_fmt := RPAD ('0',w_leng - w_scale - 1,'0');
            end if;
        else
            if  w_scale > 0 then
                    w_fmt := RPAD ('0', w_leng - w_scale - 1, '0');     -- 소숫점 제외
            else    w_fmt := RPAD ('0',w_leng - w_scale,'0');
            end if;
        end if;

        if  w_scale > 0 then    w_fmt := w_fmt || RPAD ('.',w_scale + 1,'0');
        end if;

        w_data  := to_char (w_num,w_fmt);
        if  w_num >= 0  then    w_data := substr (w_data,2);
        end if;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_data;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_ntrim (
    p_num                       IN        number,
    p_leng                      IN        number default 0,
    p_scale                     IN        number default 0
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 숫자변환
--
    w_data                      varchar(100);
    w_scale                     varchar(50);
begin
    if  p_scale = 0 then
            w_scale := '0';
    else    w_scale := RPAD ('0.', p_scale + 2, '0');
    end if;
    w_data  := trim (to_char (p_num, '999,999,999,999,999,99' || w_scale));
    if  p_leng > length (w_data)   then
        w_data  := LPAD (w_data, p_leng);
    end if;
    RETURN  rtrim (NVL(w_data,''));
end;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_num (
    p_num                       IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- to_number
--
    w_num                       number;
begin
    begin
        w_num   := to_number (p_num);
    exception when  OTHERS  then
        w_num   := null;
    end;
    RETURN  w_num;
end;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_nzero (
    p_num                       IN        number,
    p_leng                      IN        number default 0,
    p_scale                     IN        number default 0
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 숫자변환(소숫점 V 모드)
--
    w_num                       number;
    w_leng                      number;
    w_data                      varchar(100);
    w_scale                     varchar(50);
begin
    if  p_num is null   then    return null;
    end if;
    w_num   := trunc (f_num (p_num), p_scale);
    if  p_leng = 0  then
        w_scale := '9999999999999999990';
        if  p_scale > 0 then    w_scale := w_scale || RPAD ('.',p_scale + 1,'0');
        end if;
        w_data  := replace (trim (to_char (w_num, w_scale)),'.');
    else
        w_leng  := p_leng;
        if  w_num < 0   then
                w_scale := RPAD ('0',w_leng - p_scale - 1,'0'); -- sign 길이제외
        else    w_scale := RPAD ('0',w_leng - p_scale,'0');
        end if;
        if  p_scale > 0 then    w_scale := w_scale || RPAD ('.',p_scale + 1,'0');
        end if;
        w_data  := replace (to_char (w_num, w_scale),'.');
        if  w_num >= 0  then    w_data := substr (w_data,2);
        end if;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_data;
end;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OBJ_NM (
    p_bun                          IN    VARCHAR,
    p_pgm_id                       IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- Object 명
--
    w_nm                         fw_pgm_mst.pgm_nm%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT pgm_nm
          INTO w_nm
          FROM FW_PGM_MST t1
         WHERE pgm_id = p_pgm_id;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_nm    := p_pgm_id;
    END;

    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_obj_no (
    p_bun                          IN    VARCHAR,
    p_pgm_id                       IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 화면번호
--
    w_no                         fw_pgm_mst.pgm_go%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT pgm_go
          INTO w_no
          FROM FW_PGM_MST t1
         WHERE pgm_id = p_pgm_id;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
        w_no    := '';
    END;

    RETURN  w_no;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OPEN_US (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nation                       IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'US'
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 입력일 기준 이전(후) 경과 영업일 return
--
    w_next                       NUMBER;
    w_add                        NUMBER DEFAULT 0;
    w_ymd                        DATE;
BEGIN
    if  SUBSTR (p_gb,1,1) IN ('+','M')  then
            w_next  := 1;
    else    w_next  := -1;
    end if;

    w_ymd   := TRUNC(p_ymd,'dd');
    if  p_gb IN ('+','-','M','0','+0','-0','M0')    then
        LOOP
            BEGIN
                SELECT holi_ymd
                  INTO w_ymd
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE NATION_CD = p_nation
                   AND HOLI_YMD  = w_ymd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                EXIT;
            END;
            w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
        END LOOP;
    else
        LOOP
            w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
            BEGIN
                SELECT holi_ymd
                  INTO w_ymd
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE NATION_CD = p_nation
                   AND HOLI_YMD  = w_ymd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                w_add   := w_add + 1;
                if  w_add = SUBSTR (p_gb,2) then    exit;
                end if;
            END;
        END LOOP;
    end if;

    -- 익영업일이 월이 변경된 경우 전영업일(해외채권 이자일 계산시 사용 ijs_BDC 컬럼)
    if  SUBSTR (p_gb,1,1) = 'M' AND TO_CHAR (p_ymd,'yyyymm') != TO_CHAR (w_ymd,'yyyymm')    then
        w_ymd   := TRUNC(p_ymd,'dd');
        w_next  := -1;
        LOOP
            BEGIN
                SELECT holi_ymd
                  INTO w_ymd
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE NATION_CD = p_nation
                   AND HOLI_YMD  = w_ymd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                EXIT;
            END;
            w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
        END LOOP;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OPEN_YMD (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 입력일 기준 이전(후) 경과 영업일 return
--
    w_next                       NUMBER;
    w_add                        NUMBER DEFAULT 0;
    w_ymd                        DATE;
BEGIN
    if  SUBSTR (p_gb,1,1) IN ('+','M')  then
            w_next  := 1;
    else    w_next  := -1;
    end if;

    w_ymd   := trunc(p_ymd,'dd');
    if  p_gb IN ('+','-','M','0','+0','-0','M0')    then
        LOOP
            BEGIN
                SELECT holi_ymd
                  INTO w_ymd
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE nation_cd = 'KR'
                   AND holi_ymd  = w_ymd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                EXIT;
            END;
            w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
        END LOOP;
    else
        LOOP
            w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
            BEGIN
                SELECT holi_ymd
                  INTO w_ymd
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE nation_cd = 'KR'
                   AND holi_ymd  = w_ymd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                w_add   := w_add + 1;
                if  w_add = SUBSTR (p_gb,2) then    exit;
                end if;
            END;
        END LOOP;
    end if;

    -- 익영업일이 월이 변경된 경우 전영업일(해외채권 이자일 계산시 사용 ijs_BDC 컬럼)
    if  SUBSTR (p_gb,1,1) = 'M' AND TO_CHAR (p_ymd,'yyyymm') != TO_CHAR (w_ymd,'yyyymm')    then
        w_ymd   := trunc(p_ymd,'dd');
        w_next  := -1;
        LOOP
            BEGIN
                SELECT holi_ymd
                  INTO w_ymd
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE nation_cd = 'KR'
                   AND holi_ymd  = w_ymd;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                EXIT;
            END;
            w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
        END LOOP;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OPTION_PRICE (
    p_S                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_K                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_T                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_r                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_sigma                        IN    NUMBER,
    p_option_type                  IN    CHAR  /* 'C' or 'P' */
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
    d1                           NUMBER;
    d2                           NUMBER;
    call_price                   NUMBER;
    put_price                    NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_S <= 0 OR p_K <= 0 OR p_T <= 0 OR p_sigma <= 0    then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    d1  := (LN(p_S / p_K) + (p_r + 0.5 * p_sigma * p_sigma) * p_T) / (p_sigma * SQRT(p_T));
    d2  := d1 - p_sigma * SQRT(p_T);

    call_price  := p_S * F_STD_CDF(d1) - p_K * EXP( - p_r * p_T) * F_STD_CDF(d2);
    put_price   := p_K * EXP( - p_r * p_T) * F_STD_CDF( - d2) - p_S * F_STD_CDF( - d1);

    if  UPPER (p_option_type) = 'C' then
        RETURN call_price;
    elsif UPPER (p_option_type) = 'P'   then
        RETURN put_price;
    else
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OPTION_VEGA (
    p_S                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_K                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_T                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_r                            IN    NUMBER,
    p_sigma                        IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
    d1                           NUMBER;
    vega                         NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_T <= 0 OR p_sigma <= 0    then
        RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    d1  := (LN(p_S / p_K) + (p_r + 0.5 * p_sigma * p_sigma) * p_T) / (p_sigma * SQRT(p_T));
    vega    := p_S * EXP(-0.5 * d1 * d1) / SQRT(2 * 3.14159265359) * SQRT(p_T);
    RETURN vega;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OPT_COLOR (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jonga                        IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 옵션 행사가 종가대비 색상
--
    w_hangsa_ga                  NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT NVL(MAX(jm.HANGSA_GA),0)
      INTO w_hangsa_ga
      FROM SYM0YA jm
         , SYM0YZ t1
     WHERE jm.CORP_GR      = p_corp_gr
       AND jm.CHANGE_JM_CD = p_jm_cd
       AND t1.CORP_GR      = jm.CORP_GR
       AND t1.YMD          = p_ymd
       AND t1.FUND_CD      = p_fund_cd
       AND t1.JM_CD        = jm.JM_CD;

    if  w_hangsa_ga = 0 then
        RETURN 'c';
    elsif w_hangsa_ga > p_jonga then
        RETURN  'a';
    else
        RETURN  'b';
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_OPT_DATE_COLOR (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER DETERMINISTIC IS
-- 일자구간 컬러값
--
    w_days                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_fymd IS NULL  then    RETURN 255;
    end if;
    if  p_tymd IS NULL  then    RETURN 255;
    end if;

    w_days  := ABS(F_DAYS (p_tymd, p_fymd));

    if  w_days >= 10    then    RETURN 16719390;
    elsif w_days >= 5   then    RETURN 8388736;
    end if;

    RETURN 255;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_PF_PRT (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_pf_tp                        IN    VARCHAR,  /* Return구분 1:현가(단가), 2:종가 */
    p_aekm                         IN    NUMBER,   /* 액면 */
    p_mk_suik_rt                   IN    NUMBER,   /* 시장수익률 */
    p_pyom_iyul                    IN    NUMBER,   /* 표면이자율 */
    p_balh_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 발행일 */
    p_sanghw_ymd                   IN    DATE,     /* 만기일 */
    p_medo_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 매출일 */
    p_geochi_ija_mons              IN    NUMBER,   /* 거치기간 이자지급 월수 */
    p_sunme_gb                     IN    VARCHAR,  /* 선매출이자 계산여부 */
    p_trns_ymd                     IN    DATE,     /* 평가일 */
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR   /* 종목코드 */
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 원금 분할상환채권 현재단가 또는 종가를 계산
--
    r_danga                      NUMBER := 0; -- RETURN VALUE
    w_trns_ymd                   DATE; -- 만기일 반영한 평가일
    w_fst_ymd                    DATE; -- 최초원금상환일
    w_tot_ija_hoicha             NUMBER; -- 원금상환회수
    w_sanghw_ymd                 DATE; -- 상환일
    w_pay_freq_no                NUMBER; -- 이자지급회수
    w_unit_sanghw_wongm          NUMBER; -- 1회 상환원금
    w_fst_jan_wongm              NUMBER; -- 최초 잔여 미상환원금
    w_jan_wongm                  NUMBER; -- 잔여 미상환원금
    w_sanghw_won_rp_gm           NUMBER; -- 상환 원리금
    w_sunme_days                 NUMBER := 0; -- 선매출일수
    w_sunme_ija                  NUMBER; -- 선매출이자
    w_jan_sang_hoisu             NUMBER;
    w_ii                         NUMBER;
    w_jan_yy                     NUMBER;
    w_jan_dd                     NUMBER;
    w_geochi_ija_gb              scm0bj.geochi_ija_gb%type;
    w_geochi_gigan               scm0bj.geochi_gigan%type;
    w_sanghw_gb                  scm0bj.sanghw_gb%type;
    w_sanghw_biyul               scm0bj.sanghw_biyul%type;
BEGIN
    if  p_sanghw_ymd < p_trns_ymd   then
            w_trns_ymd  := p_sanghw_ymd; -- 평가일이 만기일 초과시 만기일로 변경
    else    w_trns_ymd := p_trns_ymd;
    end if;
    BEGIN
        SELECT geochi_ija_gb
             , geochi_gigan
             , sanghw_gb
             , sanghw_biyul
          INTO w_geochi_ija_gb
             , w_geochi_gigan
             , w_sanghw_gb
             , w_sanghw_biyul
          FROM SCM0BJ t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
        w_geochi_ija_gb := 0;
        w_geochi_gigan  := 0;
        w_sanghw_gb     := 0;
        w_sanghw_biyul  := 0;
    END;

    -- 원금최초상환일
    w_fst_ymd   := ADD_MONTHS(p_balh_ymd, w_geochi_gigan);

    if  p_sunme_gb = 'Y'    then    w_sunme_days    := p_balh_ymd - p_medo_ymd;    -- 선매출일수
    end if;

    w_tot_ija_hoicha    := ROUND(months_between(p_sanghw_ymd,w_fst_ymd) / 12) + 1;        -- 총 상환횟수
    w_jan_sang_hoisu    := LEAST(Floor(months_between(p_sanghw_ymd, w_trns_ymd) / 12) + 1, w_tot_ija_hoicha);       -- 잔존횟수

    -- 잔존원금 계산
    if  w_sanghw_gb = '1'   then    -- 균등상환
        w_unit_sanghw_wongm := p_aekm * w_sanghw_biyul;     -- 1회상환원금 = 액면금액 * 균등상환비율;
        w_jan_wongm         := p_aekm - w_unit_sanghw_wongm * (w_tot_ija_hoicha - w_jan_sang_hoisu);
    else
        BEGIN
            if  w_tot_ija_hoicha > w_jan_sang_hoisu then    -- 임의분할시 잔존상환원금 계산
                w_ii    := w_tot_ija_hoicha - w_jan_sang_hoisu;
                BEGIN
                    SELECT aekm
                      INTO w_jan_wongm
                      FROM SCM0IG ig
                     WHERE ig.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
                       AND ig.jm_cd    = p_jm_cd
                       AND ig.repay_no = w_ii;
                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
                    w_jan_wongm := p_aekm;
                END;
            else
                w_jan_wongm := p_aekm;
            end if;
        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
            w_jan_wongm := -1;
        END;
    end if;

    w_fst_jan_wongm := w_jan_wongm;

    w_ii    := w_tot_ija_hoicha - w_jan_sang_hoisu + 1;
    FOR cnt IN w_ii .. w_tot_ija_hoicha LOOP
        -- 다음상환일
        w_sanghw_ymd    := ADD_MONTHS(w_fst_ymd, (cnt - 1) * 12);
        if  w_sanghw_gb = '2'   then    -- 임의상환시 임의상환 단위금액 계산
            BEGIN
                SELECT sanghw_aek
                  INTO w_unit_sanghw_wongm
                  FROM SCM0IG ig
                 WHERE ig.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
                   AND ig.jm_cd    = p_jm_cd
                   AND ig.repay_no = cnt;
            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND then
                w_unit_sanghw_wongm := -1;
            END;
        end if;
        if  cnt = 1 then    -- 최초 원금상환 -> 거치이자 계산
            if  w_geochi_ija_gb = '7'   then    -- 거치기간 단리계산
                -- 선매출이자
                if  w_sunme_days > 0    then
                        w_sunme_ija := p_aekm * p_pyom_iyul * w_sunme_days / 365;
                else    w_sunme_ija := 0;
                end if;
                w_sanghw_won_rp_gm  := w_unit_sanghw_wongm + w_sunme_ija + (p_aekm * p_pyom_iyul * w_geochi_gigan / 12);

            else    -- 거치기간 복리계산
                -- 선매출이자
                if  w_sunme_days > 0    then
                        w_sunme_ija := power(1 + p_pyom_iyul, w_sunme_days / 365);
                else    w_sunme_ija := 1;
                end if;
                w_pay_freq_no       := w_geochi_gigan / p_geochi_ija_mons;
                w_sanghw_won_rp_gm  := w_unit_sanghw_wongm + (p_aekm * (Power(1 + p_pyom_iyul * p_geochi_ija_mons / 12, w_pay_freq_no) * w_sunme_ija - 1));
            end if;
            w_sanghw_won_rp_gm  := TRUNC(w_sanghw_won_rp_gm);
        else    -- 2차이상 상환 -> 원금 + 이자(단리)
            w_sanghw_won_rp_gm  := w_unit_sanghw_wongm + w_jan_wongm * p_pyom_iyul;
        end if;
        if  p_pf_tp = '1'   then    -- 현가(단가)계산
            w_jan_yy    := TRUNC((w_sanghw_ymd - w_trns_ymd) / 365);
            w_jan_dd    := (w_sanghw_ymd - w_trns_ymd) - (w_jan_yy * 365);
            r_danga     := (w_sanghw_won_rp_gm / (Power(1 + p_mk_suik_rt, w_jan_yy) * (1 + p_mk_suik_rt * w_jan_dd / 365)));
        else    -- 종가계산
            r_danga := w_sanghw_won_rp_gm * Power(1 + p_pyom_iyul, (w_tot_ija_hoicha - cnt));
        end if;
        w_jan_wongm := w_jan_wongm - w_unit_sanghw_wongm;
    END LOOP;
    RETURN  r_danga * (p_aekm / w_fst_jan_wongm);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_pgm_nm (
    p_bun                              IN  varchar,
    p_pgm_id                           IN  varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- Object 명
--
    w_nm                         fw_pgm_mst.pgm_nm%type;
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT pgm_nm
          INTO w_nm
          FROM FW_PGM_MST t1
         WHERE pgm_id = p_pgm_id;
    exception when OTHERS then
        w_nm    := p_pgm_id;
    end;

    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_PGM_NO
              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
    w_ok                        varchar(1);
BEGIN
    for xx in (
        SELECT  TRIM (to_char (ROWNUM,'00000'))  AS pgm_no
        FROM    DUAL
        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= 99999
    ) loop
        begin
            SELECT  '1'
              INTO  w_ok
            FROM    fw_pgm_mst t1
            WHERE   t1.pgm_no = xx.pgm_no;
        exception when  OTHERS  then
            RETURN xx.pgm_no;
        end;
    end loop;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_pg_cd (
    p_corp_gr                          IN  varchar,
    p_ymd                              IN  date,
    p_jm_cd                            IN  varchar
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 종목별 신용등급
--
    w_pg_cd                      varchar(4);
BEGIN
    if  p_jm_cd LIKE 'KR9%' then
        begin
            SELECT pg_cd
              INTO w_pg_cd
              FROM SHM1PG t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.ymd     = ( select max(ymd)
                                    from SHM1PG ta
                                   where ta.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                     and ta.ymd     <= p_ymd
                                     and ta.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd )
               AND jm_cd      = p_jm_cd;
        exception when others then
            w_pg_cd := '';
        end;
    else
        begin
            SELECT pg_cd
              INTO w_pg_cd
              FROM SCM1PG t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.ymd     = ( select max(ymd)
                                    from SCM1PG ta
                                   where ta.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                     and ta.ymd     <= p_ymd
                                     and ta.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd )
               AND jm_cd      = p_jm_cd;
        exception when others then
            w_pg_cd := '';
        end;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_pg_cd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_pg_nm (
    p_corp_gr                          IN  varchar,
    p_jm_cd                            IN  varchar,
    p_ymd                              IN  date
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 채권/현금 신용등급명
--
    w_pg_nm                      varchar(10);
    w_ymd                        date;
BEGIN
    if  p_jm_cd LIKE 'KR9%' then
        begin
            SELECT pg.pg_cd
              INTO w_pg_nm
              FROM SHM1PG pg
             WHERE pg.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND pg.ymd     = ( select max(ymd)
                                    from SHM1PG tt
                                   where tt.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                     and tt.ymd     <= p_ymd
                                     and tt.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd )
               AND pg.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;

            SELECT sebu_cd_nm
              INTO w_pg_nm
              FROM SZX0GR gr
             WHERE gr_cd   = 'Y6'
               AND sebu_cd = w_pg_nm;
        exception when OTHERS then
            w_pg_nm := '';
        end;
    else
        begin
            SELECT pg.pg_cd
              INTO w_pg_nm
              FROM SCM1PG pg
             WHERE pg.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND pg.ymd     = ( select max(ymd)
                                    from SCM1PG tt
                                   where tt.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
                                     and tt.ymd     <= p_ymd
                                     and tt.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd )
               AND pg.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;

            SELECT sebu_cd_nm
              INTO w_pg_nm
              FROM SZX0GR gr
             WHERE gr_cd   = 'Y5'
               AND sebu_cd = w_pg_nm;
        exception when OTHERS then
            w_pg_nm := '';
        end;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_pg_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_pg_ymd (
    p_corp_gr                          IN  varchar,
    p_jm_cd                            IN  varchar,
    p_ymd                              IN  date
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 채권 비우량 등급변경일
--
    w_jy_yn                      char(1)    := '-';
    w_ymd                        date;
BEGIN
    for xx IN (
        select ymd
             , jy_yn
          from SCM1PG pg
         where pg.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           and pg.ymd     <= p_ymd
           and pg.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd
          order by ymd desc
    ) loop
        if  w_jy_yn = '-'   then
            if  xx.jy_yn = 'N'  then
                exit;
            else
                w_jy_yn := 'Y';
            end if;
        elsif xx.jy_yn = 'Y'    then
            w_ymd   := xx.ymd;
        else
            exit;
        end if;
    end loop;
    RETURN  to_char (w_ymd,'yyyy.mm.dd');
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_pv_cb (
    i_aekm                      IN        number,           -- 액면
    i_mk_suik_rt                IN        number,           -- 시장수익률
    i_pyom_iyul                 IN        number,           -- 표면이율
    i_mang_ija                  IN        number,           -- 만기보장이자
    i_balh_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 발행일
    i_sanghw_ymd                IN        date,             -- 만기일
    i_gyul_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 결산일
    i_ija_mons                  IN        number,           -- 이자지급월수
    i_trns_ymd                  IN        date              -- 평가일
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 이표채(CB)의 단가를 계산한다.
--
    w_trns_ymd                  date;           -- 만기일 반영한 평가일
    w_unit_ija                  number;         -- 1회분 이자
    w_unit_k                    number;         -- 1회차이자
    w_unit_comp                 number;         -- 1회차이자
    w_ija_rak_hoisu             number;
    w_jan_ija_hoisu             number;
    w_tot_ija_hoicha            number;         -- 이자총지급회수
    w_gugan_ilsu                number;         -- 전이자지급일∼익이자지급일까지 총일수
    w_jan_ilsu                  number;         -- 거래일∼익이자지급일까지 잔여일수

    w_bf_ija_ymd                date;
    w_af_ija_ymd                date;
BEGIN
    if  i_sanghw_ymd < i_trns_ymd   then
            w_trns_ymd := i_sanghw_ymd; -- 평가일이 만기일 초과시만기일로 변경
    else    w_trns_ymd := i_trns_ymd;
    end if;

    w_unit_ija   := i_aekm * i_pyom_iyul * i_ija_mons / 12;     -- 1회분이자

    w_ija_rak_hoisu  := Floor(months_between(w_trns_ymd,i_gyul_ymd) / i_ija_mons) + 1;      -- 거래 전후 이자지급일
    w_tot_ija_hoicha := Floor(months_between(i_sanghw_ymd,i_gyul_ymd) / i_ija_mons) + 1;    -- 총이자회수
    w_jan_ija_hoisu  := w_tot_ija_hoicha - w_ija_rak_hoisu - 1;                             -- 이자잔존회수

    for  cnt  in  0 ..  nvl(w_jan_ija_hoisu,0)  loop
        if  (cnt = 0) And (i_gyul_ymd > w_trns_ymd) then    -- 한편넣기(초일 불산입)
            w_bf_ija_ymd    := add_months(i_gyul_ymd, i_ija_mons * -1);
            w_af_ija_ymd    := i_gyul_ymd;
            w_gugan_ilsu    := w_af_ija_ymd - w_bf_ija_ymd;     -- 총일수
            w_jan_ilsu      := w_af_ija_ymd - i_balh_ymd;       -- 잔여일수
            w_unit_k        := w_unit_ija * w_jan_ilsu / w_gugan_ilsu;
            w_unit_comp     := w_unit_k;
        else
            w_bf_ija_ymd    := add_months(i_gyul_ymd, (w_ija_rak_hoisu - 1) * i_ija_mons);
            w_af_ija_ymd    := add_months(w_bf_ija_ymd, i_ija_mons);
            w_gugan_ilsu    := w_af_ija_ymd - w_bf_ija_ymd;     -- 총일수
            w_jan_ilsu      := w_af_ija_ymd - w_trns_ymd;       -- 잔여일수
            if  cnt = w_jan_ija_hoisu   then
                    w_unit_k := i_aekm + w_unit_ija + i_mang_ija;
            else    w_unit_k := w_unit_ija;
            end if;
            w_unit_comp := nvl(w_unit_comp,0) + (nvl(w_unit_k,0) / power((1 + i_mk_suik_rt), cnt));
        end if;
    end loop;

    if  i_sanghw_ymd = w_trns_ymd   then
            RETURN  i_aekm + i_mang_ija;
    else    RETURN  nvl(w_unit_comp,0) / (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * w_jan_ilsu / w_gugan_ilsu);
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_PV_CPN (
    i_danga_gb                  IN        varchar,          -- 세금의제관련 C:세전(권리락후),B:세전,A:세후,D:경과세금
    i_aekm                      IN        number,           -- 액면
    i_mk_suik_rt                IN        number,           -- 시장수익률
    i_pyom_iyul                 IN        number,           -- 표면이율
    i_tot_ija_hoicha            IN        number,           -- 총이자회수
    i_balh_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 발행일
    i_sanghw_ymd                IN        date,             -- 만기일
    i_medo_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 매출일
    i_bf_ija_ymd                IN        date,
    i_af_ija_ymd                IN        date,
    i_sunhu_gb                  IN        varchar,          -- 이자지급구분 1:선급, 2:후급
    i_ija_mons                  IN        number,           -- 이자지급월수
    i_sunme_gb                  IN        varchar,          -- 선매출이자 계산여부
    i_trns_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 평가일
    i_halin_iyul                IN        number,               -- 할인이율
    i_mang_ija                  IN        number            -- 만기보장이자(CB)
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 이표채의 단가를 계산한다.
--
    w_trns_ymd                  date;           -- 만기일 반영한 평가일
    w_unit_ija                  number;         -- 1회분 이자
    w_ija_rak_hoisu             number;
    w_jan_ija_hoicha            number;         -- 이자총지급회수
    w_sunme_ija                 number := 0;    -- 선매출이자
    w_gugan_ilsu                number;         -- 전이자지급일∼익이자지급일까지 총일수
    w_jan_ilsu                  number;         -- 거래일∼익이자지급일까지 잔여일수
    w_k                         number;         -- 현가계수
    r_danga                     number;         -- 단가 중간값
    w_gyunggwa_ija              number default 0;
    w_gyunggwa_tax              number default 0;


    w_msg                       varchar(200);

    FUNCTION                    DIV ( I_A number,
                                     I_B number) RETURN number IS
BEGIN
    if  I_B = 0     then    RETURN 0;
    else                    RETURN I_A / I_B;
    end if;
END;

BEGIN
    if  i_sanghw_ymd < i_trns_ymd   then
            w_trns_ymd := i_sanghw_ymd; -- 평가일이 만기일 초과시 만기일로 변경
    else    w_trns_ymd := i_trns_ymd;
    end if;

    w_unit_ija  := i_aekm * i_pyom_iyul * i_ija_mons / 12;      -- 1회분이자

    -- 거래 전후 이자지급일
    w_ija_rak_hoisu := Floor(Months_Between(w_trns_ymd,i_balh_ymd) / i_ija_mons);

    w_gugan_ilsu    := i_af_ija_ymd - i_bf_ija_ymd;     -- 총일수
    w_jan_ilsu      := i_af_ija_ymd - w_trns_ymd;       -- 잔여일수

    if  i_sunme_gb = 'Y'    then    w_sunme_ija := 10000 * i_pyom_iyul * (i_balh_ymd - i_medo_ymd) / 365;   -- 선매출이자
    end if;

    w_k := 1 / (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * i_ija_mons / 12);    -- 현가계수 K = 1 / (1+수익률*이자지급주기월수/12)

    if  i_sunhu_gb = '1'    then    -- 선급
        w_jan_ija_hoicha    := Floor(Months_Between(i_sanghw_ymd,Greatest(w_trns_ymd,i_balh_ymd)) / i_ija_mons);
        -- 잔존이자회수 보정
        if  Floor(MONTHS_BETWEEN(i_sanghw_ymd,i_balh_ymd) / i_ija_mons) > (w_jan_ija_hoicha + w_ija_rak_hoisu)     then
            w_jan_ija_hoicha    := w_jan_ija_hoicha + 1;
        end if;
        -- 단가 = ((1회이자*(1-K**이자회수))/(1-K) + 액면*K**이자회수 + 선매출이자) / (1+수익률*이자지급주기/12**(발행일전구간수-1)) /
        --        (1+수익률*이자지급주기/12*잔여일수/전체일수)
        r_danga := (w_unit_ija * DIV((1 - POWER(w_k,w_jan_ija_hoicha)),(1 - w_k)) + (i_aekm + i_mang_ija) * POWER(w_k,w_jan_ija_hoicha) + w_sunme_ija)  -- 20210910 bjh
                               / POWER(1 + i_mk_suik_rt * i_ija_mons / 12,Abs(Least(w_ija_rak_hoisu + 1,0)))
                               / (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * i_ija_mons / 12 * (w_jan_ilsu / w_gugan_ilsu));
    else    -- 후급
        w_jan_ija_hoicha    := CEIL(MONTHS_BETWEEN(i_sanghw_ymd,GREATEST(w_trns_ymd,i_balh_ymd)) / i_ija_mons);
        -- 잔존이자회수 보정
        if  CEIL(MONTHS_BETWEEN(i_sanghw_ymd,i_balh_ymd) / i_ija_mons) > (w_jan_ija_hoicha + w_ija_rak_hoisu)  then
            w_jan_ija_hoicha    := w_jan_ija_hoicha + 1;
        end if;
        -- 단가 = ((1회이자*(1-K**이자회수))/(1-K) + 액면*K**(이자회수-1) + 선매출이자)
        --      / (1+수익률*이자지급주기/12**발행일전구간수)
        --      / (1+수익률*이자지급주기/12*잔여일수/전체일수) + 선매출이자 현가
        r_danga := (w_unit_ija * DIV((1 - POWER(w_k,w_jan_ija_hoicha)),(1 - w_k)) + (i_aekm + i_mang_ija) * POWER(w_k,w_jan_ija_hoicha - 1) + w_sunme_ija)
                               / POWER(1 + i_mk_suik_rt * i_ija_mons / 12,Abs(Least(w_ija_rak_hoisu,0)))
                               / (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * i_ija_mons / 12 * (w_jan_ilsu / w_gugan_ilsu));
    end if;

    dbms_output.put_line ('이표채 단가:' || F_NTRIM (r_danga) || ' ... w_unit_ija : ' || F_NTRIM (w_unit_ija) || ' w_k : ' || to_char (w_k)
                                         || ' jan_ija_hoicha : ' || w_jan_ija_hoicha || ' aekm : ' || F_NTRIM (i_aekm)
                                         || ' w_sunme_ija : ' || F_NTRIM (w_sunme_ija)
                                         || ' suik_rt : ' || to_char (i_mk_suik_rt) || ' pyom_iyul ' || to_char (i_pyom_iyul)
                                         || ' ija_mons : ' || F_NTRIM (i_ija_mons) || ' ija_rak_hoisu : ' || to_char (w_ija_rak_hoisu)
                                         || ' w_jan_ilsu : ' || F_NTRIM (w_jan_ilsu) || ' w_gugan_ilsu : ' || to_char (w_gugan_ilsu)
                                         || ' bf : ' || i_bf_ija_ymd || ' af : ' || i_af_ija_ymd);

    if  i_danga_gb = 'C'    then
        RETURN  r_danga;
    end if;

    if (i_balh_ymd < w_trns_ymd) And (w_trns_ymd = i_bf_ija_ymd)    then
        if  i_danga_gb = 'A'    then
            RETURN r_danga; -- 세후단가는 이자락후 단가 return
        end if;
        r_danga := r_danga + w_unit_ija;
    end if;

    if  i_danga_gb = 'B'    then
        RETURN r_danga;
    end if;

    w_gyunggwa_ija  := w_sunme_ija + (i_aekm * nvl(i_halin_iyul,0) * (w_trns_ymd - i_balh_ymd) / (i_sanghw_ymd - i_balh_ymd));          -- 할인분 경과이자
    w_gyunggwa_ija  := nvl(w_gyunggwa_ija,0) + greatest(0,w_unit_ija * (w_trns_ymd - i_bf_ija_ymd) / (i_af_ija_ymd - i_bf_ija_ymd));    -- 표면이자 경과이자
    w_gyunggwa_tax  := 0;

    if  i_danga_gb = 'A'    then    RETURN  r_danga - w_gyunggwa_tax;   -- 세후단가
    elsif i_danga_gb = 'D'  then    RETURN  w_gyunggwa_tax;             -- 경과세금
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_PV_ETC (
    i_danga_gb                  IN        varchar,          -- 세금의제관련 B:세전,A:세후,D:경과세금
    i_fv                        IN        number,           -- 종가(만기금액)
    i_mk_suik_rt                IN        number,           -- 시장수익률
    i_balh_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 발행일
    i_sanghw_ymd                IN        date,             -- 만기일
    i_trns_ymd                  IN        date,             -- 평가일
    i_ija_mons                  IN        number,           -- 이자지급월수
    i_tot_ija_hoicha            IN        number,           -- 총이자지급회차
    i_ija_jigub_gb              IN        varchar,          -- 이자지급구분
    i_pyom_iyul                 IN        number,           -- 표면이율
    i_medo_ymd                  IN        date,
    i_sunhu_gb                  IN        varchar,
    i_sunme_gb                  IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 할인/복리/단리채의 단가를 계산한다.
--
    w_trns_ymd                  date;   -- 만기일 반영한 평가일
    w_jan_yy                    number; -- 잔여년수
    w_jan_dd                    number; -- 잔여일수
    r_danga                     number; -- 단가 중간값
    w_sunme_ija                 number default 0;
    w_gyunggwa_ija              number;
    w_gyunggwa_tax              number;
BEGIN
    if  i_sanghw_ymd < i_trns_ymd   then
            w_trns_ymd := i_sanghw_ymd; -- 평가일이 만기일 초과시 만기일로 변경
    else    w_trns_ymd := i_trns_ymd;
    end if;
    w_jan_yy    := trunc(months_between(i_sanghw_ymd, w_trns_ymd) / 12);    -- 잔여년수
    w_jan_dd    := add_months(i_sanghw_ymd, w_jan_yy * -12) - w_trns_ymd;   -- 잔여일수

    begin   -- 단가 = 만기금액 / ((1+수익률)**잔여년수 * (1+수익률*잔여일수/년구간일수))
        r_danga := i_fv / ((Power(1 + i_mk_suik_rt, w_jan_yy)) * (1 + i_mk_suik_rt * w_jan_dd / 365));
    exception when  OTHERS  then
        r_danga := i_fv;
    end;

    if  i_danga_gb IN ('C','B') then    RETURN  r_danga;
    end if;

    if  i_sunme_gb = 'Y'    then    w_sunme_ija := 10000 * i_pyom_iyul * (i_balh_ymd - i_medo_ymd) / 365;   -- 선매출이자
    end if;
    -- 경과이자 계산
    if  i_ija_jigub_gb IN ('4','5','6','D','E','F') then    -- 복리채
            w_gyunggwa_ija  := F_FV_ETC (10000, i_balh_ymd, i_trns_ymd, i_medo_ymd, i_ija_jigub_gb, i_ija_mons, i_sunhu_gb, i_pyom_iyul, i_tot_ija_hoicha, i_sunme_gb) - 10000;
    else    w_gyunggwa_ija  := i_fv * i_pyom_iyul  * (w_trns_ymd - i_balh_ymd) / 365;
    end if;
    w_gyunggwa_tax  := 0;
    -- 세금 의제 관련
    if  i_danga_gb = 'A'    then
            RETURN  r_danga - w_gyunggwa_tax;
    else    RETURN  w_gyunggwa_tax;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_QUERYPARAMS (
    p_id                           IN    VARCHAR,
    p_params                       IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
--
-- QUERYPARAMS
    w_data                       VARCHAR2(4096);
BEGIN
    w_data  := SUBSTR (p_params,INSTR (p_params,p_id));
    w_data  := SUBSTR(w_data,INSTR(w_data,'"') + 1);
    w_data  := SUBSTR(w_data,1,INSTR(w_data,'"') - 1);

    RETURN  w_data;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_rak_jusu (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_ymd                       IN        date,
    p_balh_co                   IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 주식 권리락 주식
--
    w_jusu                      number;
begin
    SELECT  NVL(sum(bfil_boyu_jusu),0)
      INTO  w_jusu
    FROM    sjm0jm t1
    WHERE   t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
      AND   t1.ymd     = p_ymd
      AND   t1.balh_co = p_balh_co;

    RETURN  w_jusu;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_round (
    p_aek                       IN        number,
    p_fund_currency             IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 기준통화에 따른 반올림
--
BEGIN
    if  p_fund_currency = 'KRW' then
            RETURN  round (nvl(p_aek,0));
    else    RETURN  round (nvl(p_aek,0),2);
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SBOSU_NM (
    p_target_jasan                 IN    VARCHAR,
    p_bm_per                       IN    NUMBER,
    p_success_per                  IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 성과보수유형명
--
    w_sbosu_nm                   VARCHAR(80);
BEGIN
    if  p_target_jasan = '--'   then
        w_sbosu_nm  := '';
    else
        if NVL (p_bm_per,0) > 0 then
            w_sbosu_nm  := '기준수익율 ' || F_N# (p_bm_per) || '% 초과';
            if  p_success_per > 0   then
                if  p_target_jasan = '++'   then    w_sbosu_nm  := w_sbosu_nm || '수익 합계의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                elsif p_target_jasan = '-C' then    w_sbosu_nm  := w_sbosu_nm || '수익중 채권제외액의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                elsif p_target_jasan = '+J' then    w_sbosu_nm  := w_sbosu_nm || ' 주식수익의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                else                                w_sbosu_nm  := w_sbosu_nm || ' 운용수익의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                end if;
            end if;
        else
            if  p_success_per > 0   then
                if  p_target_jasan = '++'   then    w_sbosu_nm  := '수익합계의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                elsif p_target_jasan = '-C' then    w_sbosu_nm  := '채권제외 수익의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                elsif p_target_jasan = '+J' then    w_sbosu_nm  := '주식수익의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                else                                w_sbosu_nm  := '운용수익의 ' || F_N# (p_success_per) || '%';
                end if;
            else
                w_sbosu_nm  := '';
            end if;
        end if;

    end if;
    RETURN  w_sbosu_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SB_NM (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_sb_cd                     IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 사원명
--
    w_sb_nm                     varchar(40);
BEGIN
    if  p_sb_cd is null then    RETURN  null;
    end if;
    begin
        SELECT CASE WHEN out_ymd IS NULL THEN user_nm
                                         ELSE '*' || user_nm END
          INTO w_sb_nm
        FROM   fw_user_mst t1
        WHERE  t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
          AND  t1.user_id = p_sb_cd;
    exception when  OTHERS  then
        w_sb_nm := p_sb_cd;
    end;
    RETURN  w_sb_nm;
END;
/
