CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SHM0HJ (
    p_corp_gr                          IN  varchar,
    p_jm2                              IN  varchar,
    p_jm3                              IN  varchar,
    p_cash_cd                          IN  varchar,
    p_ksd_jm_cd                        IN  varchar,
    p_ymd                              IN  date,
    p_jm_nm                            IN  varchar,
    p_tr_co_cd                         IN  varchar,
    p_sanghw_ymd                       IN  date,
    p_pyom_iyul                        IN  number,
    p_mk_suik_rt                       IN  number
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 전단채 현금종목 생성
--
    w_pyom_iyul                  shm0hj.pyom_iyul%type;
    w_meib_suik_rt               shm0hj.meib_suik_rt%type;

    w_y                          varchar (1);
    w_m                          varchar (1);
    w_d                          varchar (1);
    w_jm_cd                      shm0hj.jm_cd%type;
    w_seq                        number;

    w_balh_ymd                   date;
    w_sanghw_ymd                 date;

    PRAGMA                       autonomous_transaction;

BEGIN
    w_meib_suik_rt  := p_mk_suik_rt / 100;
    w_pyom_iyul     := p_pyom_iyul / 100;

--    SR_BSS (p_corp_gr, 'CODE', '현금종목찾기', p_corp_gr || ' ' || p_ksd_jm_cd || ' ' || p_tr_co_cd || ' ' || w_meib_suik_rt || ' ' || w_pyom_iyul || ' ' || p_sanghw_ymd);

    begin
        SELECT jm_cd
          INTO w_jm_cd
          FROM shm0hj t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr      = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ksd_jm_cd    = p_ksd_jm_cd
           AND t1.tr_co_cd     = p_tr_co_cd
           AND t1.meib_suik_rt = w_meib_suik_rt
           AND t1.sanghw_ymd   = p_sanghw_ymd
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    exception when OTHERS Then
        SELECT id_dae_cd
          INTO w_y
          FROM szx0ym t1
         WHERE t1.ymd_gb = 'Y'
           AND t1.ymd_id = TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'yyyy');

        SELECT id_dae_cd
          INTO w_m
          FROM szx0ym t1
         WHERE t1.ymd_gb = 'M'
           AND t1.ymd_id = TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'mm');

        w_jm_cd := 'KR9' || w_y || w_m || to_char (p_ymd,'dd') || p_cash_cd || '___';

        SELECT NVL(MAX(SUBSTR(JM_CD,10,2)),0) + 1
          INTO w_seq
          FROM SHM0HJ t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.jm_cd   LIKE w_jm_cd;

        w_jm_cd := replace (w_jm_cd,'___',TRIM (TO_CHAR (w_seq,'00')));
        w_jm_cd := F_JM_CHECK (w_jm_cd);

        begin
            SELECT balh_ymd
                  ,sanghw_ymd
              INTO w_balh_ymd
                  ,w_sanghw_ymd
              FROM shm0hj t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr   = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.ksd_jm_cd = p_ksd_jm_cd
               AND ROWNUM = 1;
        exception when OTHERS Then
            w_balh_ymd      := p_ymd;
            w_sanghw_ymd    := p_sanghw_ymd;
        end;

        INSERT INTO SHM0HJ   tt
        VALUES ( p_corp_gr                                /* _1- */
               , w_jm_cd                                  /* _2- */
               , p_jm_nm                                  /* _3- */
               , p_cash_cd                                /* _4- */
               , 0                                        /* _5- */
               , 0                                        /* _6- */
               , 0                                        /* _7- */
               , 0                                        /* _8- */
               , w_balh_ymd                               /* _9- */
               , p_ymd                                    /* _10- */
               , w_sanghw_ymd                             /* _11- */
               , '2'                                      /* _12- */
               , CASE WHEN p_cash_cd='ZS' THEN '3'
                                          ELSE '1' END    /* _13- */
               , '2'                                      /* _14- */
               , NULL                                     /* _15- */
               , 1                                        /* _16- */
               , 1                                        /* _17- */
               , 1                                        /* _18- */
               , NULL                                     /* _19- */
               , '1'                                      /* _20- */
               , '1'                                      /* _21- */
               , w_meib_suik_rt                           /* _22- */
               , w_pyom_iyul                              /* _23- */
               , 1                                        /* _24- */
               , w_balh_ymd                               /* _25- */
               , w_sanghw_ymd                             /* _26- */
               , p_tr_co_cd                               /* _27- */
               , p_jm2                                    /* _28- */
               , NULL                                     /* _29- */
               , NULL                                     /* _30- */
               , '2'                                      /* _31- */
               , '0'                                      /* _32- */
               , NULL                                     /* _33- */
               , NULL                                     /* _34- */
               , NULL                                     /* _35- */
               , NULL                                     /* _36- */
               , NULL                                     /* _37- */
               , p_ksd_jm_cd                              /* _38- */
               , TO_NUMBER(p_jm3)                         /* _39- */
               , '1'                                      /* _40- */
               , NULL                                     /* _41- */
               , NULL                                     /* _42- */
               , NULL                                     /* _43- */
               , NULL                                     /* _44- */
               , NULL                                     /* _45- */
               , NULL                                     /* _46- */
               , NULL                                     /* _47- */
               );

        SR_SHJ0IG (p_corp_gr,w_jm_cd,'no');
    END;
    COMMIT;

    RETURN w_jm_cd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_siga_dur (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_ymd                       IN        date,
    p_jm_cd                     IN        varchar,
    p_value_gb                  IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 시가평가 채권, 현금 듀레이션
--
    w_dur                       number := 0;
    w_md                        number := 0;
    w_conv                      number := 0;
    w_jy_suik_rt                number := 0;
begin
    if  p_jm_cd not like 'KR9%' then    -- 채권
        begin   -- 듀레이션
            SELECT  NVL(dur,0)
                  , NVL(md,0)
                  , NVL(conv,0)
                  , NVL(jy_suik_rt,0)
              INTO  w_dur
                  , w_md
                  , w_conv
                  , w_jy_suik_rt
            FROM    scm1sm t1
            WHERE   t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
              AND   t1.ymd     = p_ymd
              AND   t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
        exception when  OTHERS  then
            RETURN 0;
        end;
        if  p_value_gb = 'dur'          then    RETURN  w_dur;
        elsif  p_value_gb = 'md'        then    RETURN  w_md;
        elsif  p_value_gb = 'conv'      then    RETURN  w_conv;
        elsif  p_value_gb = 'suik_rt'   then    RETURN  w_jy_suik_rt;
        else                                    RETURN  0;
        end if;
    else    -- 현금
        begin   -- 듀레이션
            SELECT  NVL(dur,0)
                  , NVL(md,0)
                  , NVL(jy_suik_rt,0)
              INTO  w_dur
                  , w_md
                  , w_jy_suik_rt
            FROM    shm1sm t1
            WHERE   t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
              AND   t1.ymd     = p_ymd
              AND   t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
        exception when  OTHERS  then
            RETURN 0;
        end;
        if  p_value_gb = 'dur'          then    RETURN  w_dur;
        elsif  p_value_gb = 'md'        then    RETURN  w_md;
        elsif  p_value_gb = 'suik_rt'   then    RETURN  w_jy_suik_rt;
        else                                    RETURN  0;
        end if;
    end if;
    RETURN  0;
end;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SJM0SM (
    p_corp_gr                   IN        varchar,
    p_ymd                       IN        date,
    p_fund_cd                   IN        varchar,
    p_mo_fund                   IN        varchar
)                 RETURN  DATE  IS
-- 전일잔고 기준일
--
    w_bf_ymd                    date;
BEGIN
    SELECT max(ymd)
      INTO w_bf_ymd
    FROM   sjm0sm t1
    WHERE  t1.corp_gr    = p_corp_gr
      AND  t1.ymd        Between  add_months(p_ymd,-6) And p_ymd - 1
      AND  t1.fund_cd    = p_fund_cd
      AND  t1.mo_fund_cd = p_mo_fund;

    if  w_bf_ymd is null    then
        SELECT p_ymd - 1
          INTO w_bf_ymd
        FROM   dual;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_bf_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SJ_NM (
    p_jm_cd                     IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 선물종목명 Found
--
    w_nm                        varchar(200);
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT  sj_nm
          INTO  w_nm
        FROM    ssx0kj t1
        WHERE   t1.corp_gr LIKE '%'
          AND   t1.sj_cd   = p_jm_cd
          AND   ROWNUM = 1;
    exception when  OTHERS  then
        w_nm    := p_jm_cd;
    end;
    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SPLIT (
    p_data                         IN    VARCHAR2,
    p_delimiter                    IN    VARCHAR2
)   RETURN t_split pipelined IS

    w_data                       VARCHAR2(32000)    := p_data;
    w_idx                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    LOOP
        w_idx   := INSTR (w_data,p_delimiter);
        if  w_idx > 0   then
            pipe ROW(SUBSTR(w_data,1,w_idx - 1));
            w_data  := SUBSTR (w_data,w_idx + LENGTH(p_delimiter));
        else
            pipe ROW(w_data);
            exit;
        end if;
    END LOOP;
    RETURN;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SPT1TX (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_text_gb                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 펀드특성, 운용개요, 공지사항
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;

--    BEGINNING                    INTEGER    := 32000;
--    ENDING                       INTEGER    := 1;
--    w_clob                       clob;
    w_str                        VARCHAR(32000) DEFAULT ' ';
BEGIN
    if  p_text_gb = '2A'    then
--        if  p_corp_gr = '2402'  then    -- 리코 계좌별
             SELECT MAX(ymd)
              INTO w_ymd
              FROM SPT1TX t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     <= p_ymd
               AND fs_gb   = 'F'
               AND fs_cd   = p_fund_cd
               AND text_gb = '2A';
--        else
--            SELECT MAX(ymd)
--              INTO w_ymd
--              FROM SPT1TX t1
--             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
--               AND ymd     <= p_ymd
--               AND fs_gb   = 'F'
--               AND fs_cd   = '%'
--               AND text_gb = '2A';
--       end if;
    elsif p_text_gb = '2B'  then
--        if  p_corp_gr = '2402'  then    -- 리코 계좌별
            SELECT MAX(ymd)
              INTO w_ymd
              FROM SPT1TX t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     <= p_ymd
               AND fs_gb   = 'F'
               AND fs_cd   = p_fund_cd
               AND text_gb = '2B';
--        else
--            SELECT MAX(ymd)
--              INTO w_ymd
--              FROM SPT1TX t1
--             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
--               AND ymd     <= p_ymd
--               AND fs_gb   = 'F'
--               AND fs_cd   = '%'
--               AND text_gb = '2B';
--        end if;
    else
        if  TO_CHAR (p_ymd,'mm') IN ('01','02','03')    then
            w_ymd   := TO_DATE (TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'yyyy') || '0331','yyyymmdd');
        elsif TO_CHAR (p_ymd,'mm') IN ('04','05','06')      then
            w_ymd   := TO_DATE (TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'yyyy') || '0630','yyyymmdd');
        elsif TO_CHAR (p_ymd,'mm') IN ('07','08','09')      then
            w_ymd   := TO_DATE (TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'yyyy') || '0930','yyyymmdd');
        else
            w_ymd   := TO_DATE (TO_CHAR(p_ymd,'yyyy') || '1231','yyyymmdd');
        end if;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT text
          INTO w_str
          FROM SPT1TX t1
         WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND ymd     = w_ymd
           AND fs_gb   = 'F'
           AND fs_cd   = p_fund_cd
           AND text_gb = p_text_gb;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        BEGIN
            SELECT text
              INTO w_str
              FROM SPT1TX t1
             WHERE corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND ymd     = w_ymd
               AND fs_gb   = 'F'
               AND fs_cd   = '%'
               AND text_gb = p_text_gb;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            w_str  := '';
        END;
    END;
    
    RETURN  w_str;

--    if  w_clob IS NULL  then
--            RETURN  ' ';
--    else    DBMS_LOB.READ (w_clob, BEGINNING, ENDING, w_str);
--    end if;
--
--    if (w_str IS NULL) OR (w_str = '')  then
--            RETURN  ' ';
--    else    RETURN  SUBSTR(w_str,1,4000);
--    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_STD_CDF (
    x                              IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
--
-- 표준 정규분포 누적분포 함수 (CDF) 근사값
--
-- 누적 정규분포 근사 (N(x)), Abramowitz & Stegun 공식
    t                            NUMBER;
    b1                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := 0.319381530;
    b2                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := -0.356563782;
    b3                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := 1.781477937;
    b4                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := -1.821255978;
    b5                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := 1.330274429;
    p                            CONSTANT NUMBER    := 0.2316419;
    c2                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := 0.3989423;
    a                            NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  x < -6.0    then
        RETURN 0.0;
    elsif x > 6.0   then
        RETURN 1.0;
    else
        t   := 1.0 / (1.0 + p * ABS(x));
        a   := 1.0 - c2 * EXP( - x * x / 2.0) * t * (b1 + t * (b2 + t * (b3 + t * (b4 + t * b5))));
        if  x < 0.0 then
            RETURN 1.0 - a;
        else
            RETURN a;
        end if;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_STD_PDF (
    x                              IN    NUMBER
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 
-- 표준정규 분포 밀도 함수 (Normal Distribution Density Function, PDF)
--
    pi                           CONSTANT NUMBER    := 3.141592653589793;
    exponent                     NUMBER;
    result                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    exponent    := -0.5 * POWER(x, 2);
    result  := (1 / SQRT(2 * pi)) * EXP(exponent);
    RETURN result;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_STOCK (
    p_date                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 거래소 영업일 확인 Return ('0':영업일, '1':휴장일)
--
    w_date                       DATE;
    w_chk                        VARCHAR(1);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT '1'
          INTO w_chk
          FROM SZX0HL t1
         WHERE nation_cd = 'KR'
           AND holi_ymd = p_date;
        RETURN  '1';
    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
        NULL;
    END;
    if  TO_CHAR (p_date,'Dy') IN ('Sat','토')    then    RETURN  '1';
    end if;

    -- 2001년부터 12/31 휴장(당일이 휴장일인경우 직전 영업일을 연말 휴장일로 함)
    if  TO_CHAR (p_date,'mmdd') > '1225'    then
        w_date  := LAST_DAY(p_date);
        LOOP
            BEGIN
                SELECT '1'
                  INTO w_chk
                  FROM SZX0HL t1
                 WHERE nation_cd = 'KR'
                   AND holi_ymd  = w_date;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
                if  TO_CHAR (w_date,'Dy') NOT IN ('Sat','토')    then    EXIT;
                end if;
            END;
            w_date  := w_date - 1;
        END LOOP;
        if  p_date >= w_date    then    RETURN  '1';
        end if;
    end if;

    RETURN  '0';
END;
/

GRANT EXECUTE ON KFP.F_STOCK TO AFMS;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_sysdate
                 RETURN  date  IS
BEGIN
    RETURN  now ();
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_systimestamp
                 RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
BEGIN
    RETURN  to_char (systimestamp,'hh24:mi:ss.ff6');
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_szx0gr (
    p_gr_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_sebu_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nm_gu                        IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'nm'
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 그룹코드명
--
    w_nm                         VARCHAR(80);
BEGIN
    if  p_sebu_cd IS NULL   then    RETURN NULL;
    end if;
    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE lower (p_nm_gu) WHEN 'efnm' THEN sebu_cd_efnm
                                    WHEN 'enm'  THEN sebu_cd_enm
                                                ELSE sebu_cd_nm END
          INTO w_nm
          FROM SZX0GR t1
         WHERE gr_cd   = p_gr_cd
           AND sebu_cd = p_sebu_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        w_nm    := NVL(p_gr_cd,'?') || '-' || NVL(p_sebu_cd,'##');
    END;
    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_szx0gr_num (
    p_gr_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_sebu_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nm_gu                        IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'num'
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 그룹코드명
--
    w_nm                         NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_sebu_cd IS NULL   then    RETURN NULL;
    end if;
    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE lower (p_nm_gu) WHEN 'num'  THEN sebu_num
                                                ELSE sebu_num END
          INTO w_nm
          FROM SZX0GR t1
         WHERE gr_cd   = p_gr_cd
           AND sebu_cd = p_sebu_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        w_nm    := NVL(p_gr_cd,'?') || '-' || NVL(p_sebu_cd,'##');
    END;
    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SZX1GR (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_gr_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_sebu_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nm_gu                        IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'nm'
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 회사별 관리 그룹코드명
--
    w_nm                         VARCHAR(500);
BEGIN
    if  p_sebu_cd IS NULL   then    RETURN NULL;
    end if;
    BEGIN
        SELECT CASE lower (p_nm_gu) WHEN 'efnm' THEN sebu_cd_efnm
                                    WHEN 'enm'  THEN sebu_cd_enm
                                                ELSE sebu_cd_nm END
          INTO w_nm
          FROM SZX1GR t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.gr_cd   = p_gr_cd
           AND t1.sebu_cd = p_sebu_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        w_nm    := p_corp_gr || '.' || NVL(p_gr_cd,'?') || '-' || NVL(p_sebu_cd,'##');
    END;
    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_SZX1GR_MULTI (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_gr_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_sebu_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_nm_gu                        IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT 'nm'
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 회사별 관리 그룹코드명
--
    w_nm                         VARCHAR(500);
    w_sebu_cd                    VARCHAR(10);
    w_t5                         VARCHAR(1);
    w_pos                        NUMBER;
    w_length                     NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_sebu_cd IS NULL   then    RETURN NULL;
    end if;

    w_sebu_cd   := p_sebu_cd;
    w_pos       := INSTR (w_sebu_cd,',');

    if  w_pos > 0   then

        LOOP
            w_pos   := INSTR (w_sebu_cd,',');
            if  w_pos = 0   then
                    w_t5    := SUBSTR (w_sebu_cd,1,1);
            else    w_t5    := SUBSTR (w_sebu_cd,1,w_pos - 1);
            end if;
            w_nm    := w_nm || f_szx1gr(p_corp_gr, 'T5', w_t5, 'nm') || chr(10);

            w_sebu_cd   := SUBSTR (w_sebu_cd,w_pos + 1);
            EXIT WHEN w_pos < 1;
        END LOOP;

    else
        w_nm    := w_nm || f_szx1gr(p_corp_gr, 'T5', w_sebu_cd, 'nm');
    end if;

    RETURN  w_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TAX (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 이자소득세율
--
    w_incom_tax                  NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT incom_tax
      INTO w_incom_tax
      FROM SZX0SB t1
     WHERE p_ymd Between f_ymd And t_ymd;

    RETURN  w_incom_tax;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TAX_GB (
    p_gwamok                       IN    VARCHAR,
    p_tax_gb                       IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 과표/비과표구분
--
    w_tax_gb                     szx0gm.tax_gb%type;
BEGIN
    if  p_tax_gb = '5'  then
        SELECT tax_gb
          INTO w_tax_gb
          FROM SZX0GM t1
         WHERE t1.gwamok = (select MAX(hab_gwamok)
                              from SZX0CC ta
                             where ta.gwamok = p_gwamok);
    else
        w_tax_gb    := p_tax_gb;
    end if;

    RETURN  w_tax_gb;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TAX_RT (
    p_tax_gb                       IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE DEFAULT sysdate
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 세율(2014년 기준)
--
    w_tax_rt                     NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if  p_tax_gb = '0'          then    w_tax_rt    := 0;       -- 비과세
    elsif p_tax_gb IN ('1','5') then    w_tax_rt    := 0.154;   -- 개인소득세
    elsif p_tax_gb = '2'        then    w_tax_rt    := 0;       -- 법인소득세
    else                                w_tax_rt    := 0; 
    end if;

    RETURN  w_tax_rt;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TIME (
    p_finish                       IN    DATE,
    p_start                        IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 
--
    w_hh                         NUMBER;
    w_mm                         NUMBER;
    w_ss                         NUMBER;

    w_rtn                        VARCHAR(10);
BEGIN
    w_hh    := trunc(((86400 * (p_finish - p_start)) / 60) / 60) - 24 * (trunc((((86400 * (p_finish - p_start)) / 60) / 60) / 24));
    w_mm    := trunc((86400 * (p_finish - p_start)) / 60) - 60 * (trunc(((86400 * (p_finish - p_start)) / 60) / 60));
    w_ss    := trunc(86400 * (p_finish - p_start)) - 60 * (trunc((86400 * (p_finish - p_start)) / 60));

    if  w_hh > 0    then    w_rtn   := f_ntrim (w_hh,2) || ':' || TRIM(TO_CHAR (w_mm,'00')) || ':' || TRIM(TO_CHAR(w_ss,'00'));
    elsif w_mm > 0  then    w_rtn   := f_ntrim (w_mm,2) || ':' || TRIM(TO_CHAR(w_ss,'00'));
    else                    w_rtn   := f_ntrim (w_ss,2);
    end if;

    RETURN  LPAD (w_rtn,8);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TODAY_BUY_QTY (
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_total_qty                    IN    NUMBER,  /* 총수량 */
    p_days                         IN    NUMBER,  /* 기준 일수 (예: 5) */
    p_bought_sum                   IN    NUMBER,  /* 지금까지 매수한 총합 */
    p_today                        IN    NUMBER   /* 오늘이 몇 번째 날인지 (1~p_days) */
 ) RETURN NUMBER IS
    v_daily_avg                  NUMBER; -- 기준 하루 매수량
    v_target_sum                 NUMBER; -- 오늘까지 매수해야 할 기준 누적량
    v_today_qty                  NUMBER; -- 오늘 매수해야 할 수량
BEGIN



/*
SELECT t1.CORP_GR
     , t1.YMD
     , t1.FUND_CD
     , t1.PORT_NUM
     , t1.JM_CD
     , NVL(ya.SRS_CD, ya.BLBG_TCKR)                                                     AS srs_cd
     , ya.JM_NM
     , t1.COL
     , t1.JONGA
     , t1.TT_PRICE
     , t1.TARGET_RETURN_PER
     , t1.USD_TUJA
     , (t1.USD_TUJA - NVL(pp.STOCK_CHUI,0) - NVL(pp.opt_nominal_amt,0)) / t2.INST_DAYS  AS mo_amt
     , t2.INST_DAYS
     , CASE WHEN NVL(t1.TT_PRICE,0)=0
            THEN 0
            ELSE CASE WHEN t1.USD_TUJA > (NVL(pp.STOCK_CHUI,0) + NVL(pp.opt_nominal_amt,0))
                      THEN TRUNC((t1.USD_TUJA - NVL(pp.STOCK_CHUI,0) - NVL(pp.opt_nominal_amt,0)) / t2.INST_DAYS / t1.TT_PRICE / 100,0)
                      ELSE 0 END END                                                    AS MIN_JUSU
     , t2.FYMD
     , ya.CURRENCY
     , pp.OPT_QTY
     , pp.opt_nominal_amt
     , pp.OPT_PREMIUM
     , tt.risk_value
     , F_OPT_COLOR (t1.CORP_GR, t1.YMD, t1.FUND_CD, t1.JM_CD, t1.JONGA)                 AS line_color
  FROM USPM      t1
     , USPM_PORT t2
     , (SELECT t1.PORT
             , t1.JM_CD
             , t1.STOCK_JUSU
             , t1.STOCK_CHUI
             , t1.OPT_NOMINAL_AMT
             , t1.OPT_QTY
             , t1.OPT_PREMIUM
          FROM USPM_OPT t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = :corp_gr
           AND t1.FUND_CD = :fund_cd
           AND t1.YMD     = :ymd) pp
     , SYM0YA    ya
     , (SELECT yz.TRUSTEE
             , ya.CHANGE_JM_CD
             , SUM(NVL(yz.DELTA_RISK_VALUE,0) + NVL (yz.GAMMA_RISK_VALUE,0) + NVL (yz.VEGA_RISK_VALUE,0))  AS risk_value
          FROM SYM0YZ yz
             , SYM0YA    ya
         WHERE yz.CORP_GR = :corp_gr
           AND yz.FUND_CD = :fund_cd
           AND yz.YMD     = :ymd
           AND ya.CORP_GR = yz.CORP_GR
           AND ya.JM_CD   = yz.JM_CD
         GROUP BY yz.FUND_CD, yz.TRUSTEE, ya.CHANGE_JM_CD) tt
 WHERE t1.CORP_GR         = :corp_gr
   AND t1.FUND_CD         = :fund_cd
   AND t1.YMD             = :ymd
   AND t1.PORT_NUM        = :port_num
   AND t2.CORP_GR         = t1.CORP_GR
   AND t2.FUND_CD         = t1.FUND_CD
   AND t2.TYMD            = t1.YMD
   AND t2.PORT_NUM        = t1.PORT_num
   AND pp.PORT(+)         = '(' || :port_num || ')'
   AND pp.JM_CD(+)        = t1.JM_CD
   AND ya.CORP_GR         = t1.CORP_GR
   AND ya.JM_CD           = t1.JM_CD
   AND tt.TRUSTEE(+)      = '(' || :port_num || ')'
   AND tt.CHANGE_JM_CD(+) = t1.JM_CD
 ORDER BY t1.PORT_NUM, t1.COL, t1.JM_CD
*/







    -- 기준 하루 수량
    v_daily_avg := p_total_qty / p_days;

    -- 오늘까지의 기준 누적 목표
    v_target_sum    := v_daily_avg * p_today;

    -- 오늘 매수해야 할 수량 (오늘까지 목표 - 이미 매수한 합계)
    v_today_qty := v_target_sum - p_bought_sum;

    -- 음수 방지 (이미 목표 이상 매수한 경우 0으로)
    if  v_today_qty < 0 then
        v_today_qty := 0;
    end if;

    RETURN ROUND(v_today_qty,2);
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_trade_avg (
    p_koscom_cd                    IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR DEFAULT '-20'
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- (20)거래일 평균 거래량
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_avg                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_ymd   := f_trade_ymd(p_ymd - 1,p_gb);
    SELECT NVL(AVG(volume), 0)
      INTO w_avg
      FROM SJT1TG t1
     WHERE t1.ymd       Between w_ymd And p_ymd - 1
       AND t1.koscom_cd = p_koscom_cd;
    RETURN  w_avg;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TRADE_YMD (
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_gb                           IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- 입력일 기준 주식 거래일만 반영한 이전(후) 주식 거래일
-- * 전(후) 계산일이 주식 거래일만 반영하므로 사용시 주의할 것
--
    w_next                       NUMBER;
    w_day                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_dd                         NUMBER := 1; -- 입력일 주식거래일 점검을 위해
    w_ymd                        DATE;
BEGIN
    if  SUBSTR (NVL(p_gb,'+'),1,1) = '+' then
            w_next  := 1;
    else    w_next  := -1;
    end if;
    if NVL (p_gb,'+') IN ('+','-')  then
        if  F_STOCK (p_ymd) = '0'   then    RETURN  p_ymd;      -- 입력일이 주식거래일이면 입력일 return
        end if;
    else    w_dd    := SUBSTR(p_gb,2);
    end if;
    w_ymd   := trunc(p_ymd,'dd');   -- 시간 제외처리
    loop
        if  w_day = w_dd    then    EXIT;
        end if;
        w_ymd   := w_ymd + w_next;
        if  F_STOCK (w_ymd) = '0'   then    w_day   := w_day + 1;
        end if;
    end loop;
    RETURN  w_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_TRUNC (
    p_aek                       IN        number,
    p_fund_currency             IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
-- 기준통화에 따른 절사
--
begin
    if  p_fund_currency = 'KRW' then
            RETURN trunc (nvl(p_aek,0));
    else    RETURN trunc (nvl(p_aek,2));
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_tr_co_nm (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_tr_co_cd                     IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 매매처명
--
    w_tr_co_nm                   VARCHAR(200);
BEGIN
    if  p_tr_co_cd IS NULL  then    RETURN NULL;
    end if;
    BEGIN
        SELECT tr_co_nm
          INTO w_tr_co_nm
          FROM SZX2MM t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.tr_co_cd = p_tr_co_cd
           AND ROWNUM = 1;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        BEGIN
            SELECT fund_nm
              INTO w_tr_co_nm
              FROM SZM0IA t1
             WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
               AND t1.fund_cd = p_tr_co_cd;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
            w_tr_co_nm  := NULL;
        END;
    END;
    RETURN  w_tr_co_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_tr_nm (
    p_tr_cd                     IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 거래코드명
--
    w_tr_nm                     szx0gc.tr_nm%type;
BEGIN
    begin
        SELECT  tr_nm
          INTO  w_tr_nm
        FROM    szx0gc t1
        WHERE   tr_cd = p_tr_cd;
    exception when  OTHERS  then
        w_tr_nm := ' ';
    end;
    RETURN  w_tr_nm;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_tr_nm1 (
    p_tr_cd                     IN        varchar
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 매매구분명
--
BEGIN
    if  p_tr_cd = 'F21'             then RETURN '입고';
    elsif  p_tr_cd = 'F47'          then RETURN '공모주상장';
    elsif  p_tr_cd = 'L12'          then RETURN '매수';
    elsif  p_tr_cd = 'L22'          then RETURN '매도';
    elsif  substr(p_tr_cd,1,1)='J'  then RETURN '매수';
    elsif  substr(p_tr_cd,1,1)='K'  then RETURN '매도';
    else                                 RETURN 'tr_nm1-'||p_tr_cd;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_UNYONG_NAME (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 운용역
--
    w_unyong                     VARCHAR(30);
BEGIN
    SELECT fu.user_nm
      INTO w_unyong
      FROM SZM0IA      t1
         , FW_USER_MST fu
     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
       AND fu.corp_gr = t1.corp_gr
       AND fu.user_id = t1.unyong_sabun;

    RETURN  W_UNYONG;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_UP_DOWN (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN UP_DOWN_TAB AS
--
-- 종가 상승, 하락일수
--
    w_tab                        UP_DOWN_TAB;

    w_days                       NUMBER := 0;
    w_u5                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_d5                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_u8                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_d8                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_u13                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d13                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_u21                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d21                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_u34                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d34                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_u55                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d55                        NUMBER := 0;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := UP_DOWN_TAB();
    FOR xx IN (
        SELECT t1.YMD
             , NVL(t1.JONGA, 0)                                                         AS jonga
             , NVL(t1.JONGA, 0) / LAG(NVL(t1.JONGA, 0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.JM_CD
                                                                  ORDER BY t1.YMD) - 1  AS rt
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.YMD     NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                    FROM SZX0HL ta
                                   WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'US'
                                     AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd)
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd
         ORDER BY t1.YMD DESC
    ) loop
        w_days  := w_days + 1;
        if  w_days <= 5     then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u5    := w_u5 + 1;
            else    w_d5    := w_d5 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 8     then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u8    := w_u8 + 1;
            else    w_d8    := w_d8 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 13    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u13   := w_u13 + 1;
            else    w_d13   := w_d13 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 21    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u21   := w_u21 + 1;
            else    w_d21   := w_d21 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 34    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u34   := w_u34 + 1;
            else    w_d34   := w_d34 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 55    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u55   := w_u55 + 1;
            else    w_d55   := w_d55 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days = 55     then    exit;
        end if;
    END LOOP;

    w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := UP_DOWN_REC ('상승일수', w_u5, w_u8, w_u13, w_u21, w_u34, w_u55);
    w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := UP_DOWN_REC ('하락일수', w_d5, w_d8, w_d13, w_d21, w_d34, w_d55);
    w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := UP_DOWN_REC ('상승-하락일수', w_u5 - w_d5, w_u8 - w_d8, w_u13 - w_d13, w_u21 - w_d21, w_u34 - w_d34, w_u55 - w_d55);

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_UP_DOWN_K (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN UP_DOWN_TAB AS
--
-- 종가 상승, 하락일수
--
    w_tab                        UP_DOWN_TAB;

    w_days                       NUMBER := 0;
    w_u5                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_d5                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_u8                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_d8                         NUMBER := 0;
    w_u13                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d13                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_u21                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d21                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_u34                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d34                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_u55                        NUMBER := 0;
    w_d55                        NUMBER := 0;
BEGIN
    w_tab   := UP_DOWN_TAB();
    FOR xx IN (
         SELECT t1.YMD
             , NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0)                                                         AS jonga
             , NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0) / LAG(NVL(t1.CLOSE, 0)) OVER (PARTITION BY t1.KOSCOM_CD
                                                                  ORDER BY t1.KOSCOM_CD, t1.YMD) - 1  AS rt
          FROM SJT1TG t1
         WHERE t1.YMD     Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.YMD     NOT IN (SELECT HOLI_YMD
                                    FROM SZX0HL ta
                                   WHERE ta.NATION_CD = 'KR'
                                     AND ta.HOLI_YMD  >= p_fymd)
           AND t1.KOSCOM_CD   = p_jm_cd
         ORDER BY t1.YMD DESC
    ) loop
        w_days  := w_days + 1;
        if  w_days <= 5     then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u5    := w_u5 + 1;
            else    w_d5    := w_d5 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 8     then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u8    := w_u8 + 1;
            else    w_d8    := w_d8 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 13    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u13   := w_u13 + 1;
            else    w_d13   := w_d13 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 21    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u21   := w_u21 + 1;
            else    w_d21   := w_d21 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 34    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u34   := w_u34 + 1;
            else    w_d34   := w_d34 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days <= 55    then
            if  xx.rt > 0   then
                    w_u55   := w_u55 + 1;
            else    w_d55   := w_d55 + 1;
            end if;
        end if;
        if  w_days = 55     then    exit;
        end if;
    END LOOP;

    w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := UP_DOWN_REC ('상승일수', w_u5, w_u8, w_u13, w_u21, w_u34, w_u55);
    w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := UP_DOWN_REC ('하락일수', w_d5, w_d8, w_d13, w_d21, w_d34, w_d55);
    w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := UP_DOWN_REC ('상승-하락일수', w_u5 - w_d5, w_u8 - w_d8, w_u13 - w_d13, w_u21 - w_d21, w_u34 - w_d34, w_u55 - w_d55);

    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_UZM0UH_BIGO (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_currency                IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 정보계 신탁재산운용현황 생성
--
    w_return                     VARCHAR(200) DEFAULT '';
BEGIN
    for xx IN  (
        select ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='11300' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g11300  /* _1- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='11400' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g11400  /* _2- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='10900' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g10900  /* _3- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='11000' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g11000  /* _4- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='10103' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g10103  /* _5- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE gwamok WHEN '10104' THEN NVL (junj,0) + NVL (dr_aek,0) - NVL (cr_aek,0)
                                     WHEN '10105' THEN NVL (junj,0) + NVL (dr_aek,0) - NVL (cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)  AS g10104  /* _6- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='11500' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g11500  /* _7- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='11600' THEN NVL(junj,0) + NVL(dr_aek,0) - NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g11600  /* _8- */
             , ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN gwamok='20200' THEN NVL(junj,0) - NVL(dr_aek,0) + NVL(cr_aek,0) ELSE 0 END) / 1000000, 2)     AS g20200  /* _9- */
          from SKM0AG t1
         where t1.corp_gr       = p_corp_gr
           and t1.ymd           = p_ymd
           and t1.work_gb       = 'A'
           and t1.fund_cd       = p_fund_cd
           and t1.fund_currency = p_fund_currency
           and t1.gwamok        IN ('11300','11400','10900','11000','10103','10104','10105','11500','20200')
    ) loop
        if  xx.g11400 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 선납원천세:'||xx.g11400;
        end if;
        if  xx.g11300 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 기타미수수익:'||xx.g11300;
        end if;
        if  xx.g10900 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 미수입금:'||xx.g10900;
        end if;
        if  xx.g11000 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 미수배당금:'||xx.g11000;
        end if;
        if  xx.g10103 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 공모청약금:'||xx.g10103;
        end if;
        if  xx.g10104 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 증거금:'||xx.g10104;
        end if;
        if  xx.g11500 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 이연자산:'||xx.g11500;
        end if;
        if  xx.g11600 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 기타자산:'||xx.g11600;
        end if;
        if  xx.g20200 != 0  then    w_return    := w_return||' 미지급금:'||-xx.g20200;
        end if;
    end loop;
    if  w_return IS NULL    then
            w_return    := '';
    else    w_return    := '※' || w_return;
    end if;
    RETURN  W_RETURN;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_UZM0UI_YMD (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE
)   RETURN DATE IS
    w_ymd                        DATE;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT MAX(ymd)
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM UZM0UI t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     Between ADD_MONTHS(p_ymd, -1)  And p_ymd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        SELECT gijunga_ymd
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    END;

    RETURN w_ymd;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_VARRAY (
    p_data                         IN    VARCHAR,
    p_char                         IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR_VARRAY_200 IS
-- 구분자로 자르기
--
    w_data                       VARCHAR(32000);
    w_p                          NUMBER;
    w_200                        varchar_varray_200;
BEGIN
    w_200   := varchar_varray_200 ();
    w_data  := p_data;

    while true loop
        w_200.EXTEND;
        w_p := INSTR (w_data,p_char);
        if  w_p = 0 then
            w_200 (w_200.LAST)  := TRIM(w_data);
            EXIT;
        end if;
        w_200 (w_200.LAST)  := TRIM(SUBSTR (w_data, 1, w_p - 1));
        w_data  := SUBSTR (w_data,w_p + 1);
        if  w_data IS NULL  then
            w_200.EXTEND;
            EXIT;
        end if;
    end loop;

    RETURN  w_200;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_VARRAY1 (
    p_data                         IN    VARCHAR,
    p_char                         IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR_VARRAY_200 IS
-- 구분자로 자르기
--
    w_p                          NUMBER;
    w_200                        varchar_varray_200;
BEGIN
    w_200   := varchar_varray_200 ();

    for xx IN (
        select LEVEL AS n, REGEXP_SUBSTR(p_data,'(.*?)(\' || p_char || '|$)',1,LEVEL,NULL,1)  AS data
        from DUAL
        CONNECT BY NOCYCLE INSTR(p_data,p_char,1,level - 1)            > 0
    ) loop
        w_200.EXTEND;
        w_200 (xx.n)    := TRIM(xx.data);
    end loop;

    RETURN  w_200;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_VARRAY2 (
    p_data                         IN    VARCHAR,
    p_char                         IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VARCHAR_VARRAY_200 IS
-- 구분자로 자르기
--
    w_data                       VARCHAR(800);
    w_next                       VARCHAR(800);
    len                          NUMBER;
    nn                           NUMBER;
    w_200                        varchar_varray_200;
BEGIN
    w_200   := varchar_varray_200 ();
    w_data  := p_data;
    len     := LENGTH (REGEXP_REPLACE (p_data,'[^' || p_char || ']')) + 1;
    w_200.EXTEND (len);
    for nn IN 1..len loop
        if  SUBSTR (w_data,1,1) = p_char    then
            w_200 (nn)  := NULL;
            w_next      := SUBSTR (w_data,2);
        else
            w_200 (nn)  := RTRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(w_data,'(.*?)(\' || p_char || '|$)',1), p_char);
            w_next      := LTRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(w_data,'\' || p_char || '.*'), p_char);
        end if;
        if  w_next IS NULL  then
            w_200 (nn + 1)  := w_data;
            EXIT;
        end if;
        w_data  := w_next;
    end LOOP;

    RETURN  w_200;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_VOLATILITY (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_type                         IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN VOLATILITY_TAB AS
--
-- 변동수준 및 괴리율
--
    w_tab                        VOLATILITY_TAB;

    w_max                        NUMBER;
    w_g                          NUMBER;
    w_g1                         NUMBER;
    w_g2                         NUMBER;
    w_g3                         NUMBER;
    w_g4                         NUMBER;
    w_min                        NUMBER;

    w_max_ymd                    DATE;
    w_min_ymd                    DATE;

    w_day1                       NUMBER;
    w_day2                       NUMBER;
    w_day3                       NUMBER;
    w_day4                       NUMBER;
    w_day5                       NUMBER;
    w_day6                       NUMBER;

    w_pos1                       VARCHAR(40);
    w_pos2                       VARCHAR(40);
    w_pos3                       VARCHAR(40);
    w_pos4                       VARCHAR(40);
    w_pos5                       VARCHAR(40);
    w_pos6                       VARCHAR(40);
BEGIN
    w_tab   := VOLATILITY_TAB();

    if  p_type = '변동수준' then
        SELECT MAX(D30_VOLATILITY)
             , MIN(D30_VOLATILITY)
          INTO w_max
             , w_min
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd;

        SELECT MAX(YMD)
          INTO w_max_ymd
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR        = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD            Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD          = p_jm_cd
           AND t1.D30_VOLATILITY = w_max;

        SELECT MAX(YMD)
          INTO w_min_ymd
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR        = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD            Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD          = p_jm_cd
           AND t1.D30_VOLATILITY = w_min;

        w_g     := w_max - w_min;
        w_g4    := w_min + (w_g * 4 / 5);
        w_g3    := w_min + (w_g * 3 / 5);
        w_g2    := w_min + (w_g * 2 / 5);
        w_g1    := w_min + (w_g * 1 / 5);

        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('10', NULL, p_tymd, '단기변동수준 (' || F_N#(w_g * 100, 0, 2) || ')', NULL, NULL);

        SELECT D30_VOLATILITY
          INTO w_g
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd;

        if  w_g between w_min AND w_g1  then
            w_day1  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos1  := '최하구간진입';
        elsif w_g between w_g1 AND w_g2 then
            w_day2  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos2  := '차하구간진입';
        elsif w_g between w_g2 AND w_g3 then
            w_day3  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos3  := '중간구간진입';
        elsif w_g between w_g3 AND w_g4 then
            w_day4  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos4  := '차상구간진입';
        elsif w_g between w_g4 AND w_max    then
            w_day5  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos5  := '최상구간진입';
        else
            w_day6  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos6  := '레인지이탈';
        end if;

        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('11', w_max * 100, w_max_ymd,'최상위', NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('12', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day5, w_pos5);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('13', w_g4 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('14', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day4, w_pos4);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('15', w_g3 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('16', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day3, w_pos3);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('17', w_g2 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('18', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day2, w_pos2);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('19', w_g1 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('20', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day1, w_pos1);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('21', w_min * 100, w_min_ymd,'최하위', NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('22', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day6, w_pos6);

    elsif p_type = '괴리율' then
        SELECT MAX(t1.SHORT_PRICE / t1.GIJUN_PRICE - 1)
             , MIN(t1.SHORT_PRICE / t1.GIJUN_PRICE - 1)
          INTO w_max
             , w_min
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd;

        SELECT MAX(YMD)
          INTO w_max_ymd
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd
           AND (t1.SHORT_PRICE / t1.GIJUN_PRICE - 1)  = w_max;

        SELECT MAX(YMD)
          INTO w_min_ymd
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     Between p_fymd AND p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd
           AND (t1.SHORT_PRICE / t1.GIJUN_PRICE - 1)  = w_min;

        w_g     := w_max - w_min;
        w_g4    := w_min + (w_g * 4 / 5);
        w_g3    := w_min + (w_g * 3 / 5);
        w_g2    := w_min + (w_g * 2 / 5);
        w_g1    := w_min + (w_g * 1 / 5);

        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('20', NULL, p_tymd, '평균가괴리율 (' || F_N#(w_g * 100, 0, 2) || ')', NULL, NULL);

        SELECT t1.SHORT_PRICE / t1.GIJUN_PRICE - 1
          INTO w_g
          FROM USPM_DD t1
         WHERE t1.CORP_GR = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.YMD     = p_tymd
           AND t1.YMD     = p_tymd
           AND t1.JM_CD   = p_jm_cd;

        if  w_g between w_min AND w_g1      then
            w_day1  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos1  := '-2구간:상승아주높음';
        elsif w_g between w_g1 AND w_g2     then
            w_day2  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos2  := '-1구간:상승높음';
        elsif w_g between w_g2 AND w_g3     then
            w_day3  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos3  := '0구간 :판단유보영역';
        elsif w_g between w_g3 AND w_g4     then
            w_day4  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos4  := '+1구간 :하락높음';
        elsif w_g between w_g4 AND w_max    then
            w_day5  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos5  := '+2구간 :하락아주높음';
        else
            w_day6  := w_g * 100;
            w_pos6  := '레인지이탈';
        end if;

        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('11', w_max * 100, NULL,'최상위', NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('12', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day5, w_pos5);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('13', w_g4 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('14', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day4, w_pos4);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('15', w_g3 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('16', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day3, w_pos3);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('17', w_g2 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('18', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day2, w_pos2);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('19', w_g1 * 100, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('20', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day1, w_pos1);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('21', w_min * 100, NULL,'최하위', NULL, NULL);
        w_tab.extend;   w_tab (w_tab.count) := VOLATILITY_REC ('22', NULL, NULL, NULL, w_day6, w_pos6);
    end if;
    RETURN  w_tab;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_week_nm (
    p_ymd                       IN        date
)              RETURN  VARCHAR  IS
-- 요일(한글)
--
    w_week                      varchar_varray_50;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd is not null   then
        w_week  := varchar_varray_50 ('(일)','(월)','(화)','(수)','(목)','(금)','(토)');
        RETURN  W_WEEK ( to_char(p_ymd,'d') );
    else
        RETURN  NULL;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_WORKDATE (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_next_day                     IN    NUMBER DEFAULT 0
)   RETURN DATE IS
-- Calender 익(전)영업일자
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
BEGIN
    if  p_next_day > -1 then
        SELECT f_open_ymd(hyun_ymd,'+' || p_next_day)
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    else
        SELECT f_open_ymd(hyun_ymd,p_next_day)
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    end if;
    RETURN  w_ymd;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN  now ();
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_WORK_DAYS (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 일수(한편넣기)
--
    w_days                         NUMBER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT F_DAYS (p_fymd, p_tymd) - COUNT(*)
          INTO w_days
          FROM SZX0HL t1
         WHERE t1.nation_cd = 'KR'
           AND t1.holi_ymd  BETWEEN p_fymd AND p_tymd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        w_days  := 0;
    END;
    RETURN  w_days;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_work_gb (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    i_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR,
    i_date                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN VARCHAR IS
-- 결산구분 return  ...
--
    w_work_gb                    skt0bu.work_gb%type;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'B'
          INTO w_work_gb
          FROM SKT0GS t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr  = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.fund_cd  = i_fund_cd
           AND t1.gyul_ymd = i_date;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        RETURN  'A';
    END;
    RETURN  w_work_gb;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_YDANGA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE DEFAULT NULL
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 해외주식 단가
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_danga                      syt0lp.danga%type;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd IS NULL   then
        SELECT F_TRADE_YMD(junyong_ymd,'-')
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    else
        w_ymd   := p_ymd;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT danga
          INTO w_danga
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = w_ymd
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        w_danga := NULL;
    END;

    RETURN  w_danga;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.f_yegum (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fund_cd                      IN    VARCHAR
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 펀드별 예금 잔액 조회(회계처리분 반영)
--
    w_workdate                   DATE;

    w_aek                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    w_workdate  := f_workdate (p_corp_gr);

    SELECT NVL(SUM(NVL(junj, 0) + NVL(dr_aek, 0) - NVL(cr_aek, 0)), 0)
      INTO w_aek
      FROM SKM0AG t1
     WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
       AND t1.ymd     = w_workdate
       AND t1.fund_cd = p_fund_cd
       AND t1.gwamok  = '10101'
       AND t1.work_gb = 'A';

    RETURN  w_aek;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_YJONGA (
    p_corp_gr                      IN    VARCHAR,
    p_jm_cd                        IN    VARCHAR,
    p_ymd                          IN    DATE DEFAULT NULL
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 해외주식 종가
--
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_jonga                      syt0lp.jonga%type;
BEGIN
    if  p_ymd IS NULL   then
        SELECT F_TRADE_YMD(junyong_ymd,'-')
          INTO w_ymd
          FROM SZX0AA t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr;
    else
        w_ymd   := p_ymd;
    end if;

    BEGIN
        SELECT jonga
          INTO w_jonga
          FROM SYT0LP t1
         WHERE t1.corp_gr = p_corp_gr
           AND t1.ymd     = w_ymd
           AND t1.jm_cd   = p_jm_cd;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        w_jonga := NULL;
    END;
    RETURN  w_jonga;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_YONG_DAYS (
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 구간 영업일수(한편넣기)
--
    w_days                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(*)
      INTO w_days
      FROM FW_CALENDAR t1
     WHERE SYS_ID   = 'SY'
       AND YMD      Between TO_CHAR(p_fymd,'yyyymmdd') AND TO_CHAR(p_tymd,'yyyymmdd')
       AND DAY_TYPE = 'NOR';

    RETURN  w_days;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.F_YONG_DAYS1 (
    p_nation_cd                    IN    VARCHAR,
    p_fymd                         IN    DATE,
    p_tymd                         IN    DATE
)   RETURN NUMBER IS
-- 국가별 구간 영업일수(한편넣기)
--
    w_days                       NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(*)
      INTO w_days
      FROM FW_CALENDAR t1
     WHERE SYS_ID = 'SY'
       AND YMD    Between TO_CHAR(p_fymd,'yyyymmdd') AND TO_CHAR(p_tymd,'yyyymmdd');

    SELECT w_days - NVL(COUNT(*), 0)
      INTO w_days
      FROM SZX0HL t1
     WHERE t1.nation_cd = p_nation_cd
       AND t1.holi_ymd  Between p_fymd AND p_tymd;

    RETURN  w_days;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.now 
RETURN  date  IS
begin
    RETURN  sysdate;
end;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SEQVAL (
    p_seqnm                     IN        varchar
)               RETURN  NUMBER  IS
begin
    if  p_seqnm = 'seq_bss'         then
        RETURN  seq_bss.nextval;
    elsif p_seqnm = 'excel_seq'     then
        RETURN  excel_seq.nextval;
    elsif p_seqnm = 'fw_pgmlogseq'  then
        RETURN  fw_pgmlogseq.nextval;
    elsif p_seqnm = 'rt_key'        then
        RETURN  rt_key.nextval;
    elsif p_seqnm = 'join_seq'      then
        RETURN  join_seq.nextval;
    elsif p_seqnm = 'seq'           then
        RETURN  seq.nextval;
    else
        RETURN 0;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SPF_FW_DEFINE_WORD_C1 (
    ps_sys_id                      IN    VARCHAR2,
    ps_word_sec                    IN    VARCHAR2,
    ps_word                        IN    VARCHAR2
)   RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
    ps_word_next                 VARCHAR2(200);
BEGIN

    SELECT CASE WHEN ps_word_sec IN ('lng_org','lng_kor') THEN ta.lng_kor
                WHEN ps_word_sec='lng_eng'                THEN NVL(ta.lng_eng,ta.lng_kor)
                WHEN ps_word_sec='lng_chn'                THEN NVL(ta.lng_chn,ta.lng_kor)
                WHEN ps_word_sec='lng_vit'                THEN NVL(ta.lng_vit,ta.lng_kor)
                WHEN ps_word_sec='lng_loc'                THEN NVL(ta.lng_loc,ta.lng_kor)
                                                          ELSE ps_word
           END  AS word_next
      INTO ps_word_next
      FROM FW_LNG01 ta
     WHERE ta.sys_id  = ps_sys_id
       AND ta.lng_gb  = '02'
       AND ta.lng_org = ps_word;

    RETURN ps_word_next;

EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
    RETURN ps_word;
WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN 'FAIL';
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SPF_FW_FULLROLLMENU_1SUB (
    Ps_sys_id                      IN    VARCHAR,
    Ps_pgm_no                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
    vl_level_no                    NUMBER (2);
BEGIN
    WITH MAXLVL
       ( LEVEL_NO
       , SYS_ID
       , PGM_NO
       , PARENT_PGM
       )
     AS ( SELECT 1              AS level_no
            , lz.sys_id      AS sys_id
            , lz.pgm_no      AS pgm_no
            , lz.parent_pgm  AS parent_pgm
         FROM FW_PGM_MST lz
        WHERE lz.sys_id = Ps_sys_id
          AND lz.pgm_no = Ps_pgm_no
       UNION ALL
       SELECT level_no + 1
            , t2.sys_id
            , t2.pgm_no
            , t2.parent_pgm
         FROM FW_PGM_MST t2
        Inner JOIN MAXLVL t3  ON t2.sys_id     = Ps_sys_id
                             AND t3.sys_id     = t2.sys_id
                             AND t3.parent_pgm = t2.pgm_no
        )
      --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      -- 내부 함수&테이블 이용한 쿼리 실행
      --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      SELECT NVL(MAX(lx.level_no), 0) INTO vl_level_no FROM MAXLVL lx;

    RETURN vl_level_no;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
    RETURN '';
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SPF_FW_FULLROLLMENU_2SUB (
    Ps_sys_id                      IN    VARCHAR,
    Ps_pgm_no                      IN    VARCHAR,
    Ps_langtype                    IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
    vs_pgm4path                    VARCHAR (200);
BEGIN
    WITH t_pgm4path
       ( level_no
       , sys_id
       , pgm_no
       , parent_pgm
       , pgm_nm
       )
     AS ( SELECT 1              AS level_no    /* _1- */
            , lz.sys_id      AS sys_id      /* _2- */
            , lz.pgm_no      AS pgm_no      /* _3- */
            , lz.parent_pgm  AS parent_pgm  /* _4- */
            , lz.pgm_nm      AS pgm_nm      /* _5- */
         FROM FW_PGM_MST lz
        WHERE lz.sys_id = Ps_sys_id
          AND lz.pgm_no = Ps_pgm_no
       UNION ALL
       SELECT level_no + 1    /* _1- */
            , t26.sys_id      /* _2- */
            , t26.pgm_no      /* _3- */
            , t26.parent_pgm  /* _4- */
            , t26.pgm_nm      /* _5- */
         FROM FW_PGM_MST t26
            , T_PGM4PATH t25
        WHERE t26.sys_id     = Ps_sys_id
          AND t25.sys_id     = t26.sys_id
          AND t25.parent_pgm = t26.pgm_no
        )
      --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      -- 내부 함수(테이블) 이용한 쿼리 실행
      --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      SELECT MAX(CASE When level_no=5 THEN lx.pgm_no ELSE '0' END) || ';' ||
             MAX(CASE When level_no=4 THEN lx.pgm_no ELSE '0' END) || ';' ||
             MAX(CASE When level_no=3 THEN lx.pgm_no ELSE '0' END) || ';' ||
             MAX(CASE When level_no=2 THEN lx.pgm_no ELSE '0' END) || 'tab' ||
             MAX(CASE When level_no=4 THEN SPF_FW_MLANG4NODE1 ('biz4penta', Ps_langtype, lx.pgm_nm) ELSE '' END) || ' > ' ||
             MAX(CASE When level_no=3 THEN SPF_FW_MLANG4NODE1 ('biz4penta', Ps_langtype, lx.pgm_nm) ELSE '' END) || ' > ' ||
             MAX(CASE When level_no=2 THEN SPF_FW_MLANG4NODE1 ('biz4penta', Ps_langtype, lx.pgm_nm) ELSE '' END)  AS pgm_path
        INTO vs_pgm4path
        FROM T_PGM4PATH lx;

    RETURN vs_pgm4path;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
    RETURN '';
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SPF_FW_MENU001 (
    v_sys_id                       IN    VARCHAR2,
    v_pgm_no                       IN    VARCHAR2
 )  RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
    vs_pgm_nm                      VARCHAR2 (200);
BEGIN
    SELECT ta.pgm_nm
      INTO vs_pgm_nm
      FROM FW_PGM_MST ta
     WHERE ta.sys_id = v_sys_id
       AND ta.pgm_no = v_pgm_no;

    RETURN vs_pgm_nm;

EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
    RETURN '';
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SPF_FW_MLANG4NODE1 (
    Ps_sys_id                      IN    VARCHAR,
    Ps_tobetype                    IN    VARCHAR,
    Ps_asistext                    IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN VARCHAR IS
    vs_tobetext                    VARCHAR (200);
BEGIN
    SELECT CASE When Ps_tobetype IN ('ORG','KOR') THEN la.kor
                When Ps_tobetype='ENG'            THEN NVL(la.eng, la.kor)
                When Ps_tobetype='CHN'            THEN NVL(la.chn, la.kor)
                When Ps_tobetype='VIT'            THEN NVL(la.vit, la.kor)
                When Ps_tobetype='LOC'            THEN NVL(la.loc, la.kor)
                                                  ELSE Ps_asistext END  AS tobetext
      INTO vs_tobetext
      FROM FW_FRME30M la
     WHERE la.sys_id = Ps_sys_id
       AND la.org    = Ps_asistext;

    RETURN vs_tobetext;

EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN 
    RETURN Ps_asistext;
WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN 'FAIL';
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION KFP.SPF_FW_STATE02_DB1_CPU (
    in_sys_id                      IN    VARCHAR
 )  RETURN NUMBER IS
    n_cpu_use                      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT SUM(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(t2.VALUE) / 100,2))  AS db1_cpu
      INTO n_cpu_use
      FROM V$SESSION t1
         , V$SESSTAT t2
         , V$STATNAME t3
     WHERE t1.status     = 'ACTIVE'
       AND t1.username   IN ('AAMS','KFP','AFMS')
       AND t2.sid        = t1.sid
       AND t3.name       LIKE 'CPU used by this session'
       AND t3.statistic# = t2.statistic#;

    RETURN n_cpu_use;

EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN 
    RETURN 0;
WHEN OTHERS THEN
    RETURN -1;
END;
/
